### [1] [Memory flows: geometry and dynamics of sequential retrieval in input-driven Hopfield networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09995)
*Simone Betteti,Matteo Martin,Morten Gram Pedersen,Sandro Zampieri,Giacomo Baggio*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 提出一个双时间尺度、输入驱动的Hopfield网络，用慢反馈变量在给定记忆转移图上调制快速联想检索，借助慢-快与几何奇异摄动理论刻画记忆固定、稳定性丧失与逃逸的几何条件，并给出转移起始、自维持检索与崩溃的判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典联想记忆模型将检索视为收敛到存储模式，但序列检索（从一个记忆有序转移到下一个记忆）的理论仍相对有限；需要既保留解析可处理性、又能产生自主且受图约束的转移的框架。

Method: 构建双时间尺度输入驱动Hopfield网络：快速联想检索 + 在既定记忆转移图上演化的慢反馈变量。采用慢-快分析与几何奇异摄动理论，通过记忆流形刻画记忆固定，识别控制稳定性丧失与逃逸的几何区域；进一步约化出慢动力学，导出转移起始、自维持检索与崩溃的显式判据（逃逸时间、增益阈值、不动点）。

Result: 识别出决定记忆稳定性丧失与逃逸的几何区域；给出约化慢动力学及转移起始、自维持检索和崩溃的显式解析判据，以逃逸时间、增益阈值和不动点形式表达。

Conclusion: 该工作为Hopfield网络中反馈驱动的序列检索提供了一个可解析处理的理论框架，将经典联想检索推广到图约束的自主序列转移。

Abstract: Associative memory models classically describe retrieval as convergence to stored patterns, but the theory of sequential retrieval remains comparatively limited. We develop a two-timescale input-driven Hopfield network in which fast associative retrieval is modulated by a slow feedback variable evolving over a prescribed memory-transition graph. This yields autonomous, graph-constrained transitions while preserving analytical tractability. Using slow-fast and geometric singular perturbation theory, we characterize memory fixation via memory manifolds and identify the geometric regions that govern stability loss and escape from retrieved memories. We further derive reduced slow dynamics and explicit criteria for transition onset, self-sustained retrieval, and collapse, expressed through escape times, gain thresholds, and fixed points. Together, these results provide a tractable framework for analyzing feedback-driven sequential retrieval in Hopfield networks.

</details>


### [2] [How Path Integral Diffusion Decides a Finite Spin Glass](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11826)
*Michael Chertkov*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该论文证明任意各向同性PID生成器都是时间重标度的高斯随机定域化，并刻画其"部分承诺"为仅沿类别对比演化的鞅；每个分裂近似为信噪比1.04/λ_P的二值高斯信道。在Bethe晶格与SK自旋玻璃上，轨迹先选定镜面半边，再进入玻璃态类别，宏观决策呈一两个离散事件，而有限步协议只影响晚期局域阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究AI扩散型生成器在采样受阻自旋玻璃时，先做哪些宏观决策、何时做出，以及这些决策是否依赖于具体的扩散协议（信号噪声调度）。

Method: 针对点源出发的Path Integral Diffusion（PID）桥扩散模型：证明各向同性PID与时间重标度高斯随机定域化的等价性；用嵌套配置类上的"部分承诺"概率（鞅）描述生成器状态，以磁化对比的平方给出各层级信息获取速率，并将每次分裂建模为信噪比1.04/λ_P的二值高斯信道；在Bethe晶格与Sherrington–Kirkpatrick玻璃（最多64自旋）上做数值验证。

Result: PID协议与信号噪声调度的对应关系表明宏观决策与协议无关，协议只决定"何时"与"代价"。分裂阈值由父类相关矩阵的主特征值λ_P决定。数值上轨迹普遍先向零场测度的两个镜像半边之一倾斜，再在其内部选择玻璃态类别；玻璃态类别在玻璃转变附近出现并在降温中保持身份。嵌套玻璃态层级λ_P相近，因此被一并决定，轨迹通常只发生一到两次独立承诺事件（镜像选择与复合玻璃态决策），随耦合强度与体系尺寸增大而分离更明显，与谱预测一致。

Conclusion: 生成器的宏观决策是协议无关的内禀性质，由目标测度的类别对比与谱结构决定；有限步生成时协议仅通过晚期的局域阶段产生影响。

Abstract: When an AI diffusion-type generator samples a frustrated spin glass, which macroscopic alternatives does it decide first, and when? We study this for bridge-diffusion models of the Path Integral Diffusion (PID) type started from a point source. We show that every isotropic PID is a time-changed Gaussian stochastic localization, and conversely every signal-to-noise schedule is realized by a PID protocol. What a continuous-time generator decides therefore does not depend on the protocol, which only sets when and at what cost. We describe the generator's state by its partial commitments: the probabilities it assigns, at each moment, to nested classes of configurations fixed by the target. The partial commitments are martingales that move only along class contrasts. Their ensemble average gives the information about each level at an exact rate set by squared magnetization contrasts. Each split is decided, approximately, like a binary Gaussian channel at signal-to-noise ratio 1.04/\lambda_P, with \lambda_P the leading eigenvalue of the parent's correlation matrix. The theory is applied to Bethe-lattice and Sherrington--Kirkpatrick (SK) glasses with up to 64 spins. It is observed generically that a trajectory first shifts its weight to one of the two mirror halves of the zero-field measure and then to the glassy classes inside it. These classes appear near the glass transition and keep their identity on cooling. Nested glassy levels have similar \lambda_P and are decided together, so a trajectory commits in one or two distinct events---the mirror choice and a compound glassy decision---which separate further with coupling and size, as the spectra predict. For a generator that takes finitely many steps, the protocol matters through the late, local stage rather than the macroscopic decisions.

</details>


### [3] [Spatial effects of Kuramoto modeling on the power-grid frequencies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12132)
*Kristóf Benedek,Michelle T. Cirunay,Sangita Dutta,Attila Balogh,Bálint Hartmann,Géza Ódor*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 基于匈牙利16个变电站100 ms频率实测与Kuramoto模型（387节点输电网络及二维晶格）仿真，揭示电网频率谱的双标度区（指数约1.63与2.23），并论证空间耦合非线性动力学与外部驱动共同塑造频率波动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间耦合与异质性会产生复杂电网频率动力学，但空间结构、内在非线性动力学与外部驱动各自的贡献尚不清晰，需要分离并量化这些机制。

Method: 分析匈牙利输电网16站点一个月的100 ms频率数据；在387节点输电网络和二维晶格上分别模拟含/不含非相关噪声的Kuramoto模型（KM）；使用超统计高斯混合模型刻画增量分布，并估计非平稳长时间自相关指数，与实证结果对照。

Result: 实测功率谱呈两个标度区，指数约1.63和2.23；1秒频率增量快速去相关，而其局部逆方差的关联缓慢衰减，初始呈幂律τ^{-0.26(2)}，持续约15分钟；基于局部方差的超统计高斯混合可解释大部分非高斯增量分布，但无法刻画稀有极端事件。低维网络KM仿真给出代数衰减的自相关与功率谱，与实测及稳态下的Wiener–Khinchin关系一致；异质输电网络上部分同步与频率锁定产生持续空间模式与多峰频率分布。

Conclusion: 确定性KM动力学与独立高斯驱动均无法同时复现实测谱与增量统计；实测波动在各变电站间高度共享，而仿真波动主要反映节点相对运动。因此空间耦合非线性动力学与外部驱动（含15分钟功率调度）共同贡献于所观测的频率波动。

Abstract: Spatial coupling and heterogeneity can generate complex power-grid frequency dynamics. We investigate the contributions of spatial structure, intrinsic nonlinear dynamics, and external driving using the Kuramoto model (KM), with and without uncorrelated noise, and compare its predictions with real-grid measurements.
 We analyse one month of 100 ms frequency measurements from 16 Hungarian transmission-grid sites and simulate the KM on a 387-node transmission network and two-dimensional lattices. The measured power spectra exhibit two scaling regimes, with exponents of approximately 1.63 and 2.23. One-second frequency increments decorrelate rapidly, while correlations of their local inverse variance decay slowly, initially as a power law, $\tau^{-0.26(2)}$, up to 15 minutes. A superstatistical Gaussian mixture based on measured local variances captures much of the non-Gaussian increment distribution, but not the rare extreme events.
 We also estimate the non-stationary long-time frequency autocorrelation exponent. KM simulations on low-dimensional networks exhibit algebraically decaying autocorrelations and power spectra, consistent with the measurements and the Wiener-Khinchin relation in the steady state. On the heterogeneous transmission network, partial synchronization and frequency locking produce persistent spatial patterns and multi-peaked frequency distributions. However, neither deterministic KM dynamics nor independent Gaussian driving reproduces the measured spectra and increment statistics simultaneously. Moreover, empirical frequency fluctuations are largely shared across substations, whereas simulated fluctuations mainly reflect relative node motion. These findings suggest that spatially coupled nonlinear dynamics contribute to observed frequency fluctuations alongside external driving, including 15-minute power dispatches.

</details>

### [4] [Exceptional Topological Bound States in the Continuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10077)
*Luohong Liu,Qicheng Zhang,Shuaishuai Tong,Chunyin Qiu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文首次提出并实验验证“奇异拓扑连续域束缚态（ET-BIC）”这一三重奇异结构：将拓扑相位、奇异点（EP）与连续域束缚态（BIC）三者合并于同一模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑相位、奇异点与BIC是凝聚态与经典波动物理中的三大核心概念，两两组合已产生丰富的奇异模式，但三者合一的ET-BIC此前未被探索。

Method: 构建由四条耦合拓扑链组成的声学系统：y方向镜像堆叠产生成对拓扑BIC；z方向堆叠并引入偏置损耗，形成广义镜像对称，使分处不同子空间的两支模式在临界损耗处坍缩并合并为ET-BIC。实验采用电可调损耗的声学平台。

Result: 实验同时直接观测到本征能量的合并与本征态的合并，完整验证了ET-BIC的形成过程。

Conclusion: 三重协同的ET-BIC为同时需求鲁棒性、灵敏度与BIC局域性的波动器件开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Topological phases, exceptional points, and bound states in the continuum (BICs) are three key concepts in condensed matter and classical wave physics. Their pairwise combinations have yielded a rich variety of intriguing singular modes, but the triple merger--exceptional topological BICs (ET-BICs)--has yet to be explored. In this Letter, we theoretically propose and experimentally demonstrate a universal approach for constructing such elusive exceptional structures. The system consists of four coupled topological chains: y-mirror stacking generates pairs of topological BICs, while z-stacking with biased loss yields a generalized mirror symmetry under which each pair--separated in different subspaces until their collapse--merges into an ET-BIC at a critical loss. Experimentally, using an acoustic platform with electrically tunable loss, we not only directly observe the coalescence of eigenenergies but also the coalescence of eigenstates, offering a complete verification of the ET-BIC formation. The triply synergistic ET-BICs may pave the way for wave devices that simultaneously demand robustness, sensitivity, and BIC confinement.

</details>


### [5] [Optimizing one-dimensional lithographically defined nanowires in semiconducting heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10177)
*Leah Tom,Yik Kheng Lee,Karina L. Hudson,Krittika Kumar,Jackson S. Smith,Alex R. Hamilton,S. N. Coppersmith,Jared H. Cole,Mark Friesen*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 通过理论与计算模型优化半导体异质结中分裂栅的几何结构，可提升光刻定义一维纳米线输运沟道质量，并对分辨Rashba诱导自旋能隙等特征至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一维纳米线虽能承载新奇的电子性质，但光刻定义的纳米线受无序与杂质影响显著（即便异质结构限制与栅极势可提升迁移率），实验难以观察到理想行为，且新效应的实现与解释易被无序掩盖，故需降低无序影响以提升沟道品质。

Method: 采用理论分析与数值计算方法，对半导体异质结中分裂栅（split gates）的几何构型进行建模，并通过优化栅极几何参数来改善所形成一维输运沟道的品质。

Result: 预测优化分裂栅几何可得到用于输运测量的更高质量沟道；并证明该优化对于分辨Rashba诱导的自旋能隙等感兴趣特征是必要的。

Conclusion: 该改进的光刻定义导电路径方法具有更好的能量分辨率，且对无序更不敏感，为在一维尺度上实现与解读新奇物理效应提供了可行途径。

Abstract: One-dimensional nanowires can host remarkable electronic properties and are central to studying quantum transport. Although heterostructure-defined confinement combined with gate-defined potentials can greatly reduce the effects of impurities as measured via electron mobilities, the realization and interpretation of novel effects at this scale are complicated by disorder. Experiments on lithographically defined one-dimensional nanowires therefore do not exhibit ideal behavior. Using theoretical and computational models, we predict that optimizing the geometry of split gates in semiconducting heterostructures will lead to higher-quality channels for transport measurements. Further, we show that this optimization is essential for resolving features of interest such as the Rashba-induced spin gap. This new approach to lithographically defined conduction channels provides better energy resolution and is less susceptible to disorder.

</details>


### [6] [Coherent Electrometer Using Hybridised Double Quantum Dot - Resonator States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10274)
*Samuel Andersson,Pierre Glidic,Andrea Cicovic,Harald Havir,Sebastian Lehmann,Kimberly A. Dick,Ville F. Maisi*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 提出基于微波谐振腔与双量子点相干相互作用的相干静电计，实现高速、高灵敏且低耗散的电荷探测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统电荷敏感系统通过射频腔探测电导或电抗变化进行快速单电荷静电测量，但所需读出功率高、样品加热显著，限制探测速度与量子极限性能。

Method: 设计一种基于相干相互作用的探测器概念，利用微波谐振腔与双量子点之间的相干耦合实现电荷态读出，替代传统的电导/电抗变化探测方式。

Result: 该相干静电计在16 μs内以信噪比11.7分辨两个电荷态，达到同类最佳读出速度，且读出信号功率比此前达到同等速度所需低两个数量级，样品加热可忽略。

Conclusion: 凭借高灵敏度与低耗散，相干静电计为纳秒级、最小反作用的量子极限电荷探测开辟了途径。

Abstract: Probing the change in conductance or reactance of a charge sensitive system using an rf cavity is a standard way of doing fast single-charge electrometry. In this article, we present a detector concept which is instead based on coherent interaction. We show that the coherent interaction between a microwave resonator and double quantum dot gives rise to a fast, highly sensitive electrometer. The coherent electrometer achieves a state-of-the-art readout speed, resolving two charge states with SNR of 11.7 in 16 $\mu$s, with a readout signal two orders of magnitude lower in power than previously required to reach this speed. In this way, heating of the sample becomes negligible. Thanks to its sensitivity and low dissipation, the coherent electrometer opens up the avenue for quantum limited charge detection with nanosecond speed and minimal back-action.

</details>


### [7] [Extended No-Go Theorem and Its Verification in Spin-Selective Transport through Chiral Cyclodextrin Assemblies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10449)
*Kai-Yuan Zhang,Shu-Zheng Zhou,Xi Sun,Hua-Hua Fu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文将经典两端口单通道"no-go"定理推广到非幺正散射体系：证明当散射矩阵满足TRS†与中心反演对称时仍无自旋选择性，并在手性γ-环糊精分子组装体中分别验证幺正与非幺正情形下的定理及其多通道推论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典两端口单通道no-go定理依赖时间反演对称性(TRS)与散射矩阵幺正性。开放系统中被忽略的通道或环境泄漏破坏幺正性，使TRS与辛互易对称TRS†不再等价，经典前提失效，因此在非幺正区域缺乏判定CISS是否必然出现的理论依据。

Method: 理论推导：在非幺正散射框架下，以TRS†加中心反演对称作为充分条件，证明两端口单通道透射保持无自旋选择性；并讨论条件被破坏或解除单通道约束时出现自旋选择性输运。数值验证：以手性γ-环糊精分子组装体为器件，分别检验幺正情形的经典no-go定理与非幺正情形的补充定理及多通道推论。

Result: 当对应no-go定理条件成立时，组装体的手性诱导自旋选择性(CISS)消失；条件被违反时CISS出现。结果与理论预言一致，构成对经典no-go定理在非幺正区域的重要补充。

Conclusion: no-go定理被成功推广至非幺正散射体系，为研究真实分子组装体中的CISS提供了有效理论框架，并表明TRS†与中心反演对称是抑制自旋选择性的关键对称性判据。

Abstract: The classical two-terminal single-channel no-go theorem relies on time-reversal symmetry (TRS) and scattering-matrix unitarity. In open systems, omitted channels or environment leakage break unitarity, so TRS and the symplectic reciprocity symmetry TRS\(^{\dagger}\) are no longer equivalent, invalidating the classical premises. We prove that if the two-terminal single-channel scattering matrix satisfies TRS\(^{\dagger}\) plus central inversion symmetry, transmission remains no spin selectivity; this result provides an important complement to the classical no-go theorem in nonunitary regime. When these conditions are violated or the single-channel constraint is lifted, spin-selective transport occurs. In charge transport through devices based on chiral \(\gamma\)-cyclodextrin molecular assemblies, we verify separately the classical no-go theorem in unitary case and its supplementary version together with its multichannel corollary in nonunitary case: when the conditions for the corresponding no-go theorem hold, the chirality-induced spin selectivity (CISS) of the assembly disappears, whereas when they are violated, CISS emerges. These results extend the no-go theorem to nonunitary scattering systems and establish an effective theoretical framework for studying CISS in real molecular assemblies.

</details>


### [8] [Probing Chern Topology through Hall-Sensitive Photonic Spin Hall Shifts in a d-wave Altermagnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10706)
*Mahnoor Sajid,Maha Siddiqui,Muzamil Shah*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究了二维Rashba耦合d波交变磁Chern体系在面外交换场下的光子自旋霍尔效应，计算反射高斯光束的螺旋度相关有限束腰位移，揭示其可作为探测Hall活性拓扑相的光学探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将偏振分辨的光子束位移作为探测Hall活性拓扑相及其特征光谱的灵敏手段，需要厘清d波交变磁交换与Chern相变如何共同塑造光学Hall响应与自旋分裂。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the photonic spin Hall effect in a two-dimensional Rashba-coupled $d$-wave altermagnetic Chern system subjected to a uniform out-of-plane exchange field. Band inversions at high-symmetry points generate both a topologically trivial regime $(C=0)$ and Chern-insulating phases $(C= \pm 1)$. Employing the Kubo formalism for the optical conductivity together with the reflection matrix for a substrate-supported conducting sheet, we compute helicity-dependent finite-waist displacements of a reflected Gaussian beam. The resulting optical response exhibits a minimum interband absorption threshold, a Van Hove-type spectral feature, and distinct transitions at the $X$ and $Y$ points arising from the momentum dependence of the exchange field. Under the specified Hall-tensor and polarization sign conventions, the longitudinal helicity half-separation is odd upon reversal of the optical Hall conductivity and correspondingly changes sign between opposite-Chern phases, whereas the transverse half-separation is even in the Hall response. The maximal spin-dependent splitting occur in the vicinity of the pseudo-Brewster reflectance minimum. The longitudinal spin splitting reverses sign between opposite Chern phases, reflecting the exchange-controlled optical Hall response, while its pronounced spectral variations reveal the influence of inequivalent interband transitions induced by the $d$-wave altermagnetic exchange. These findings establish polarization-resolved photonic beam shifts as a sensitive probe of Hall-active topological phases and their characteristic optical spectra.

Abstract: Multidimensional coherent spectroscopy probes the response of a system to a sequence of time-separated light pulses, and has attracted significant interest in recent years. In the frequency space, it generates multidimensional spectra, in which information about different degrees of freedom of the system is generically disentangled. In this work, we develop a quantum geometric formulation of multidimensional optical susceptibilities in insulating systems. Focusing on a two-pulse setup and working in the velocity gauge, we derive analytical expressions for second-order and third-order two-dimensional coherent spectra (2DCS). We focus on the third-order ABB response, which remains finite in centrosymmetric systems where the second-order susceptibility vanishes by inversion symmetry. Its paramagnetic contribution supports an echo signal that probes products of four interband Berry-connection matrix elements, which reduce in two-band systems to bilinear combinations of the quantum geometric tensor of the occupied band. Using continuum and square-lattice models of two-dimensional Chern insulators, we show how the echo resolves the momentum-space sign structure of these geometric quantities. For the $xxyx$ response of the models considered, the number of nodes in the echo signal is related to the band topology. Our results extend the quantum-geometric interpretation of multidimensional spectroscopy to higher-order responses, showing that third-order 2DCS provides access to geometric structures beyond those probed at lower orders.

Abstract: Artificial spin arrays assembled on surfaces enable atomic-scale studies of quantum correlations. Here we establish a hierarchy of connections between spin inelastic electron tunneling spectroscopy (IETS) and entanglement in spin-$1/2$ systems. We first show that the number of spectroscopically active transitions in a spin-$1/2$ dimer serves as an entanglement witness. Using the leading-order tunneling conductance for the tip placed on spin $i$, $dI(i)/dV$, we then relate spectral moments $\mathcal{M}^{(n)}(i)=\int V^n(d^2I(i)/dV^2)\,dV$ to quantum correlations. The zeroth moment probes the one-tangle in systems with a gapped, nondegenerate ground state. For rotationally invariant Heisenberg systems, the first moment yields thermal spin correlators $\langle\boldsymbol S(i)\cdot\boldsymbol S(j)\rangle$, enabling reconstruction of nearest-neighbor concurrence and spatial imaging of entanglement in spin chains. It also provides access to the internal energy and magnetic specific heat, both usable as entanglement witnesses. Applying this approach to experimental IETS data from nanographene spin chains, we find good agreement with theory. We further discuss how the second moment probes longer-range correlations and the inverse moment relates to local spin susceptibility. For spin-$1/2$ trimers, we connect inter-multiplet inelastic spectral weight to genuine tripartite entanglement quantified by the concurrence fill. Finally, we reconcile the local character of IETS with its sensitivity to nonlocal entanglement through an analogy with digital quantum circuits, where Hamiltonian evolution implements multiqubit operations. These results establish IETS as a tool for quantifying and imaging entanglement in engineered on-surface spin systems.

Abstract: Soft transverse optical (TO) phonons in doped SrTiO$_3$ couple to electrons through the polarization field, quadratically in the paraelectric phase and, additionally, linearly in the polar phase due to the onset of spontaneous polarization. We investigate heat flow between a three-dimensional electron gas and soft TO phonons. For two-TO-phonon scattering, the heat flow of hot electrons crosses over from $T^5$ to $T^3$ at the Bloch-Gruneisen temperature. In the polar phase, an additional one-TO-phonon channel emerges, yielding a softer temperature dependence of the heat flow, $T^3$ at low and $T$ at high temperatures, with the spectral weight set by the spontaneous polarization. Abstract: Topological insulators have enabled a new class of spin-sensitive scanning tunneling microscopy (STM) probes based on helical boundary states. However, how these probes generate a net spin-polarized tunneling current remains unclear. We present a theory of spin-polarized STM with topological insulator tips and show that asymmetric tunneling-contact geometry and spin dephasing enable helical boundary states to generate a spin-polarized tunneling current. Within the standard Landauer-Büttiker formalism, we demonstrate this mechanism for a quantum spin Hall (QSH) insulator tip: asymmetric contacts make the two counter-propagating helical edge states, which carry opposite spins, traverse path lengths that differ by $\Delta L$, and spin dephasing induces backscattering that reduces the conductance more strongly for the longer path. For a representative asymmetric contact and weak spin dephasing, the QSH tip exhibits a tunneling spin polarization of $P_s=-17.2\%$ under positive bias, whose magnitude can reach $50\%$ for larger $\Delta L$, and which remains robust against Rashba spin-orbit coupling. Crucially, this polarization, dominated by helical edge transport rather than by bulk states, naturally reverses sign with the bias polarity owing to spin-momentum locking. Abstract: Silicon quantum-dot spin qubits are among the most promising routes to large-scale quantum computation, but the residual 29Si nuclear spins in natural silicon impose a hyperfine dephasing limit that must be overcome by isotopic purification. Here, we report the epitaxial growth of high-purity 28Si on float-zone silicon using an industrial reduced-pressure chemical vapor deposition (RPCVD) system. Secondary-ion mass spectrometry (SIMS) confirms an 28Si isotopic purity of 99.997%, with the 29Si concentration below 30 ppm. Through a systematic vacuum annealing matrix (850-1150 C), combined with atomic force microscopy and SIMS, we investigate the defect-mediated decomposition of the native oxide and the injection of non-equilibrium vacancies. We identify a thermal-budget window below 950 C within which the thin-layer structure of 28Si remains intact. At 1.5 K, magnetotransport measurements reveal a peak Hall mobility of 12,858 cm^2 V^-1 s^-1 and a quantum scattering lifetime of 0.659 ps, corresponding to a single-particle level broadening of 499 micro-eV. A double quantum dot fabricated on this platform exhibits a T2* of 0.62 us, a Hahn-echo coherence time of 28.8 us, and single-qubit gate fidelities of up to 99.9% measured by randomized benchmarking. Our results establish industrial RPCVD as a promising route toward high-coherence, high-fidelity silicon spin qubits.

Abstract: The sub-cycle acceleration of charge carriers in an electronic circuit by tailored terahertz (THz) electric-field waveforms, rather than conventional pulsed bias voltages, opens a transformative frontier for lightwave electronics operating far beyond the scaling limits of gigahertz electronic technologies. Yet gate-tunable coherent control of multi-order and non-perturbative currents has remained elusive. Here, we demonstrate lightwave control over the amplitude, phase flips, and zero-crossings of THz field-driven high-order currents by utilizing phase-locked pairs of single-cycle THz fields in a dual-gated bilayer graphene (BLG) device. By leveraging the in-situ tuning of both bandgap and charge doping, we reveal extreme THz photocurrent rectification and sub-THz-cycle current generation that are governed by a strong field-driven crossover from interband multi-photon coherence to intraband lightwave acceleration, whose competition provides an exceptional sensitivity to the THz-pulse pair drive. Our interferometric nonlinear THz photocurrent spectroscopy of sub-cycle electronic excitations accesses and controls non-perturbative, multi-quantum photocurrents, which manifest as an extreme-order THz frequency comb. Quantum-kinetic simulations map these lightwave-controlled electron trajectories, revealing that sub-THz-cycle ballistic transport underpins gate- and THz-field-driven functionality enabled by electrostatic doping and bandgap tuning within the atomically thin device architecture. The demonstrated on-demand coherent control of nonperturbative photocurrent transport regimes can be extended to a broad range of atomically thin device architectures, opening new avenues for gate- and field-programmable logic, memory, and quantum simulators in which electronic pathways are steered on sub-cycle, petahertz timescales and atomic length scales.

Result: 整流电压随微波功率线性变化，符合GMR整流的预期；由整流信号提取的GMR系数为0.18–0.20%，与独立直流磁阻测量结果吻合；直流电流反转Néel矢量可使GMR整流电压符号发生非易失性、可控的翻转。

Conclusion: GMR整流可作为探测合成反铁磁中Néel矢量动力学的有效电学手段，并为基于Néel矢量的非易失存储与自旋电子器件提供可行读出机制。

Abstract: We observe room-temperature giant magnetoresistance (GMR) rectification driven by Néel vector dynamics in synthetic antiferromagnets. During spin-torque ferromagnetic resonance, the out-of-phase (optical) mode makes the relative angle between the two sub-moments oscillate, producing a rectified voltage due to GMR. This component is isolated from anisotropic and spin Hall magnetoresistance contributions by its angular symmetry, with its voltages scaling linearly with microwave power. We extract a GMR coefficient of 0.18-0.20 % from the rectification, which agrees with independently measured dc magnetoresistance. Reversing the Néel vector with a dc current changes the sign of the GMR rectification voltage in a non-volatile and controlled manner.

### [17] [The Photonic Bath at Infinity: Foundations, Derivations, and Consistency Checks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11816)
*Jian-Sheng Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文将"无穷远光子浴"作为NEGF形式中的有效库，系统澄清环境涨落的引入方式（避免对自由推迟格林函数中出射辐射响应的重复计数），并用四种互补方法推导浴自能；给出1D、平面与球面几何的显式浴自能，重现Planck谱、Stefan–Boltzmann定律与辐射压，并补全光子NEGF能量守恒所需的辐射贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非平衡格林函数（NEGF）框架下描述辐射输运时，需要把无界电磁环境建模为"无穷远光子浴"，以同时计入出射辐射与环境入射涨落。但环境涨落与自由推迟格林函数中已包含的出射辐射响应之间存在重复计数风险，且浴自能的构造缺乏统一、可从边界条件直接导出的表述，几何依赖的显式结果也缺失。

Method: 提出四种互补的浴自能推导途径：(1) 无穷小环境吸收（infinitesimal absorption）；(2) 半无限引线（semi-infinite leads）方法；(3) 自由格林函数求逆；(4) 带出射辐射边界条件的半双线性形式（sesquilinear form）。针对平面几何给出显式的三格点（three-site）矩阵求逆算例，并构造将浴自能与Silver–Müller辐射条件相联系的半双线性形式，进而建立四种方法之间的等价关系，得到一维、平面、球面几何的显式浴自能。

Result: 四种方法相互一致并给出可显式表达的浴自能；所得浴自能可重现Planck谱、Stefan–Boltzmann定律与辐射压，并给出光子NEGF中满足能量守恒所需的辐射贡献项。

Conclusion: "无穷远光子浴"可作为NEGF中自洽的辐射库：在避免出射辐射重复计数的前提下，环境涨落可被一致地纳入；四法等价且适用于多种几何；所得自能具备正确的热力学与力学极限，并闭合了光子NEGF的能量守恒描述。

Abstract: The photonic bath at infinity represents the unbounded electromagnetic environment as an effective reservoir in the nonequilibrium Green's function (NEGF) formalism. It accounts for outgoing radiation and incoming electromagnetic fluctuations, allowing radiative transport to be described consistently. We clarify how environmental fluctuations are incorporated without double-counting the outgoing radiation response already contained in the free retarded Green's function. We develop four complementary approaches to deriving the bath self-energy: infinitesimal environmental absorption, semi-infinite leads, inversion of the free Green's function, and sesquilinear forms with outgoing radiation boundary conditions. In particular, we present an explicit three-site matrix inversion for a planar geometry and introduce a sesquilinear form that relates the bath self-energy to the Silver--Müller radiation condition. We establish the connections among these approaches and obtain explicit bath self-energies for one-dimensional, planar, and spherical geometries. The resulting bath self-energies reproduce the Planck spectrum, the Stefan--Boltzmann law, and radiation pressure, and provide the radiative contribution required for energy conservation in photon NEGF.

</details>

Result: 观测到直至ε_ph = 8的高阶PIROs；确认相关模式为声速s = 3.4 km/s的横声学声子；亚声速区振幅调制与理论吻合；在声速势垒(ε_dc = ε_ph)处观察到电子-声子相互作用振荡的相移。

Conclusion: 直流加热结合HIROs振幅调制是探测高阶PIROs的有效手段；声速势垒处的相移独立验证了近期实验结果，为电子-声子耦合在漂移区间的行为提供了新证据。

Abstract: We report the observation of phonon-induced resistance oscillations (PIROs) up to order $\varepsilon_\mathrm{ph} = 8$ in a two-dimensional electron gas containing artificial scatterers, activated by electron heating due to an applied dc current which enables phonon emission. Under equilibrium conditions, only two weak PIRO maxima are detectable. The dc current simultaneously generates Hall field induced resistance oscillations (HIROs). A precise analysis of their amplitude, which is modulated by the double resonance with PIROs, dramatically enhances the visibility of the latter compared to the equilibrium case. We identify transverse acoustic phonons with a sound velocity of $s = 3.4$\,km/s as the relevant mode and analyze the oscillatory amplitude modulation in the subsonic regime ($\varepsilon_{\mathrm{dc}} < \varepsilon_{\mathrm{ph}}$) within the theoretical framework of Dmitriev \textit{et al.} At the sound barrier ($\varepsilon_{\mathrm{dc}} = \varepsilon_{\mathrm{ph}}$), where the electron drift velocity reaches the speed of sound, we observe a phase shift in the oscillatory electron-phonon interaction that independently confirms recent experimental findings.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有耦合线构造非阿贝尔分数量子霍尔态通常依赖空间变化的磁场来提供动量转移（momentum shift），在莫尔晶格体系中缺乏自然对应；同时晶格可公度性（commensurability）如何影响非阿贝尔态的可达性及其边缘/界面动力学尚不清楚。

Method: 采用耦合线（coupled-wire）构造方法，利用晶格动量仅在倒格矢意义下守恒（modulo reciprocal lattice vector）这一性质，在无外加强磁场梯度的情况下构造半填充莫尔体系中的Pfaffian与anti-Pfaffian态；进而分析二者界面的相互作用，包括Umklapp散射对电荷扇区的开隙效应，以及Majorana模按平移对称性的分组。

Result: 晶格公度性使得两种非阿贝尔态均可在无空间变化动量转移的条件下由耦合线实现；在Pfaffian–anti-Pfaffian界面上，Umklapp过程能使反传电荷扇区开隙，而四个同向传播的Majorana模自然归并为两个由平移对称性组织的扇区。

Conclusion: 晶格公度性从根本上改变了非阿贝尔态的耦合线可达性，并提供了直接约束其边缘与界面动力学的微观动量结构。

Abstract: We study Pfaffian and anti-Pfaffian non-Abelian fractional quantum anomalous Hall states in half-filled moiré lattice systems. Owing to crystal-momentum conservation modulo a reciprocal lattice vector, these lattice systems admit coupled-wire constructions of the two phases without requiring spatially varying magnetic-field-induced momentum shifts. For the resulting Pfaffian--anti-Pfaffian interface, a Umklapp process can gap the counter-propagating charge sector, while the remaining four co-propagating Majorana modes naturally group into two sectors organized by translation symmetries. Our results show that lattice commensurability qualitatively changes the coupled-wire accessibility of non-Abelian states while providing a microscopic momentum structure that directly constrains their edge and interface dynamics.

</details>

Abstract: Monolayer stanene exhibits strong intrinsic spin-orbit coupling (SOC) and notably distinct low-energy band features involving both $\Gamma$ and K valleys together with strong electron-hole asymmetry among group-IV monolayers. Its plasmonic properties and external-field tunability remain largely unclarified. Here, we systematically investigate its high-energy plasmon structures and low-energy collective excitation modulated by temperature, electrostatic doping and perpendicular electric field. Calculations use a $sp^3$ tight-binding model with SOC and sublattice staggered potentials, combined with the finite-temperature multiband Lindhard function under the random-phase approximation. Besides the $\pi$ and $\pi+\sigma$ plasmon known in graphene/silicene, stanene supports two new high-energy plasmon branches. For low-energy plasmons, thermal excitation extends the accessible energy-momentum range while preserving high quality factors and collective coherence. Electrostatic doping yields distinct plasmon behaviors: moderate doping gives K-valley dominated intraband $\sqrt{q}$ Dirac plasmons; heavy electron doping retains $\sqrt{q}$ dispersion via Drude contributions from $\Gamma$ and K valleys; heavy hole doping triggers interband transitions near the $\Gamma$ point, producing new $\sigma$-$\sigma$ and $\sigma$-$\sigma^*$ interband plasmon modes and complex multi-branch spectra. Furthermore, the perpendicular electric field enables continuous tuning of the $\sqrt{q}$ plasmon via band reconstruction below 0.3 V/Å and induces extra plasmon modes near band-gap closure. These tunable low-energy plasmons span terahertz-to-mid-infrared frequencies, promising for nanophotonic applications.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 采用栅极脉冲光谱技术：以量子点本身作为探针，测量SET能谱对量子点光谱的影响；通过偏压依赖关系提取SET的电子温度；随后将体系切换为连接单一储库的双量子点（典型的读取构型），在少电子稳定图中系统绘制其特征锁存（latching）行为。

Result: SET能谱会直接影响量子点的脉冲栅极光谱；SET电子温度强烈依赖所加偏压，在仅50 μV偏压下即比参考实验高出100 mK；双量子点—单储库构型的特征锁存行为可通过少电子稳定图进行系统标定与调控。

Conclusion: 在SET兼作储库与传感器的器件中，应优先选择不依赖与储库耦合的自旋量子比特初始化方案；同时，读取用双量子点构型的锁存行为可被有效调控，为器件参数优化提供依据。

Abstract: Many currently envisaged scalable architectures for Si/SiGe spin qubit devices utilize gate-controlled multi-dot configurations characterized by their peculiar Coulomb blockade physics. One example is the concurrent use of a single electron transistor (SET) as a charge sensor and as a reservoir for adjacent qubit quantum dots (QD). We find that the SET energy spectrum impacts pulsed gate spectroscopy of the QD when the SET simultaneously acts as the reservoir and the sensor. Then, using the qubit QD as a probe, we quantify how strongly the electron temperature of the SET depends on the applied bias. It surpasses a reference experiment by 100 $mK$ even at 50 $\mu V$ bias. Our results provide a strong incentive for choosing spin qubit initialization schemes which do not rely on coupling to the reservoir in such devices. Switching to a double quantum dot connected to a single reservoir, a frequent configuration for qubit readout, we finally demonstrate the tunability of the characteristic latching behavior by systematically mapping it in the few-electron stability diagram.

*Théo Torres,Renaud Côte,Marc Pachebat,Antonin Coutant*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 体-边对应表明体能拓扑性质可保证受保护边缘态的存在，通常伴随背散射免疫这一有吸引力的性质。作者希望将这一图景拓展到经典（声学）体系，探索平移与手性对称性反对易时是否可产生由D类拓扑超导体同一不变量所保护的拓扑相，从而把拓扑超导中的对称性分类思想移植到经典波动系统。

Conclusion: 经典时间反演不变系统中平移与手性对称性的反对易可产生由D类拓扑超导体相同不变量保护的拓扑相，并可在声学网络中实验实现；其边缘态具有背散射免疫性，说明拓扑超导的对称性分类与保护机制可有效推广至经典波动平台，为声学拓扑器件与鲁棒波导设计提供依据。

Abstract: The bulk-boundary correspondence states that topological properties of a bulk material guarantee the existence of protected edge modes, oftentimes associated with the attractive property of backscattering immunity. In this Letter, we consider a time reversal invariant classical system with both translation and chiral, or sublattice, symmetries. We show that when these two symmetries anti-commute, the material exhibits a topological phase protected by the same invariant as type D topological superconductors. We realize such a system experimentally and observe the existence of the associated edge modes in an acoustic network. Our measurements are in excellent agreement with theoretical predictions from discrete lattice models. Finally, we show that these edge modes are immune to backscattering for perturbations consisting in two subsequent identical defects.

Abstract: Rhombohedral graphene multilayers provide a flexible platform for correlated physics associated with their flat-band electronic structure. While a variety of correlated states appear in these systems as a function of bias and electrostatic doping, promoting specific correlated flavor symmetry breaking states remains a challenge. Here we show that magnetic encapsulation by CrGeTe3 achieves this in rhombohedral pentalayer graphene, by using switchable exchange couplings induced by ferromagnetic substrates. We show that correlations in rhombohedral multilayers at charge neutrality strongly renormalize the exchange proximity splitting,giving rise to collective spin modes whose character is switched by the parallel or antiparallel alignment of the encapsulation. We further show that upon doping of the flat bands, spin triplet f-wave superconducting solutions emerge, whose layer and spin selectivity is controlled by the magnetic encapsulation, hosting in-gap excitations at the edges. Our results establish magnetic alignment as a means to control spin symmetry breaking and unconventional superconductivity in rhombohedral pentalayer graphene.

Abstract: We propose and analyze a three-terminal superconducting transistor switch in which a gate-defined quantum dot region interrupts a flux-controlled loop. The output supercurrent is switched off completely when the two loop arms couple to the dot with equal tunneling strengths and half a flux quantum threads the loop. The device then operates as a symmetric SQUID at half flux. The condition follows from a single geometric factor $\chi$, which reduces the three-terminal problem to an equivalent two-terminal Anderson impurity model and makes the switching mechanism independent of the Coulomb interaction, the dot level position, the total hybridization and temperature. These enter only through a prefactor and thus decide how tolerant the device is to imperfect barrier tuning. Numerical renormalization group calculations across the full coupling parameter space show that the resonance is embedded in a broad region of elevated switching ratio at intermediate hybridization $\Gamma \approx \Delta$, while both weaker and stronger coupling reduce it to a thin isolated line. Finite temperature smooths the underlying singlet-doublet transition and lowers the supercurrent amplitude, but affects the open and closed states comparably and leaves the switching ratio largely intact. Within a zero-bandwidth two-level model, a second dot level is shown to open a leakage channel that grows as the two levels approach in energy. Raising the output barrier and the realistic presence of inter-level Coulomb repulsion, however, suppress the leakage.

Abstract: Tensor network methods have been instrumental in addressing exponentially large many-body problems. Most recently, these techniques have also been applied to single-particle tight-binding Hamiltonians, allowing observables to be evaluated on exponentially large lattices. Here, we show that the scale-separated nature of these methods allows for a natural encoding of self-similar structures, such as the Sierpiński gasket, a scale-invariant fractal with Hausdorff dimension $\log 3/\log 2 \approx 1.58$, yielding an exact and compact representation of Hamiltonians on such lattices. Using this encoding, we study a Chern insulator, the flux kagome model, confined to this fractal geometry, and develop methods to investigate its topological states within a tensor network framework. This allows us to reach much higher fractal generations than would otherwise be possible with dense-matrix methods, resolving the density of states of a deep fractal with over 100 million sites. Furthermore, the larger structures allow us to resolve topology in smaller gaps, suggesting that these methods could be instrumental in understanding the interplay between topology and non-integer dimensionality.

TL;DR: 论文将"与哈密顿量反对易的对称性对零模的保护"表述为对称表示加法群模掉遗忘相容分级的映射之像所得的商群，借助Mackey方法对有限分级对称群构造全部不可约超余表示（允许反幺正操作与投影因子系统），得到2种复型和10种实型结构类型，并给出特征标判据与整数值的扇区指标（0、Z、Z₂），进而联系体拓扑与sigma模型拓扑项。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Symmetries that anticommute with a Hamiltonian can protect zero modes by obstructing their removal through symmetry-compatible perturbations. We formulate this obstruction as a quotient of the additive group of symmetry representations by the image of the map that forgets a compatible representation grading. To compute the quotient, we use Mackey's method to construct all irreducible super corepresentations of finite graded symmetry groups, allowing antiunitary operations and projective factor systems. Starting from the unitary Hamiltonian-preserving normal subgroup, this construction yields two complex and ten real structural types, characterized by residual Altland--Zirnbauer symmetry algebras. The residual and induced graded modules have isomorphic graded commutants, and their represented symmetry algebras share a graded Morita class that is super opposite to that of the commutant. We derive character criteria that identify these types. Exhaustive enumeration of the restriction relations gives independent sector contributions $0$, $Z$, or $Z_2$, with only integer factors in the purely unitary case. In the finite semisimple setting, the complete index vanishes if and only if all zero modes can be lifted by a symmetry-compatible perturbation. Applications to one-dimensional end states and interpolation cylinders of disordered boundary Dirac theories recover protected multiplet counts and connect the quotient to bulk topology and sigma-model topological terms using bulk--boundary correspondence, texture-index theorems, and fermion-phase calculations.

Abstract: We propose a spin-valve effect in which an insulating magnetic van der Waals multilayer controls the conductance of an adjacent non-magnetic conductor by electrostatic coupling, without transport-electrons entering the magnetic layers. The coupling is induced by the variations of the electronic structure when the relative orientation of the magnetization of adjacent monolayers changes from parallel to antiparallel, modifying both the dielectric constant and band alignment. We consider two types of coupling and device concepts: charge transfer to a graphene monolayer and capacitive coupling in a single-electron transistor. We use density functional calculations to assess the magnitude of the effect in various materials, including CrI$_3$, CrBr$_3$, CrCl$_3$ and NiBr$_2$. We predict magnetoresistance ratios in the range of 1.14-1.20 for the charge-transfer device and up to $10^3$ in the single-electron transistor.

Abstract: Spin-torque nano-oscillators are promising nanoscale microwave sources, yet their performance is often limited by narrow operational ranges and sensitivity to device parameters. Here, we numerically investigate the dynamics of compensation domain walls in ferrimagnetic systems driven by spin-transfer torque as a microwave source candidate. These walls arise in structures with adjacent regions of different compensation character, where the competition between exchange and Zeeman energies under an applied magnetic field stabilizes a distinct magnetic texture. We demonstrate steady current-driven rotation of compensation domain walls over a broad range of current densities within the model. To elucidate the underlying mechanism, we develop a collective-coordinate model and derive an analytical expression for the oscillation frequency, which quantitatively reproduces the dependence on current and magnetic field observed in micromagnetic simulations. Our results identify compensation domain walls as a candidate platform for tunable spin-torque nano-oscillators with intrinsic field-induced confinement.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手性分子桥通过手性诱导自旋选择性（CISS）效应，有可能在硅量子点之间提供新的两比特纠缠资源，但这一设想缺乏严格的量子信息层面判定，同时CISS的微观起源尚未定论、硅自旋量子比特的硬件窗口也未量化。

Abstract: Chiral molecular bridges between silicon quantum dots can provide novel two-qubit entangling resources via the chirality-induced spin-selectivity (CISS) effect. We evaluate this using Makhlin invariants. Using the Kaplan-Shekhtman-Entin-Wohlman-Aharony (KSEA) gauge argument, we prove this interaction is locally equivalent to isotropic exchange; thus, a single chiral bond does not constitute a new entangling class. Breaking this gauge symmetry reveals genuine chiral utility: chiral exchange converts a uniform transverse field into a synthetic gradient, bringing the two-qubit propagator within $7\times10^{-4}$ of the CNOT class in invariant space 4.7 times faster than an equivalent orthogonal gradient. Shuttling a spin through bridges with non-collinear axes implements a non-Abelian $\mathrm{SU}(2)$ holonomy, yielding rotationally non-reciprocal transport and closed-loop geometric gates that suppress dynamical-phase noise to second order. To address the unsettled microscopic origin of CISS, we introduce a unified transport channel combining coherent rotation, a direction-reversing spin filter, and pointer-basis dephasing. This model obeys an exact $\tanh$ composition law for pumped polarization and a closed-form transverse decay law, yielding four observables that experimentally over-determine the bridge's coherent fraction $F_c$. Finally, we quantify silicon-specific hardware constraints, establishing the Landau-Zener valley-adiabaticity window ($E_v \geq 2.1\sqrt{\hbar v}$) and charge-noise limit ($\sigma_J/J \leq 3\%$) required for $99\%$ gate fidelity.

Using semiclassical quantization of electron skipping orbits, we show that the drift velocity equals 1/2 of the electron Fermi velocity in the limit of a large number of edge channels crossing the Fermi level. This result, being universal for a hard edge and for isotropic dispersion, is checked numerically for massive electrons and for massless electrons in graphene. The drift velocity at smooth electrostatically defined edges lacks such universality. In the case of anisotropic dispersion, the semiclassical drift velocity at the hard edge reduces to a coordinate-velocity correlation along the cyclotron orbit.

Abstract: Engineering large and reproducible valley splittings is essential for the scalable operation of silicon spin qubits. In Si/SiGe quantum dot devices, valley splitting arises predominantly from atomic-scale disorder and is therefore inherently random, motivating approaches that introduce a large deterministic contribution. Strain-assisted Wiggle Wells provide a route to deterministic valley splitting by combining an oscillatory Ge concentration in the Si well with shear strain; however, generating the required strain in a manner compatible with large-scale architectures remains a challenge. A graphical representation in Bloch space is used to construct pumping schemes. In this framework, the Bloch vector traces a closed loop that evolves in time. The Chern number of the synthetic band is then determined by the motion of this loop. Quantum pumping phenomena are demonstrated for a filled band and for a bosonic wave packet driven by Bloch oscillations. We further show that the Haldane model can be mapped to the driven extended SSH chain by replacing a quasimomentum by the pumping time. The quantized charge pump indicates that the Chern number of a two-dimensional model can be read out from a one-dimensional pumping protocol, offering an accessible route for detecting higher-dimensional topology, such as in photonic and cold-atom platforms.

Abstract: We investigate the formation, stability, and dynamics of three-dimensional (3D) elongated multipole quantum droplets (QDs) in a Bose-Einstein condensate (BEC) of polar molecules with long-range interactions between molecular electric dipoles. The condensate is confined by a 2D trapping potential, which is a combination of the harmonic-oscillator potential and a central Gaussian potential hill. The system is modeled by an extended Gross-Pitaevskii (GP) equation, incorporating the dipole-dipole interactions and beyond-mean-field Lee-Huang-Yang (LHY) correction, which play a crucial role in stabilizing self-bound QD structures. In regimes of weak and strong confinement, stable multipole states with the number of lobes $n=2S$ are created by means of the imaginary-time-propagation (ITP) method from inputs with vorticity $S\leq 2$. The so created multipole QDs carry no angular momentum. Stable higher-order multipole states can be produced by the ITP method from inputs with $S\geq 3$ up to $S=6$ (i.e., $2S=12$ lobes) only under strong confinement and when the molecule number $N$ exceeds a threshold value. We analyze the dependence of the chemical potential, energy per particle, and peak density on $N$ and $S$ in the weak- and strong-confinement regimes. A noteworthy fact is that, below another threshold value of $N$, the minimum-energy (ground) state is created by the input with $S=1$, rather than $S=0$. Simulations of perturbed evolution reveal that, despite satisfying energetic stability criteria, only a subset of the multipole QDs are truly stable, with higher-order ones tending to develop instabilities or relax toward lower-order states. These results highlight the essential roles of the trapping potential, anisotropy of the dipole-dipole interactions, and LHY-induced repulsion in the formation and structural stability of multipole QDs.

Abstract: An interacting condensate can retain an observable imprint of its preparation after the interactions are switched off. For light, accessing this regime requires separating the formation of a photon condensate from the material medium that redistributes its energy. We investigate a multimode-cavity scheme in which an interacting dark-state-polariton condensate is coherently converted into a confined photonic Bose-Einstein condensate as the atom-cavity coupling is removed. The polariton mass, matter fraction, and particle number control the condensate occupation during preparation. Remarkably, changing the mass also modifies the condensate's response to repulsion through the spatial overlap of its orbitals, even when the entire single-particle spectrum is held fixed. Ideal mode-preserving conversion conserves the full natural-orbital occupation spectrum, while finite-time few-body calculations show that additional occupation changes during transfer are much smaller than those established during interacting preparation. Once decoupled from matter, the photons evolve under the free cavity Hamiltonian while retaining correlations acquired in the interacting gas. Interference between cavity modes produces oscillations in the photon density, while resonant pair correlations persist after interaction removal and distinguish the resulting photon state from a quadratic thermal state with the same one-body density matrix.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Love方程是Fredholm积分方程的典型代表，广泛应用于静电问题与一维可解模型；在一维量子气体中其解对应赝快度归一化密度，但数值求解缺乏简洁、高精度且点数少的通用逼近方案，尤其缺乏覆盖全部耦合区间的闭式能量表达式。

Method: 采用Chebyshev节点上的Hermite插值构造解的参数化表示，逼近第一类与第二类Love方程的正规化解；沿同一框架推导解的第二、四、六阶矩的近似公式；针对Lieb-Liniger模型，将小耦合与大耦合的已知渐近结果与中间耦合区间的Hermite-Chebyshev插值拼接，构造无量纲基态能量的闭式估计。

Result: 当插值点数低至17+2时即可获得"remarkably accurate"的结果；Love方程解的矩(二、四、六阶)可用同一插值框架高效求得；所得基态能量闭式估计对全部无量纲相互作用参数取值均高度精确；并据此计算了无量纲局域双粒子关联函数与Lieb-Liniger模型的black soliton解。

Conclusion: Hermite-Chebyshev插值为Love方程及一维量子气体赝快度密度提供了一种点数极少、精度极高的通用逼近工具，兼具解析闭式表达与数值效率，可自然推广至矩的计算及相关物理量(关联函数、孤子解)的求解。

Abstract: The Love equation is a paradigmatic example of a Fredholm integral equation of great interest in mathematics and physics, where its applications range from electrostatic problems to exactly solvable models for one-dimensional systems. In this work we propose a method to approximate the normalized solutions of Love equations of the first and second kind via a parametrization obtained by Hermite interpolation at Chebyshev nodes. Following the same approach, we also obtain approximations for the second, fourth and sixth momenta of the solution of the Love equations. We focus in particular on one-dimensional quantum gases described by the Lieb-Liniger and the Gaudin-Yang models. For these models, the solutions of Love equations correspond to the normalized densities of pseudorapidities. We show that remarkably accurate results can be obtained using the Hermite-Chebyshev interpolation with a number of points as low as 17+2. In the case of the Lieb-Liniger model, we also propose a very accurate closed-form estimate of the dimensionless ground state energy valid for all values of the dimensionless interaction parameter incorporating known results for small and large couplings supplemented by the Hermite-Chebyshev interpolation for intermediate couplings. Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 提出一种确定性变分框架，构造"马尔可夫屏蔽典型态"（MST），用局部马尔可夫熵替代非局域von Neumann熵做自由能最小化，得到从基态到无穷高温极限连续、可唯一确定、局域约化密度矩阵逼近吉布斯态的热纯态表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统热平衡典型态（如随机高温柔态）本质上是随机的，存在统计涨落，且无法与基态连续衔接，难以作为可扩展的波函数方法来描述热平衡。

Method: 将自由能最小化中的非局域von Neumann熵替换为局部、计算可行的马尔可夫熵，构造确定性变分框架，求取唯一的MST态。

Result: 建立了MST自由能的严格上下界，证明其紧邻热自由能；MST态的局域约化密度矩阵紧密逼近吉布斯态，并覆盖从基态到无限温极限的连续区间。

Conclusion: 该工作将典型性概念推广到热平衡的确定性纯态表示，提供了一种可扩展的波函数方法，在经典与量子计算中均有潜在应用。

Abstract: Pure states of quantum many-body systems at thermal equilibrium are known to be typical, in a sense that a randomly chosen pure state from an appropriate energy shell reproduces the Gibbs-state expectation values of most observables acting on a small subsystem. However, such typical states are intrinsically stochastic, conventionally generated from the random high-temperature state, suffer from statistical fluctuations, and do not provide a continuous connection to the ground state. Here, we introduce a deterministic variational framework for constructing a Markov-Shielded Typical (MST) state, a unique thermal pure-state representative whose local reduced density matrices closely approximate those of the Gibbs state, from the ground state up to infinite-temperature limit. Our approach reformulates free-energy minimization by replacing the nonlocal von Neumann entropy with a local, computationally tractable Markov entropy, where we establish rigorous upper and lower bounds of MST free energy to lie in the very vicinity of the thermal free energy. This framework extends the concept of typicality to deterministic pure-state representations of thermal equilibrium, offering a scalable wave-function approach with potential applications to both classical and quantum computing.

</details>

Result: 气泡生长指数α≈2/3，符合惯性流体动力学粗化机制；C_ag(t,t_w)表现出依赖等待时间的弛豫即老化；不同等待时间的数据以ℓ(t)/ℓ(t_w)为变量塌缩到同一标度函数，该函数呈幂律衰减，指数λ≈3；气泡与液滴的粗化指数及老化指数明显不同。

Conclusion: 气泡粗化由惯性流体动力学主导（α≈2/3）并伴随动力学标度老化（λ≈3）；尽管气泡与液滴均为不连通形貌，其粗化与老化普适类并不相同，说明形貌连通性不足以决定相分离动力学指数。

Abstract: We investigate the coarsening and aging dynamics of vapor bubbles during liquid-vapor phase separation in a three-dimensional single-component Lennard-Jones fluid using large-scale molecular dynamics simulations. Following a temperature quench from a homogeneous high-temperature state into the two-phase coexistence region, vapor bubbles nucleate and grow in the liquid background. The characteristic bubble size follows a power-law growth, $\ell(t)\sim t^\alpha$, with a growth exponent $\alpha \simeq 2/3$, characteristic of inertial hydrodynamic coarsening. We also investigate the two-time autocorrelation function $C_{ag}(t,t_w)$ which follows waiting-time dependent relaxation, revealing aging. Furthermore, data at different waiting times collapse onto a scaling function when plotted as a function of the length ratio $\ell(t)/\ell(t_w)$, demonstrating dynamical scaling during aging. The corresponding scaling function exhibits an algebraic decay with an exponent $\lambda \simeq 3$. Interestingly, bubble and droplet growth exhibit distinctly different domain-growth and aging exponents despite both being disconnected morphologies.

</details>

In addition, we derived the exact expression for spontaneous magnetization. The usage of both expressions made it possible to perform the comprehensive studies of thermodynamic and magnetic properties of the Calandre lattice itself and a wide variety of its affiliated lattices. Among them, diversified interrelations between Calandre lattice and already known and even some novel lattices. These lattices have also revealed such effects as magnetic frustrations, multiple phase transitions and magnetic reentrancy.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 该结果把ergotropy严格形式化为"偏离平衡程度的量化指标"，建立了量子热力学与最优传输理论之间的桥梁，为ergotropy提供了新的几何与信息论解释。

Abstract: We show that the ergotropy (a.k.a available energy) of a thermally isolated system is the $1$-Wasserstein distance between two energy probability density functions. Specifically, between the energy profile of the system state, and the energy profile of its associated passive state. We prove this for both classical and quantum systems, and illustrate it with examples. Our result formalises the intuition that ergotropy is a quantifier of distance from equilibrium

</details>

Abstract: We present a method for computing thermodynamic properties of classical spin systems by reformulating the partition function as a sum over even subgraphs of the interaction graph. Starting from the high-temperature expansion, we show that the expansion coefficients are determined by the weight distribution of the null space of the binary incidence matrix over $\mathbb{F}_2$, and that this statement holds verbatim for multi-spin interactions once the graph is replaced by a hypergraph. We introduce a Monte Carlo scheme based on the $1/t$ Wang-Landau algorithm that performs a random walk directly in this null space. Because the weight distribution is temperature independent, a single estimate yields the zero-field partition function, and the thermodynamic quantities obtained from its temperature derivatives, at arbitrary temperature. We validate the approach against exact finite-size results on the two-dimensional triangular-lattice Ising model, apply it to the three- and four-dimensional hypercubic Ising models where no exact solution is available, and to the two- and three-dimensional Baxter-Wu models, whose three-spin interactions place them outside the reach of Pfaffian methods. For the two-body models we compare against conventional Wang-Landau sampling in energy space; for the Baxter-Wu models we validate against exact enumeration at the smallest size in each dimension.

</details>

Abstract: The Mpemba effect depends on both the relaxation dynamics and the protocol used to prepare the initial states. Here, this dependence is investigated using the time-delayed Newton cooling law, comparing protocols in which two samples are exposed to piecewise-constant bath temperatures before being placed in a common final bath. For protocols with at most two quenches and a common waiting time, exact conditions for the occurrence of the effect are derived. They rule out the Pontus protocol within this model and show that the synchronous two-reservoir protocol maximizes the effect magnitude within the class considered. The robustness of this optimum is then examined by allowing asynchronous quenches or an additional quench for one sample. Numerical optimization of these extensions identifies the synchronous two-reservoir protocol as the maximizing limit in the cases studied. This evidence motivates a conjecture that the same protocol remains optimal for more general bath histories, although that broader statement is not proved.

Abstract: Periodically driven clean interacting systems generically heat up, which destroys discrete time-crystalline order. Here we ask whether continuous weak measurement can stabilize such order. We study a long-range interacting spin chain whose local occupation is continuously monitored. Measurements generally induce a transition from volume-law to area-law entanglement. Yet low entanglement alone is not enough: along typical quantum trajectories, the system still reaches a featureless state with no subharmonic response. In contrast, the most-probable trajectory, obtained by suppressing measurement fluctuations, shows robust period doubling. To connect these limits, we introduce a bounded-noise protocol that restricts measurement fluctuations to a finite window. Narrow windows preserve period doubling. Intermediate windows produce coexisting time-crystalline and non-time-crystalline trajectories. Over time, trajectories leave the time-crystalline group one by one, so the order is long lived but finite. Wide windows destroy the order. Our results establish bounded measurement noise, discarding the large-deviation trajectories, as a route to time-crystalline order in clean systems. They also show that the measurement-induced area-law phase is necessary, but not sufficient, for this order.

</details>

Abstract: The kinetic approach for q-distribution is extended through a "bottom-up" procedure to the corresponding entropic formula. In this way, the validity of Tsallis' formula, S_q(W) = k_B ln_q(W), is established from first principles. The resulting q-entropy is additive and the zeroth law of thermodynamics is automatically satisfied because the conventional identification beta = 1/(k_B T) remains valid. Entropy, energy, and all relevant quantities for both discrete and continuous cases are evaluated using standard (linear) averages. The "bottom-up" and "top-down" approaches yield the same q-distribution for velocities. The standard results are naturally recovered in the limit q -> 1.

Motivation: 平衡系统临界点处扩散系数或趋于零或保持非临界，而活性物质的对齐相变属于非平衡过程，其输运行为是否遵循类似规律尚不清楚；同时二维连续对称系统受Mermin-Wagner定理限制难以存在序-无序相变，需要理解活性系统中该限制如何被突破。

Result: D_s在临界点出现强烈增强，与平衡临界行为根本不同；D_s在整个有序相中表现出显著的有限尺寸效应，这在平衡体系中不存在；增强的扩散产生有效长程相互作用，从而放松了Mermin-Wagner定理对二维连续对称系统序-无序相变的限制。

Conclusion: 活性物质的对齐相变伴随反常的输运增强，其机制可归因于增强扩散诱导的有效长程相互作用，这对非平衡输运的定量理论构建及实验研究具有重要意义。

Abstract: We simulate dynamics in two-dimensional aligning active-matter models exhibiting order-disorder transitions in the velocity field. Analysis reveals that the single-particle diffusivity, $D_s$, exhibits strong enhancements at criticality which we quantify via a finite-size scaling method. This dynamical behavior at nonequilibrium flocking transitions is fundamentally different from that at the criticality of equilibrium systems, where diffusivities either vanish or remain non-critical. Furthermore, $D_s$ exhibits pronounced finite-size effects throughout the ordered phase, which also are absent in equilibrium settings. We interpret these counterintuitive transport anomalies within the framework of a flocking theory. Our results suggest that enhanced diffusion generates effective long-range interactions. This relaxes the constraints of the Mermin-Wagner theorem that otherwise prevents order-disorder transitions in two dimensions for systems possessing continuous symmetry. These results are of much experimental relevance and have important implications in formulating quantitative theories for nonequilibrium transports.

Abstract: Band topology and non-Hermitian spectral theory are now applied to mechanical metamaterials, chiral active fluids, stochastic networks, and open quantum systems. We give a tutorial synthesis organized by holonomy and by one question: what can the spectral data of a linear generator reveal about detailed balance? Three holonomies recur: the Berry phase of a band, the Burgers vector of a dislocation (the integrated torsion of the defect geometry), and the cycle affinity of a Markov network (the holonomy of the connection $\ln(W_{ij}/W_{ji})$). We rederive the Chern numbers $C_m=m(\mathrm{sgn}\,\Omega+\mathrm{sgn}\,\nu_o)$ of a compressible odd-viscous fluid and restate the dislocation zero-mode count of Paulose, Chen, and Vitelli as a pairing of integrated torsion with topological polarization. Our central message is that spectral topology can obstruct detailed balance but does not determine the magnitude of dissipation. Detailed balance is self-adjointness in the inner product fixed by the stationary state, so a non-real eigenvalue, an exceptional point (EP), or a nonzero point-gap winding number of a Markov generator rules it out and forces entropy production $\sigma>0$, with $\sigma\ge k_B j^2/D$ for any current, whereas a real, diagonalizable spectrum does not in general certify it. In the Hatano-Nelson ring, which is normal yet has complex spectrum, $\sigma$ sweeps $(0,\infty)$ at fixed winding number. EPs need a separate assessment of the dynamics: with generalized detailed balance including time-reversal parity, an EP is compatible with equilibrium only through time-reversal-odd dynamics, as at critical damping of a Langevin oscillator, whereas an EP of a system with only even variables, such as an odd elastic medium, certifies a nonequilibrium steady state; in both cases $\sigma$ is analytic across the EP. We propose benchtop tests for each statement.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 采用动态熵（dynamic-entropy）形式：在固定滞后相关的约束下，最大化对数飞行长度序列的因果熵，由此得到定义在紧区间上的连续自旋链；链的深度由数据选择，从而区分更新型、马尔可夫型及具有更长记忆的行走者。一阶情形可精确解析求解，并利用交换长短飞行的对偶性给出分析。

Conclusion: 更新假设在多数真实运动数据中被拒绝，需要用具有有限记忆深度的马尔可夫链式描述替代独立的飞行抽样；该动态熵框架提供了可检验、可解释记忆深度与相互作用结构的统一工具。

Abstract: Lévy walks are recognized by the histogram of their flight lengths, while the renewal hypothesis on which the textbook model rests, that every flight is drawn afresh, is seldom tested. We test it with the dynamic-entropy formalism. Maximizing the causal entropy of a sequence of logarithmic flight lengths at fixed lagged correlations yields a chain of continuous spins on a compact interval, whose depth, selected by the data, tells a renewal walker from a Markovian one and from one with a longer memory. With one lag the chain is exactly solvable. A duality that exchanges long and short flights locates a first-order condensation of the flights at the upper cutoff, with the logarithmic range of the flights in the role of system size, and a positive memory makes the histogram heavier-tailed than the rule that generates each flight. For groups, the same construction separates the memory of each individual from delayed interactions and from drives common to all units, which an analysis of the group average confounds, and it gives the collective speed a critical point. On open data, renewal is rejected in seven of ten conditions of Drosophila larvae and in every termite, whose memory reaches a few flights back and lowers the exponent of the histogram by more than half a unit below that of the one-step rule. Wild baboons align with where their nearest neighbors, those ahead above all, were heading 10 to 30 s earlier, and homing pigeons are coupled across their small flocks. Zebrafish T cells, a negative control, remember their own speed, but no delayed coupling between the speeds of different cells survives the controls.

</details>

Abstract: Natural systems are often composed of processes evolving on multiple timescales and coupled through complex interaction networks. Inferring such networks is especially challenging when timescales are widely separated, since resolving the fastest processes while accurately sampling the slowest ones is generally prohibitive. In this work, we propose a framework to reconstruct a multiscale interaction tree by combining a rescaled version of the mutual information rate, its conditional counterpart, and the ordering of timescales. We derive an exact theory for stationary Gaussian processes, finding that these measures identify directly connected processes, with the asymptotic mutual information rate distinguishing fast-to-slow from slow-to-fast couplings. We show that this reconstruction can be achieved from the spectral matrix and illustrate its robustness to finite trajectory lengths, moderate timescale separation, and weak nonlinearities. Our work paves the way for exploiting the presence of multiple timescales in real-world systems as a source of structural identifiability rather than an obstacle to network inference.

</details>

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文构建基于图拉普拉斯与Dirac算子谱性质的、逐级增强的自同构不变签名层次，并引入"探测"（临时结构扰动）技术，用于求解顶点轨道划分问题，在包括低维WL难以细分的图上恢复精确轨道划分。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 利用图拉普拉斯算子与Dirac算子的谱性质构造一系列自同构不变的签名（signature），按表达力递增形成层次；在前缀反例序列的引导下逐步强化这些签名，最终引入探测（probing）技术——对图施加临时结构扰动以暴露原未扰动图中被隐藏的区分；并在多类基准图族上进行评估，同时分析计算与数值局限性。

Result: 在多种基准图族上恢复出精确的轨道划分，包括抵抗低维Weisfeiler–Leman细化的困难实例；同时界定了所构造方法的计算与数值局限，区分经验成功与理论完备性。

Abstract: The defining features of a physical system are its symmetries and its invariants. For a network, the symmetries are the automorphisms, and the invariants are the vertex orbits they define. Two vertices of a graph may appear structurally indistinguishable without being interchangeable under any graph automorphism. Distinguishing apparent similarity from genuine symmetry lies at the heart of the vertex-orbit partition problem. In this work, we develop a hierarchy of increasingly expressive, automorphism-invariant signatures that draw on the spectral properties of the graph Laplacian and the Dirac operator to reveal subtle structural differences. Guided by a sequence of counterexamples, we progressively strengthen these signatures, ultimately incorporating probing techniques, which are temporary structural perturbations, that expose the hidden distinctions in the original unperturbed graph. We evaluate our approach across diverse benchmark families, recovering exact orbit partitions in challenging instances, including graphs that resist low-dimensional Weisfeiler--Leman refinement. We also examine the computational and numerical limitations of our constructions, distinguishing empirical success from theoretical completeness. Our findings highlight the potential of spectral and probing-based methods for uncovering the structural distinctions that define vertex orbit.

</details>

*Sandro M. Reia,José F. Fontanari*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 并行模拟吸收态随机过程时，轨迹时长依赖于最终吸收结局会导致系统性采样偏差；文章用两吸收态更新模型给出精确解析，比较连续采样与"首轨迹过滤"（FTF），证明FTF的残余偏差随计算预算指数衰减，并给出使FTF更优的预算相关交叉工作者分配，最后在Axelrod模型近相变处验证该机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并行worker在轨迹吸收后立即重启，在固定计算预算下会优先记录快速终止的轨迹，使观测到的系综统计偏离真实分布。作者希望量化这一偏差的来源与规模，并检验"只保留每个worker首次尝试轨迹"能否抑制它，同时厘清提高算力是否真能提升统计精度。

Method: 构建含两个吸收态、时长服从状态依赖指数分布的最小更新过程模型；用更新理论推导固定预算下已完成轨迹数量与组成（各吸收结局占比）的精确表达式；将连续采样与First-Trajectory Filtering（每worker只保留初始轨迹）对比，分析有限截止时间带来的残余删失偏差，并解析计算均方误差以确定交叉工作者数；进一步讨论吸收概率与特征时长均未知、删失严重时统计推断的局限；最后在Axelrod模型非平衡转变附近做模拟验证。

Result: 偏差随执行时间代数衰减，但在固定预算下随并行worker数增加而持续存在（不消失）；FTF的残余删失偏差随预算指数衰减；MSE分析给出依赖预算的交叉worker分配，超过该阈值后FTF尽管收集轨迹更少却更准确；Axelrod模型模拟显示快速吸收的碎片化构型被优先采样，证实更新机制在空间相互作用系统中同样相关。

Conclusion: 提高计算吞吐量并不必然提升统计准确性；对吸收过程的并行模拟必须显式考虑轨迹时长的异质性，并在适当预算区间内采用首轨迹过滤等策略以控制采样偏差。

Abstract: Asynchronous parallel simulations of stochastic processes with absorbing states can exhibit systematic sampling bias when trajectory durations depend on their eventual absorbing outcomes. Workers that continuously restart upon absorption preferentially record rapidly terminating trajectories within a fixed computational budget, distorting the observed ensemble statistics. We investigate this effect using a minimal renewal model with two absorbing states and state-dependent exponential durations. Renewal theory yields exact expressions for the expected number and composition of completed trajectories, showing that the resulting bias decreases algebraically with execution time but persists as the number of parallel workers increases at fixed budget. We compare continuous sampling with First-Trajectory Filtering, which retains only the initial trajectory attempted by each worker. Although finite execution deadlines introduce residual censoring bias, this bias decreases exponentially with the computational budget. An analytical mean-squared error calculation reveals budget-dependent crossover worker allocations beyond which First-Trajectory Filtering becomes more accurate despite collecting fewer trajectories. We also examine the limitations of statistical inference under severe censoring when the absorbing-state probabilities and characteristic durations are unknown. Finally, simulations of the Axelrod model near its nonequilibrium transition demonstrate the preferential sampling of rapidly absorbing fragmented configurations, illustrating the relevance of the renewal mechanism in a spatially interacting system. Our results show that increasing computational throughput does not necessarily improve statistical accuracy and highlight the importance of accounting for trajectory-duration heterogeneity in parallel simulations of absorbing processes.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文用自洽与含时Hartree-Fock理论结合多带线性化Eliashberg分析，研究电子掺杂菱形堆叠多层石墨烯中金属Wigner晶体（MWC）的集体激发能否媒介超导配对。结果显示，在MWC接近不稳定性时，一个由带间电子-空穴激发构成的集体模式发生软化，并在实验相关温度下驱动有限动量的配对，形成"超导Wigner晶体"（超固体的电子类比）。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期在菱形堆叠多层石墨烯中观测到的金属Wigner晶体与破缺时间反演对称性的超导相邻近，暗示电子晶体的集体激发可能媒介剩余巡游载流子之间的配对，从而产生"超导Wigner晶体"。作者希望检验这一可能性。

Method: 采用含自旋与谷极化的电子掺杂菱形多层石墨烯的realistic模型（低对称性消除了常规Cooper不稳定性）；用自洽与含时Hartree-Fock理论描绘自掺杂比例的演化并刻画MWC的集体模式谱；随后进行多带线性化Eliashberg配对分析。

Result: 随密度增加，一个弱色散的未占据能带趋近化学势，触发MWC的不稳定性。在稳定MWC深处配对很弱；但在接近不稳定性时，出现一个由带间电子-空穴激发构成并发生软化的集体模式。该模式在实验相关温度下驱动配对。主导的配对不稳定性位于有限动量，并混合费米面态与萌芽带（incipient band）态，使得仅基于费米面或单带的配对分析不充分。

Conclusion: 本文确立了集体模式媒介的超导在MWC中是菱形石墨烯的一个可行图景，并推动在其实验超导相中寻找共存的电子电荷序。

Abstract: Recent experiments on rhombohedral multilayer graphene have reported metallic Wigner crystals (MWCs), in which spontaneously broken translational symmetry coexists with mobile charge carriers. Their proximity to time-reversal-symmetry-breaking superconductivity raises the intriguing possibility that collective excitations of the electron crystal mediate pairing among the residual itinerant carriers, giving rise to a superconducting Wigner crystal---an electronic analog of a supersolid. Here we investigate this possibility using a realistic model of electron-doped rhombohedral multilayer graphene with spin and valley polarization, whose low symmetry removes the usual Cooper instability. We use self-consistent and time-dependent Hartree-Fock theory to map the evolution of the self-doping fraction and characterize the collective-mode spectrum of the MWCs, followed by a multiband linearized Eliashberg analysis of pairing. Upon increasing density, we find an instability of the MWC, triggered by a weakly dispersing unoccupied band approaching the chemical potential. Pairing remains weak deep in the stable MWC. However, on approaching the instability, we identify a collective mode composed of interband electron-hole excitations that softens. We show that this mode drives pairing at experimentally relevant temperatures. The leading pairing instability is at finite momentum and combines Fermi surface states with states in the incipient band, making a Fermi surface or single-band pairing analysis inadequate. Our results establish collective-mode-mediated superconductivity in MWCs as a viable scenario for rhombohedral graphene and motivate experimental searches for coexisting electronic charge order in its superconducting phases.

</details>


### [52] [Energy-resolved current response and the crossover between two approximately linear-in-$T$ resistivity regimes in the infinite-$U$ Hubbard model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10124)

This discrepancy can be traced back to the non-Kramers nature of Ho3+ where the CEF lifts the quartet degeneracy, leaving a quasi-triplet ground state. Unlike quartets or higher multiplets, we find that the effective spin-1 triplet does not allow independent dipolar ordering without strong quadrupolar (nematic) interactions that act to break rotational symmetry, revealing an essential multipolar prerequisite for tilted magnetic ground states. These results establish a quartet CEF manifold as the minimal degeneracy required for dipolar-driven independent ordering. Mapping the surrounding CEF parameter space further reveals competing magnetic, non-magnetic singlet, and potential quadrupolar phases, driven by multifold ground state degeneracies. These results establish CEF tuning as a versatile route to unconventional quantum phases across the broader RT6X6 (T = transition metal; X = Sn, Ge) family.

Abstract: We report the growth of high-quality single crystals and the magnetic, thermodynamic, and dielectric properties of rose-phase Na$_2$CoP$_2$O$_7$, a low-symmetry magnet in which corner-sharing Co$^{2+}$ dimers form an approximately triangular network. Broad low-temperature maxima in the magnetic susceptibility and heat capacity suggest the development of short-range magnetic correlations below approximately 4 K. The large separation between the absolute Curie--Weiss temperatures and the characteristic correlation temperature indicates strong magnetic frustration arising from competing interactions within the coupled-dimer network. Applied magnetic fields suppress these anomalies and reduce the recovered magnetic entropy, suggesting further enhancement of frustration under field. A systematic magnetodielectric response closely follows the field dependence of the magnetization, indicating coupling between magnetic and dielectric degrees of freedom. The low lattice symmetry enables multiple inequivalent exchange pathways and anisotropic interactions while relaxing symmetry constraints on coupling among spin, lattice, and dielectric degrees of freedom. These results establish rose-phase Na$_2$CoP$_2$O$_7$ as a platform for exploring frustrated quantum magnetism and coupled functionalities in effective $S=1/2$ magnets.

Abstract: Quantum spin ice attracts considerable attention due to its remarkable low-energy properties, described by the effective gauge theory, which mimics the quantum electrodynamics (QED). The ``effective" QED in quantum spin ice is beyond the ``real" QED in our universe in many respects. Above all, the effective QED hosts two kinds of gapped particles, the electric monopole, in addition to the magnetic monopole. Among the two monopoles, the electric monopole is of purely quantum origin, and its character has not been well resolved. In this work, we focus on the Rokhsar Kivelson point of the quantum dimer model, as the low-energy description of the quantum XXZ model on a pyrochlore lattice, and address the excitation spectrum of the electric monopoles, by combining the quantum Monte Carlo simulation with the stochastic analytical continuation. Most strikingly, the electric monopole spectrum exhibits clear double-peak structure, corresponding to the bound states and scattering states of electric monopoles. The lower-energy peak is attributed to the exciton-like bound state of an electric monopole pair, a signal of positronium in quantum spin ice. We found that the flippable loop operator serves as a local physical observable to excite a pair of electric monopoles, implying that monopoles can be detected through lattice or optical response.
