Abstract: In unconventional superconductivity, the pairing mechanism remains one of the central unresolved problems in solid-state physics. Moreover, the normal state shows strong deviations from conventional Fermi-liquid behavior, most notably a scattering rate that is linear in energy and temperature. Here we study the momentum dependence of the energy and damping of collective excitations in optimally doped cuprates in the normal state. In particular, we analyze existing Electron Energy Loss Spectroscopy (EELS) data of Bi2Sr2CaCu2O8 [Phys. Rev. B 44, 7155 (1991)] and new analogous optical plasmon data of Bi2Sr2Ca2Cu3O10. Furthermore, we reanalyze similar Resonant Inelastic Xray Scattering (RIXS) acoustic plasmon data [Phys. Rev. R 6, 043184 (2024)] of La1.84Sr0.16CuO4 and La1.84Ce0.16CuO4. We contrast these two-particle excitation results with one-particle excitations measured by Angle-Resolved Photoemission Spectroscopy (ARPES). Most of the plasmon behavior can be described well by combined self-energies of a hole and an electron. The dispersion of optical plasmons at high energy is only weakly determined by correlation effects, indicating resilient quasiparticles. The damping of the optical plasmons at low energy, however, is strongly influenced by the reduced scattering rates described in the marginal Fermi liquid model for "strange-metal" behavior. In addition, the same model describes the linear-in-energy damping of acoustic plasmons. Contrary to some theoretical predictions, we see no overdamped optical plasmons below the classical Lindhard critical wave vector.

Abstract: We introduce a class of two-dimensional tensor network states obtained by categorifying matrix product states. This procedure leads us to consider bond spaces provided by a 2-vector space associated with the 2-cells of the underlying lattice. Tensor network representations are then defined as module categories over the free monoidal category on a monoidal signature encoding the lattice geometry. We prove a fundamental theorem stating that two representations generating the same states with suitable open boundary conditions are equivalent as module categories. Finally, we introduce a notion of 2-injectivity, a categorified analogue of the injectivity condition for matrix product states.

Abstract: We study the spin-1/2 Heisenberg antiferromagnet with an anisotropic $\Gamma$ term on a zigzag ladder, which can be exactly mapped to the spin-1/2 XYZ zigzag ladder by a spin rotation. This model has recently attracted considerable interest as an effective model for the quasi-one-dimensional magnetic semiconductor YbCuS$_2$. For the model, the ground-state phase diagram was previously proposed by Saito and Hotta [Phys. Rev. Lett. 132, 166701 (2024); Phys. Rev. B 110, 024409 (2024)]. We systematically reexamine the phase diagram through complementary numerical and analytical approaches, including exact diagonalization, the density-matrix renormalization group method, and low-energy effective theories. We thereby demonstrate that when the anisotropic $\Gamma$ term is present, the model exhibits the Néel, dimer, ferromagnetic, and double-Néel phases, all of which have a finite excitation gap. The gapless Tomonaga-Luttinger-liquid (TLL) phase appears only in the isotropic case where the $\Gamma$ term is absent. We also confirm that the transitions between the Néel and dimer phases and between the Néel and ferromagnetic phases are continuous transitions governed by Gaussian criticality with central charge $c=1$. The TLL parameter characterizing the critical states at the Gaussian transitions is estimated numerically.

The emergence of superconductivity, with $T_{c} \approx 0.31$K, is preceded by an ordered state (phase I), with an ordering temperature $T_{0} \approx 0.46$K. The precise nature of the order parameter in phase I is still unknown, although evidence of dipolar ordering was found. To investigate a possible symmetry breaking at $T_{\textrm{0}}$, we conducted longitudinal thermal-expansion measurements along the crystallographic [100], [010], [110], and [1$\overline{1}$0] directions on a single-crystalline sample cut into an octagonal shape. Our results demonstrate the breaking of the fourfold in-plane symmetry below $T_{\textrm{0}}$, evidencing a small tetragonal-to-orthorhombic distortion (orthorhombicity $\delta \sim 10^{-7}$). The breaking of the tetragonal symmetry at $T_{\textrm{0}}$ might imply that the superconducting order parameter that develops within phase I cannot be multi-component. More generally, the distortion places restrictions on the possible order parameters for phase I.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多重要量子码（balanced-product、bivariate-bicycle、genon码及五量子比特码）都具有"商码"结构，但缺乏统一的理论框架来理解其逻辑量子比特、逻辑算子与距离的来源。作者希望用凝聚态物理中扭曲紧化的思想统一并推广这些构造，从而系统性地产生新码、非阿贝尔拓扑序和逻辑门。

Method: 1. 建立商码与流形 $X\times F$ 上沿 $F$ 带对称性背景的扭曲紧化之间的对应（双向使用）。2. 对Pauli稳定子码的单项商，证明转移定理：由母码导出逻辑qudit、逻辑算子与距离界，并用Tate上同调群计数新增逻辑算子。3. 对高阶形式与cup-product对称性，将扭曲实现为纠缠对角电路；有限覆叠平凡化背景时得到"dressed quotient"，使阿贝尔Pauli码产生非阿贝尔拓扑序（如 $D(D_4)$）。4. 分析dressing的Clifford层级与三次扭曲下的码维数（cup乘积中二次方程解数）。5. 利用Barkeshli–Hsin–Kobayashi的自旋-拓扑响应公式识别逻辑作用。

Result: 转移定理给出逻辑算子与距离界，Tate上同调精确计数新逻辑算子；dressing的Clifford层级至多比扭曲次数少一；三次扭曲下码维数为cup乘积中二次方程的解数，当维数不是2的幂时证明该码非Pauli稳定子码；环面与slab上距离随尺寸线性增长。$S^3$上三个环面码本无逻辑qubit，但在$\mathbb{RP}^3$上同一电路实现逻辑$CCZ$。3D费米子环面码的四分之一转扭曲给出从$[[768,2,9]]$起的线性距离码族，其$p+ip$泵浦是精确保持码空间的显式有限时间准局域演化，逻辑作用为$T$（相差Clifford因子）。扭曲阿贝尔规范理论仅能达到$[G,G]$中心的量子双重$D(G)$。

Conclusion: 商码即扭曲紧化的格点形式，这一对应既提供构造量子码与计算距离的强有力工具，又揭示扭曲可产生非阿贝尔拓扑序、超出Pauli稳定子框架的码以及逻辑门。其副作用是把可实现的拓扑序限制在交换子群中心的量子双重类，并建立了逻辑门、任意子与对称性背景之间的新联系。

Abstract: Many quantum codes, including balanced-product, bivariate-bicycle and genon codes and the five-qubit code, are quotient codes: a larger code folded onto itself by identifying each check with its image under a deck transformation composed with a symmetry. We show that this is the lattice form of twisted compactification, in which a theory on $X\times F$ carries a symmetry background along $F$, and we use the correspondence in both directions. For monomial quotients of Pauli stabilizer codes, a transfer theorem gives the logical qudits, logical operators and distance bounds from those of the parent, with a Tate cohomology group counting the logical operators that are new. For higher-form and cup-product symmetries the twist is an entangling diagonal circuit; when a finite cover trivializes the background, the code is a dressed quotient, so abelian Pauli codes give non-abelian topological order such as $D(D_4)$. The dressings have Clifford level at most one less than the degree of the twist, and for cubic twists the code dimension is a count of solutions of quadratic equations in cup products, which certifies that the code is not a Pauli stabilizer code when it is not a power of two; on tori and slabs the distance is linear in the size. Twists also create logical gates: three toric codes on $S^3$ have no logical qubits, but on $\mathbb{RP}^3$ the same circuit is a logical $CCZ$. A quarter-turn twist of the 3D fermionic toric code gives codes of linear distance, from $[[768,2,9]]$ up, whose $p+ip$ pump is an explicit finite-time quasi-local evolution that preserves the code space exactly; using the spin-topological response formula of Barkeshli, Hsin and Kobayashi, we identify its logical action as $T$ up to Clifford factors. Finally, twisting abelian gauge theories reach only the quantum doubles $D(G)$ with $[G,G]$ central.

</details>

Abstract: Quantum communication networks are best supported by maximally entangled states, but imperfections in entanglement generation and distribution generally limit the distributed states to being only partially entangled. Therefore, protocols typically consisting of local operations and classical communication are required to create suitably entangled networks. Here, we introduce a power-of-choice (PoC) protocol based on reversing the selection criteria in the Achlioptas process previously considered in classical explosive percolation. The resulting PoC protocol anticipates the entanglement percolation transition, thereby reducing the entanglement resources needed to establish quantum communication. Notably, in contrast to explosive percolation, the PoC protocol admits an implementation using local information with no appreciable loss of performance.

</details>

Method: 在固定查询次数下，将二值相位分类的目标直接写成测量响应；GQSP把该响应限制为有界三角多项式，而贝叶斯分类误差对响应是线性的，由此得到关于全局贝叶斯最优响应的精确有限维半定规划；再通过Fejér–Riesz因子分解与GQSP合成得到物理可实现的相干控制序列；该框架可推广到样本化设计与加性相位不确定性。

Conclusion: 以测量响应为变量的凸优化框架可避免非凸控制参数训练的初始化依赖，获得全局贝叶斯最优的量子感知方案，并自然兼容采样估计与相位不确定性。

Abstract: Quantum computational sensing performs task-specific inference directly through coherent sensing dynamics, avoiding an intermediate estimation step. Existing designs, however, typically optimize quantum control parameters through nonconvex programs whose performance can depend strongly on initialization. This contribution formulates binary phase classification directly in terms of the sensor's measurement response. For a fixed query budget, generalized quantum signal processing (GQSP) restricts this response to a bounded trigonometric polynomial, while the Bayes classification error is linear in the response. These properties yield an exact finite-dimensional semidefinite program for the globally Bayes-optimal sensor response. Fejér--Riesz factorization and GQSP synthesis then recover a physically realizable control sequence. The same formulation extends naturally to sample-based design and additive phase uncertainty. Numerical tests show improved accuracy and optimization reliability over direct control-angle training; noise-aware redesign also lowers error under phase uncertainty.

</details>

Method: 1）理论上证明任意S∈Sp(2N,R)可分解为相邻模双模辛门的三角阵列＋单模辛门；2）据此设计辛Reck网格硬件实现该因式分解，推广光学神经网络中的酉Reck网格；3）训练时将该网格接在未知高斯酉操作之后，借助光学探针与测量，逐次置零局部响应块实现原位自配置；4）引入局部条件数量化单次置零所需压缩量及其对测量误差的敏感度；5）数值实验考察有限采样训练与门标定系统误差下的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 将自配置光学网络从酉变换扩展到辛变换，把物理学习引入连续变量量子光子硬件，为原位逆向未知高斯量子操作提供了可扩展的架构与算法。

Abstract: Programmable photonic circuits are a natural platform for physical learning, where hardware parameters are trained directly from measured feedback. We propose a physical learning algorithm and architecture for continuous-variable quantum systems where a photonic circuit is trained in situ to invert an unknown lossless Gaussian quantum operation. We prove that a generic symplectic matrix $S\in\mathrm{Sp}(2N,\mathbb{R})$ admits a decomposition into a triangular array of two-mode symplectic gates on neighboring modes, followed by single-mode symplectic gates. We then introduce the symplectic Reck mesh, which physically realizes this factorization, generalizing the unitary Reck mesh widely used in optical neural networks. When placed after an unknown Gaussian unitary, the mesh is trained in situ by nulling local response blocks one at a time using optical probes and measurements. We identify a local condition number that quantifies the squeezing required for a nulling step and its sensitivity to measurement error. We numerically demonstrate finite-shot training and robustness to systematic gate-calibration errors. Our work extends self-configuring optical networks from unitary to symplectic transformations, bringing physical learning to continuous-variable quantum photonic hardware.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 定义品质量Q为频率与衰减率之比，通过理论推导给出状态数为d的随机跳跃系统满足的振荡数目上界2(d-1)/(πQ)，并构造嵌套循环（nested cycles）结构验证该界的可达性。

Result: d态随机跳跃系统中比值超过Q的振荡数严格小于2(d-1)/(πQ)；嵌套循环结构给出的振荡数渐近逼近该上界，说明界是紧的。

Conclusion: 该计数界可由可观测振荡反推隐藏系统的最小规模，为随机跳跃系统的谱结构提供了定量约束。

Abstract: Weyl's law counts the vibration modes that fit into a cavity, and a quantum system with $d$ levels can oscillate at up to $d(d-1)/2$ frequencies without decay. For systems that jump at random between states, no such count was known: they oscillate only out of equilibrium, and the random timing of their jumps damps every oscillation. We find the missing count: if $Q$ is the ratio of frequency to decay rate, a system of $d$ states sustains fewer than $2(d-1)/(\pi Q)$ oscillations whose ratio exceeds $Q$. Nested cycles approach this bound, and measured oscillations reveal how large a hidden system must be.

</details>

Abstract: Almost all solutions to vision tasks $\unicode{x2014}$ offered by nature and technology alike $\unicode{x2014}$ rely on capturing an image using a lens or a similar optical instrument, followed by computational analysis by neurons of a brain or a digital computer. This approach is inherently suboptimal in photon-limited and noisy conditions, where critical information is irreversibly lost at the measurement stage. We address this gap by introducing AI-driven optimization of quantum measurements on the incoming photons, learning how to extract the maximum possible information for a given computer vision task from each incoming photon. The trained measurement consists of a diffractive optical neural network (DONN) followed by a detector array. We train the DONN with a quantum information-theoretic objective that maximizes the rate at which the discrimination error decays as photons accumulate. The resulting measurement achieves exponentially lower error than both direct imaging and a DONN optimized for single-photon discrimination. We validate the advantage in a noiseless setting as well as a photon-counting experiment with an electron-multiplying CCD with the noise corresponding to $10^4$ photons per single signal photon, where the measurement also learns to mitigate the detector noise.

</details>


### [72] [Regev's Reduction Reaches Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10283)
*Stephen P. Jordan,Quynh T. Nguyen,Leo Zhou*

TL;DR: 本文把 Regev 归约与量子信念传播解码 BPQM 结合，用于从自旋玻璃哈密顿量的 Gibbs 分布采样（等价于 D-正则 max-k-LINSAT 的平均情形）。作者证明该组合能达到的最低温度恰等于树网络上含噪信道的重构阈值温度，并用几百组参数的大规模数值实验证实该温度对应 1RSB 预言的自旋玻璃动力学转变点，从而说明 Regev+BPQM 达到了经典局部动力学高效采样的猜想极限。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从经典自旋玻璃 Hamilton 量的 Gibbs 分布采样（即 D-正则 max-k-LINSAT 平均情形）在低温下变难。经典局部采样器（Metropolis Monte Carlo、Glauber 动力学）被猜想在重构阈值温度以下无法高效平衡，但缺乏严格的量子-经典对比结论；作者希望明确 Regev 归约结合量子解码究竟能推进到多低的温度，并刻画其极限来源。

Method: 采用 Regev 归约（基于量子傅里叶变换，在格与码相关问题间做归约）把 Gibbs 采样问题转化为解码问题，再用量子解码算法 BPQM（Belief Propagation with Quantum Messages）求解。理论工具上引入 Renes 的经典-量子信道对偶性，将量子解码可行性等价地刻画为对偶问题上经典重构的不可行性，从而把可达最低温度与树网络信道重构阈值温度相等这一结论严格证明出来；同时进行覆盖数百组问题参数的大规模数值实验，检验该温度与 1RSB 腔方程预言的动力学玻璃转变温度的一致性。

Result: 主要理论结果是严格证明：使用 Regev+BPQM 所能达到的最低温度，恰好等于树网络上传输信号时含噪信道的重构阈值温度；该等式可借 Renes 的经典-量子信道对偶性理解——量子解码恰在经典对偶问题重构不可行时可行。该重构温度即 Mézard 与 Montanari 由 1RSB 腔方程识别出的动力学玻璃转变温度。大规模数值实验在数百组不同问题参数下与该预言高度吻合。

Conclusion: Regev+BPQM 严格达到一个温度，而据作者所知经典方法在该温度下没有相匹配的保证；换言之，该量子方案触及了经典局部动力学高效 Gibbs 采样的猜想极限（重构/1RSB 动力学转变温度），为量子与经典采样能力之间提供了严格的分离性证据。

Abstract: Regev's reduction uses a quantum Fourier transform to carry out reductions between various problems related to lattices and codes. We apply it to the problem of sampling from Gibbs distributions of certain classical spin glass Hamiltonians, which can equivalently be thought of as cost functions for average-case instances of an optimization problem called $D$-regular max-$k$-LINSAT. This problem becomes hard at low temperature. Here, we use a quantum decoding algorithm called Belief Propagation with Quantum Messages (BPQM) to solve the decoding problem arising from Regev's reduction. Our main contribution is a proof that the minimum temperature reachable using Regev+BPQM is exactly equal to the reconstruction threshold temperature for noisy channels transmitting signals on a tree network. This equality can be understood via Renes' duality between classical and quantum channels, which we use to show that quantum decoding is possible exactly when classical reconstruction on the dual problem is impossible. M{é}zard and Montanari identified this reconstruction temperature with a dynamical glass transition predicted by the one-step replica-symmetry breaking (1RSB) cavity equation. It is conjectured that local samplers such as Metropolis Monte Carlo and Glauber dynamics cannot equilibrate efficiently below this reconstruction temperature but may do so above it; our large numerical experiments show close agreement with this prediction across hundreds of different problem parameters. Therefore, our results rigorously show that Regev+BPQM reaches a temperature for which, to our knowledge, there is no matching classical guarantee, and brings Regev+BPQM to the conjectured limit of efficient Gibbs sampling by classical local dynamics.

</details>


### [73] [Practical Ellipse Fitting for Atom Interferometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10289)
*N. Fowler,D. B. Reinhardt,M Meister,C. A. Sackett*

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了差分原子干涉测量中用于提取差分相移的多种椭圆拟合方法，包括统计、代数、几何与贝叶斯方法，并提出一种新的"倒数"几何方法，同时公开了各方法的Python与Matlab代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 差分原子干涉测量中两个独立含噪干涉仪之间的差分相移提取依赖椭圆拟合，但不同方法在参数设置与性能上存在差异，缺乏针对原子干涉应用场景的系统比较。

Method: 以椭圆拟合为统一框架，对比分析统计类、代数类、几何类与贝叶斯类椭圆拟合方法，并结合原子干涉测量相关的参数与性能指标进行评估；同时提出一种新的"倒数"（reciprocal）几何方法。

Result: 给出了各方法在原子干涉相关条件下的性能对比，表明每种方法均有其优缺点，优劣取决于具体使用场景；所提倒数几何方法具有一定优势。

Conclusion: 不存在普适最优的椭圆拟合法，应根据所需应用场景选择；文中提供各方法的基础Python与Matlab代码作为补充材料，便于复现与实用化。

Abstract: In differential atom interferometry, it is necessary to extract the differential phase shift between two interferometers that are individually noisy. A variety of techniques can be used for this purpose, under the general label of ellipse fitting. We compare several methods for ellipse fitting, considering parameters and performance relevant to atom interferometry. Statistical, algebraic, geometric and Bayesian methods are discussed, and we describe a novel "reciprocal" geometric method that offers some benefits. Each method has advantages and disadvantages that depend on the use case required. Supplemental material includes basic Python and Matlab code for each method.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Superconducting circuits hosting long-lived bosonic modes are an excellent platform for emulating the dynamics of light and matter and exploiting them for quantum information processing. The space of accessible dynamics is greatly extended by introducing the frequency tunability of artificial atoms, whereby the interactions with the light field of a microwave cavity can be modified on demand. However, using external flux bias to control the frequency of transmons introduces significant losses to coupled cavities, reducing their single-photon lifetimes to a few tens of $\mu$s. These imperfections compromise the usefulness of the bosonic mode, which loses its ability to store and manipulate the quantum information of many-photon states. This thesis investigates the issue and demonstrates how to enhance the flux control of microwave cavities without compromising protection. Several hardware solutions were explored, including off-plane remote flux sources routed by a magnetic hose, and lithographically printed on-chip lines equipped with multiple stages of filtering. These developments equip the systems with broadband flux control and photon lifetimes of several hundred $\mu$s. They enable the real-time manipulation of the two central parameters of the Rabi model: detuning and coupling strength. This flexibility translates into operational primitives of interest, such as the calibration of nonlinearities to different interaction regimes, the activation of parametric resonances with adjustable coupling strengths, and the use of number-selective transmon-cavity transitions for qudit state preparation and control. These results lay the groundwork for exploring richer synthetic light-matter systems, controlling cavities with more complex nonlinear circuits, and engineering driven-dissipative systems with applications in quantum computation and sensing.

</details>


### [75] [Adaptive Protocol for Rényi Entropy Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10330)
*Mu Niu,Yi Hong Teoh,Roger G. Melko,Rajibul Islam*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种自适应协议，用基于梯度的迭代优化在随机测量过程中动态调整"基组设置数"与"投影测量次数"之比例，从而在受散粒噪声限制的量子多体实验中更高效地估计二阶Rényi熵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二阶Rényi熵是刻画量子多体实验中所制备态纠缠结构的关键量，可通过随机局域幺正操作后重复投影测量的协议估计。但在测量次数受限（shot-limited）的器件上，固定比例的采样方案难以兼顾精度与预算，如何合理分配不同幺正设置数与每个设置下的测量次数成为影响估计精度的核心问题。

Method: 将"设置数与每设置测量数之比"视为可优化变量，从很小的初始测量集出发，采用基于梯度的迭代优化策略自适应地调整该比例；在合成数据上针对若干典型量子态进行验证，并与暴力网格搜索得到的最优比例以及固定比例协议进行对比。

Result: 对多种典型态，自适应方法收敛到的比例与网格搜索的最优值相当；在达到固定目标精度时，所需测量预算随系统规模的增速慢于固定比例协议。

Conclusion: 该自适应协议通过有效利用有限测量预算，提升了有限尺寸、受散粒噪声限制的量子实验中Rényi熵估计的效率，为实验中的纠缠结构表征提供了实用方案。

Abstract: The second-order Rényi entropy is one of the most important quantities used for characterizing the entanglement structure of states prepared in quantum many-body experiments. With access to qubit projective measurement data, it can be estimated via a protocol in which repeated measurements are taken after each of a set of random local unitary operations is applied to the quantum state. To obtain accurate values for the second Rényi entropy on shot-limited devices, care must be taken when choosing the number of different unitary settings to apply, and the respective number of projective measurements to take. In this paper, we propose an adaptive protocol for estimating the Rényi entropy that iteratively optimizes the ratio of basis settings to measurements, using a gradient-based approach and starting from an initial small set of measurements. Using synthetic data, we demonstrate that for several prototypical states, our method converges to values for this ratio that are comparable to the optimal values obtained by a brute-force grid search, and it exhibits slower growth of the measurement budget required for a fixed target accuracy than fixed-ratio protocols. Our adaptive protocol therefore promotes efficient estimation of the Rényi entropy in finite-size, shot-limited quantum experiments by making effective use of the available measurement budget.

</details>


### [76] [Realizing Unstructured PDE Forecasting on Superconducting Quantum Processors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10351)
*Erik Connerty,Jonathan Eastridge,Taylor Patti,Gerasimos Angelatos,Ethan N. Evans*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出量子储层网络(QRN)，在IBM超导硬件上用最多52个量子比特实现高维非结构网格PDE预测，通过部分测量-重置机制使电路总运行时间达相干时间的~24倍，突破比特寿命限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维非结构PDE的量子模拟是重要挑战，但现有方法仅在少量比特、简化问题上验证；且变分量子算法存在梯度困境，硬件噪声与短相干时间限制预测视野。

Method: 采用无梯度的量子储层网络，提供高达2^52维的大核空间（超出精确经典模拟能力）；引入部分测量-重置机制，并配合精心的线路设计（转译优化、动态观测量重构）缓解转译、指数级采样、噪声与寿命问题。

Result: 在~34k单元、Re=1000的2D圆柱绕流及非线性Gray–Scott反应扩散问题上，于CUDA-Q仿真及26/52比特超导处理器上获得高保真预测；将QRN特征加入自编码器线性预测流程使误差降低21%-43%；并实现闭环自回归预测。

Conclusion: 首次证明深度超过比特相干时间的量子电路可在高度非线性PDE上实现准确的非结构网格预测，为现实非结构非线性PDE的量子硬件预测开辟路径。

Abstract: Simulating high dimensional unstructured Partial Differential Equations (PDEs) with quantum processors is a major challenge with significant potential benefit. Existing methods have been demonstrated at small qubit counts, on simplifications of realistic problems. We implement a quantum reservoir network (QRN) for forecasting high-dimensional unstructured PDEs using up to 52 qubits on IBM superconducting hardware. Our method is gradient-free and does not suffer from the challenges of variational quantum algorithms. Instead, the QRN provides a large kernel space, up to 2^52 dimensions and beyond the reach of exact classical simulation, that remains useful under current hardware noise levels. Furthermore, a partial measure-and-reset mechanism enables total circuit runtime for ~24 times the qubit coherence time, so qubit lifetimes do not limit the forecast horizon. Thus, our method enables forecasting realistic unstructured nonlinear PDEs on quantum hardware. Typical difficulties associated with predicting large scale PDEs, such as transpilation, exponential shot requirements, hardware noise, and short qubit lifetimes are mitigated through careful design considerations and a dynamic observable reconstruction method. We validate our approach using a ~34k cell unstructured 2D cylinder flow at Reynolds number 1000 and a challenging nonlinear Gray--Scott reaction--diffusion problem, demonstrating high fidelity prediction on CUDA-Q simulation and on 26 and 52-qubit superconducting quantum processors. Specifically, we show that adding QRN features to an autoencoder linear prediction pipeline reduces prediction error by 21%-43%. Finally, we demonstrate closed-loop autoregressive PDE forecasting on these problems. This work demonstrates, for the first time, quantum circuits with depth beyond qubit lifetimes achieving accurate unstructured mesh forecasting on highly nonlinear PDEs.

</details>


### [77] [A trace costs one quantifier: the exact complexity of perfect play in nonlocal games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10481)
*Seth Douglas,Nidhal Mghirbi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明：在正规空间策略模型下，存在完美策略的判定问题是 Σ⁰₂-完全的，严格难于有限维模型（Σ⁰₁-完全）与交换算子模型（Π⁰₁-完全）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限非局部博弈中"玩家能否必然获胜"的可判定性在不同量子策略模型下存在层级差异；作者希望精确刻画空间（正规）策略模型及纠缠熵/迹约束条件下的计算复杂度，厘清可数性、有限熵与迹所对应的量词代价。

Method: 将完美正规空间策略的存在性归约为算术层级问题，通过构造硬度实例（强制每个完美策略选取有限时钟周期或有限树深度），并分析交换模型中有限/半有限 von Neumann 宿主、固定类型 III_λ 子型、有限秩、Tr h^α<∞ 等约束下的语言；同时对两种形式进行 Lean 形式化验证。

Result: 完美正规空间策略存在性为 Σ⁰₂-完全，即使限定值为 1 的博弈亦然，故即使有停机问题神谕也不可判定；交换模型中有限或半有限宿主为 Σ⁰₂-完全，而固定 III_λ 子型与一般交换博弈同为 Π⁰₁-完全；要求有限纠缠熵时仍为 Σ⁰₂-完全；对每个可计算 0<α<1，Tr h^α<∞ 亦为 Σ⁰₂-完全，构成有限秩与有限熵之间的严格阶梯；另证正规纠缠下一次零误差通信的 Σ⁰₂-完全性，并构造出每个给定规模零误差码均需无限纠缠熵的有限信道。

Conclusion: 迹约束恰好等价于一个存在数词量词；空间完美博弈的可判定性在算术层级中严格高于有限维与交换模型，纠缠熵条件形成严格复杂度阶梯，且结果经 Lean 形式化验证。

Abstract: Given a finite nonlocal game, can the players win with certainty? We prove that the existence of a perfect normal spatial strategy is $\Sigma^0_2$-complete, even when restricted to games of value one, so it cannot be decided even with an oracle for the halting problem. This is strictly harder than perfect play in the finite-dimensional and commuting-operator models, which are $\Sigma^0_1$-complete and $\Pi^0_1$-complete. Within the commuting model, the existence of a perfect normal strategy with a finite or semifinite von Neumann host is $\Sigma^0_2$-complete, whereas for every fixed type $\mathrm{III}_\lambda$ subtype it has the same $\Pi^0_1$-complete language as unrestricted commuting play. A trace thus costs one existential number quantifier: the hardness constructions force every perfect strategy to choose a finite clock period or a finite tree depth. Spatial $\Sigma^0_2$-completeness also holds when finite entanglement entropy is required, and both forms are formally verified in Lean. For every computable $0<\alpha<1$, perfect spatial play with $\mathrm{Tr}\,h^\alpha<\infty$ for the reduced density $h$ is $\Sigma^0_2$-complete as well; these conditions form a strict ladder between finite rank and finite entropy. We also prove $\Sigma^0_2$-completeness for one-shot zero-error communication with normal entanglement, and construct a finite channel with zero-error codes of a given size each of which needs infinite entanglement entropy.

</details>


### [78] [On the estimation of linear-optical amplitudes and probabilities with entangled input state](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10500)
*Jean Senellart,Pierre-Emmanuel Emeriau*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文针对纠缠输入（矩阵乘积态，键维 χ）下的玻色采样器，给出输出振幅的无偏估计器：任意干涉仪、任意输出模式与纠缠下，N 次采样后均方误差 ≤‖ψ‖²/N；单次成本 O(nLχ²+nL³+mn)，预处理 O(nLχ³)，加性误差 ε 需 O(‖ψ‖²ε⁻²log(1/δ)) 次采样。该结果把 Gurvits 型经典可估性从乘积输入推广到具有多项式（受记忆限制的）纠缠的光子源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数玻色采样应用（如光子机器学习模型）并不直接使用样本本身，而是用有限 shots 以加性精度估计输出概率、边际或交叠。对单光子等乘积输入，这些量已可由 Gurvits 算法及其推广经典估计。作者追问：纠缠输入是否改变这一图景，即在什么纠缠条件下这些估计仍可经典高效完成。

Method: 考虑任意 n 光子输入，其为矩阵乘积态（每格点一个光子、每格点 L 个模式、键维 χ）。构造输出振幅的无偏估计器，按矩阵乘积态的收缩方式逐点计算，避免显式展开指数多的振幅；给出单次采样复杂度 O(nLχ²+nL³+mn)、预处理 O(nLχ³)，并以采样均值控制均方误差；同时将同一估计框架用于计算追踪掉记忆后的概率、交叠与单个边际概率。用切诺夫/赫夫丁界把均方误差转为高概率加性误差界。

Result: 证明了与干涉仪、输出图样和纠缠程度无关的均方误差上界 ‖ψ‖²/N，因而加性误差 ε 与失败概率 δ 只需 O(‖ψ‖²ε⁻²log(1/δ)) 次采样，内存量级为输入规模。主要应用是量子发射器顺序发射的光（如基于自旋的光子簇态源）：χ 由源保留的量子记忆维数界定——单个自旋在任何长度下 χ=2，k 个量子比特（存于发射器或延迟线中的光子）给出 χ≤2^k，故只要 k=O(log n)，成本保持多项式。估计器同时给出追踪记忆概率、交叠与边际概率的估计。

Conclusion: 纠缠输入并不必然破坏输出统计量的经典可估性：只要输入是低键维矩阵乘积态（等价于源记忆受限），振幅、边际与交叠均可多项式时间、加性精度经典估计。但所有保证都是加性的——既不能相对估计指数小的概率，也不构成输出分布的采样器。这为光子量子优势主张划出了边界：基于有限 shots 估计概率/边际的应用，需要超出该 MPS/记忆受限类的纠缠才可能体现量子优势。

Abstract: Most proposed applications of boson samplers, including photonic machine-learning models, do not use the samples themselves but estimates of output probabilities, marginals or overlaps, obtained to additive precision from a finite number of shots. For product inputs such as single photons, these quantities can already be estimated classically with Gurvits's algorithm and its generalisations. We ask whether entangled inputs change this picture. For any $n$-photon input that is a matrix-product state of bond dimension $\chi$, with one photon per site and $L$ modes per site, we give an unbiased estimator of any output amplitude of an $m$-mode interferometer, whose mean-square error after $N$ draws is at most $\|\psi\|^2/N$ whatever the interferometer, the output pattern and the entanglement of the state. Each draw costs $O(nL\chi^2+nL^3+mn)$ operations after $O(nL\chi^3)$ preprocessing, and an additive error $\varepsilon$ with probability at least $1-\delta$ requires $O(\|\psi\|^2\varepsilon^{-2}\log(1/\delta))$ draws, with memory of the order of the input size. The main application is to light emitted sequentially by quantum emitters, such as spin-based sources of photonic cluster states, for which $\chi$ is bounded by the dimension of the source's retained quantum memory: one spin gives $\chi=2$ at any length, and a memory of $k$ qubits, held in emitters or in photons stored in a delay line, gives $\chi\le2^k$, so the cost stays polynomial as long as $k=O(\log n)$. The same sampler also estimates traced-memory probabilities, overlaps and individual marginal probabilities. All guarantees are additive: the estimator gives neither relative estimates of exponentially small probabilities nor a sampler of the output distribution.

</details>


### [79] [Delta-Corrected Shot Reduction for Bypassing Optimization Plateaus in Variational Quantum Eigensolvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10524)
*Jamie Evander Ghozaly,Pekik Nurwantoro,Gagus Ketut Sunnardianto,Yanoar P. Sarwono,Leong-Chuan Kwek*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 针对VQE中方差保持型shots缩减（VPSR）因有限样本偏差导致的收敛不稳定与优化停滞问题，提出delta校正shots缩减（DCSR），利用多元delta方法推导经验缩减因子的高阶偏差修正项，显著加速收敛且不增加测量总成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VQE是量子模拟的核心混合量子-经典算法，但测量开销巨大制约其可扩展性。VPSR虽能降低开销，却因有限pilot shots带来的有限样本偏差，出现收敛不稳定与优化停滞。

Method: 提出DCSR（delta校正shots缩减）解析增强型shots分配策略：采用多元delta方法，对经验缩减因子推导显式的高阶偏差修正项，将偏度、峰度等高阶统计矩纳入分配估计。

Result: 理论与数值结果表明，引入高阶统计矩可提供必要的梯度分辨率，从而绕过噪声诱导的贫瘠高原（barren plateau）；在多种噪声与资源条件下，DCSR相较标准VPSR显著加速收敛，同时累计测量成本持平或更低。

Conclusion: DCSR为近中期硬件上的方差稳定化shots分配建立了数学上严格的框架，有效缓解有限样本不稳定性，提升VQE优化的收敛效率与稳定性。

Abstract: The variational quantum eigensolver (VQE) remains a cornerstone hybrid quantum-classical algorithm for quantum simulation, yet its scalability is fundamentally constrained by heavy measurement overheads. While strategies such as variance-preserved shot reduction (VPSR) mitigates this cost, they suffer from convergence instability and optimization stagnation caused by finite-sample bias in limited pilot shots. To overcome this limitation, we introduce delta-corrected shot reduction (DCSR), an analytically enhanced shot allocation strategy that rigorously mitigates finite-sample instability. Utilizing the multivariate delta method, we derive explicit higher-order bias correction terms for the empirical reduction factor. Both theoretical analysis and numerical simulations reveal that incorporating higher-order statistical moments, such as skewness and kurtosis, provides the gradient resolution necessary to bypass noise-induced barren plateaus. DSCR significantly accelerates convergence compared to standard VPSR across diverse noise and resource regimes while matching or decreasing cumulative measurement costs, establishing a mathematically rigorous framework for variance-stabilized shot allocation on near-term hardware.

</details>


### [80] [QAOA Inside ADMM: Subproblem Wins Do Not Become Decomposition Wins at Matched Budgets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10527)
*Mohammad Arif Hossain,Sanim Yousuf Fahim*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文在与经典求解器等预算匹配的条件下，将QAOA作为两区块ADMM中混合二值问题的二值子问题求解器进行基准测试；结果显示QAOA在最终读数下劣于随机搜索与多种经典启发式，仅在按最佳样本计分时占优，且没有任何问题族上使ADMM取得优于最佳经典求解器的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献常单独报告QAOA的近似比或最优采样概率，但缺少在真实优化流程（如ADMM分解框架）内、与经典求解器在相同评估预算下的公平比较；本文旨在填补这一空白，并检验QAOA作为子问题求解器能否改善整体ADMM求解效果。

Method: 以两区块ADMM求解混合二值问题，二值子问题分别用QAOA与经典方法（随机搜索、局部启发式、SDP+局部搜索、带重启的禁忌搜索）求解，每个子问题固定23次优化器评估；采用两种计分约定（最终读数 vs 最佳样本），在无噪声12量子比特模拟中测试角度迁移的深度2 QAOA，并覆盖可退化为无约束二值二次问题的基准类及两个不可退化问题族；同时设置正确性门槛并记录假设检验日志。

Result: 最终读数下，n=12与16时QAOA劣于随机搜索（n=20不确定，属探索性/事后检验）；按最佳样本计分则胜出。12量子比特无噪声模拟中，深度2 QAOA在97.3%的植入族子问题上找到精确最优，四种最强局部启发式为50.6%–67.4%（按最佳四个比特串计分为100%对90.4%–94.4%），与SDP+LS的96.5%无显著差异；按评估次数计，其求解时间比局部启发式低3–5倍但增长指数更大，为SDP+LS的10倍，秒级为带重启禁忌搜索的54倍。在单位罚参数下ADMM会重新求解该子问题，较大罚参数下精确解逐渐停滞；两个不可退化问题族上，较低精度求解器反而更常找到问题最优。

Conclusion: 在匹配预算的严格基准下，QAOA作为ADMM二值子问题求解器未展现出对经典方法的优势：计分方式显著改变结论，且没有哪个问题族使QAOA带来优于最佳经典求解器与罚参数组合的ADMM结果。研究强调需明确评估计数与计分约定，并提示该基准类的ADMM二值步本质上是约简问题上的近端步。

Abstract: We benchmark the quantum approximate optimization algorithm (QAOA) as the binary subproblem solver in a two-block alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM) for mixed-binary problems against classical solvers under matched budgets. With 23 optimizer evaluations per subproblem, scoring determines the comparison: on its final readout, the optimized QAOA loses to random search at $n=12$ and 16 binary variables (inconclusive at 20; exploratory and post hoc tests). When credited with its best sample, it wins. In a noiseless 12-qubit simulation, transferred-angle depth-2 QAOA finds the exact optimum of 97.3\% of planted-family subproblems, against 50.6\%--67.4\% for the four strongest local heuristics (100\% against 90.4\%--94.4\% crediting the four best bitstrings) and a not significantly different 96.5\% for semidefinite rounding with local search (SDP+LS). Under our evaluation-counting convention, its time to solution there is a factor of three to five below the local heuristics' but grows with a larger exponent; it is ten times that of SDP+LS and, in seconds, 54 times that of tabu search with restarts. For the benchmark class, an unconstrained binary quadratic problem in disguise, ADMM's binary step is a proximal step on the reduced problem: at unit penalty ADMM re-solves it, and for larger penalties exact solutions increasingly stall. On two families that do not reduce to one, less accurate solvers found the problem optimum more often at unit and larger penalties. No family gave QAOA a better ADMM outcome than the best classical solver and penalty. The benchmark includes a correctness gate and a log of its hypothesis tests.

</details>


### [81] [Beyond the Mean Gap: What Transferred-Angle QAOA Retains on a Superconducting Processor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10529)
*Sanim Yousuf Fahim,Mohammad Arif Hossain*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文指出QAOA硬件验证中仅比较平均代价会掩盖优化关键信息；在全局去极化噪声下，均值归一化差距与任意比特串集合（含最优解）的底噪校正概率应保持相同比例的"相对均匀采样的无噪改进"。在ibm_marrakesh上对ADMM子问题的深度2 QAOA实验显示：12量子比特时均值差距保留29%–46%的改进，但最优解概率仅保留5%–15%，且设备匹配噪声模型高估最优解保留度5–8倍。作者建议验证必须报告最优解与近优集合的保留度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有QAOA硬件验证习惯用设备平均代价与模拟对比，但该指标可能遗漏对优化真正重要的信息（如命中最优解的概率）。作者希望检验"平均代价改进的保留比例"是否能代表"最优解概率的保留比例"，从而评估常用验证口径的充分性。

Method: 理论分析：在全局去极化信道下推导均值归一化差距与各比特串集合（含最优解）的底噪校正概率均保持相同比例的无噪改进。实验：在IBM Heron处理器ibm_marrakesh上，对交替方向分解（ADMM）二值子问题运行深度2、角度迁移的QAOA线路，配置动态解耦（DD）与twirling开关；12量子比特规模，并辅以设备匹配噪声模型仿真与"崎岖植入"问题族测试（768-shot分块统计）。

Result: 12量子比特主族线路保留29%–46%的均值差距改进，但最优解概率仅保留5%–15%，每次运行至少10个实例中有9个如此；近优比特串的损失程度与最优解相近，而对劣质比特串的规避保留最好。首次投稿中使用设备匹配噪声模型虽再现了该模式，却将最优解保留度高估5–8倍。崎岖植入问题族上，设备在62%的768-shot分块中找到最优解（无噪为98%），处于局部启发式方法范围内。

Conclusion: 平均代价指标会系统性高估QAOA相对优化能力，故优化线路的硬件验证应同时报告最优解与近优集合的概率保留度，而非仅报告均值代价。

Abstract: Hardware executions of the quantum approximate optimization algorithm (QAOA) are often validated by comparing the device's mean cost with a simulation, a check that can miss what matters for optimization. Under global depolarization, the mean normalized gap and the floor-corrected probability of every set of bitstrings, the optimum included, retain the same fraction of their noiseless improvement over uniform sampling. We test this prediction with depth-2, transferred-angle QAOA circuits for binary subproblems of an alternating-direction decomposition on the IBM Heron processor \texttt{ibm\_marrakesh}, with and without dynamical decoupling and twirling. At 12 qubits, the main-family circuits retained 29\% to 46\% of their mean-gap improvement but only 5\% to 15\% of their probability of the optimum, lower on at least nine of ten instances per run. Near-optimal bitstrings were lost about as much as the optimum, whereas the avoidance of poor bitstrings was retained best. In the first submission, a device-matched noise model reproduced the pattern but overestimated the retention of the optimum five- to eightfold. On a rugged planted family, the device found the optimum in 62\% of 768-shot blocks (noiseless: 98\%), within the range of local heuristics. Validations of optimization circuits should report retention of the optimum and of near-optimal sets.

</details>


### [82] [Compact Quantum Channel Attention for Static ASL Recognition: A Controlled Study with Parameter-Matched Baselines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10544)
*Ronak Bhalgami,Deep Kothadiya,Nikita Bhatt*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 8 量子比特通道注意力门替代 SE 块，参数减少 7.51 倍，跨签名者准确率 94.50%，与经典对照无显著差异——仅证参数高效，未证量子优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合量子-经典模型在图像任务上常报告较高准确率，但很少能确认性能提升是否真正来自量子部分；作者希望以受控对比实验检验量子模块的实际贡献。

Method: 提出 QCA-DenseNet：以 8 量子比特变分线路构成量子通道注意力（QCA）门，作用于 DenseNet121 特征；QCA 门含 17,464 个参数，比 SE 块少 7.51 倍。在 MU HandImages 的 1,815 张图像上采用五折留一签名者（leave-one-signer-out）协议，并在 Kaggle ASL Alphabet 数据小子集上做冻结主干消融；对照包括 SE 块、参数匹配的经典 MLP 及无注意力模型。

Result: 未见签名者上 QCA-DenseNet 平均准确率 94.50%，高于 SE（93.72%），与最优模型相差 0.28 个百分点；参数匹配经典 MLP 与无注意力模型表现相近，各差异均不显著。

Conclusion: 量子注意力门能以远少得多的参数达到与 SE 相当的精度且无可检测的精度损失，但未观察到量子优势；结果为混合量子-经典模型中的"量子增益"提供了谨慎的负面证据。

Abstract: Hybrid quantum-classical models often report good accuracy on image tasks, but it is rarely clear whether the quantum part is responsible. This paper proposes QCA-DenseNet, a static American Sign Language (ASL) recognition pipeline in which an eight-qubit variational circuit acts as a channel-attention gate on DenseNet121 features. The quantum channel attention (QCA) gate has 17,464 parameters, 7.51 times fewer than a squeeze-and-excitation (SE) block. The pipeline is tested on 1,815 MU HandImages images by holding out one of five signers at a time, and a frozen-backbone ablation uses small parts of the Kaggle ASL Alphabet data. On unseen signers, QCA-DenseNet reaches 94.50% mean accuracy, above SE (93.72%) and within 0.28 points of the best model. A matched classical MLP and a model without attention perform about the same, and no difference is significant. QCA gives a much smaller attention gate without a detectable loss in accuracy, but no quantum advantage.

</details>


### [83] [Initialization masks overcome decoding obstructions in compressed QAOA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10588)
*Emilio Semre,Steven Frankel*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在压缩量子优化中，解码非对易可观测量会引入结构性限制；仅改变初始化（签名乘积态掩码）即可移除该限制，而增加电路深度无法做到。对n=3r连通三次图的显式无限族，全加号QAOA在任何有限深度、任意实角序列下cut≤2r，而固定深度一的均匀签名掩码期望cut为13r/4（加性优势5n/12）。数值上，在1,699个随机三次MaxCut实例上所选掩码平均比0.9100，显著优于全加号基线0.7067，接近r²个独立优化随机掩码池的最优值0.9125。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 压缩量子优化把变分态经解码非对易可观测量转成经典赋值，作者关注这一解码步骤本身是否造成表达能力的结构性瓶颈，并检验"提高电路深度"与"改变初始化"谁能突破该瓶颈。

Method: 构造n=3r顶点的连通三次图的无限族，固定Pauli编码与确定性符号解码器，证明全加号QAOA在所有有限深度与任意实角下cut上界为2r；给出固定深度一角度下均匀签名乘积态掩码的期望cut=13r/4的加性优势，并在子族上证明存在最优掩码。另构造在弱加权耦合、有界代价-角度域与指定生产编码器排序下仍保持线性深度一分离的实例。随后进行无噪声仿真，在能量优化前先做经典掩码选择，并在随机三次MaxCut、加权MaxCut、稀疏Ising、Max-2-XOR与Max-2-SAT上评测，用有限池次序统计分离"选择效应"与"随机抽样效应"。

Result: 理论上，全加号QAOA的cut≤2r对任意深度与角度成立，而深度一均匀签名掩码期望为13r/4，形成5n/12的加性优势。实验中，1,699个随机三次MaxCut实例上单一所选掩码平均比0.9100，全加号X基线为0.7067，r²个独立优化随机掩码的最优为0.9125；在加权MaxCut、稀疏Ising、Max-2-XOR、Max-2-SAT的完整队列上，所选掩码在每个规模上都优于基线，但小规模时劣于掩码池，仅在较大MaxCut与Ising规模上显著领先。

Conclusion: 解码后的表达能力、优化得到的能量与经验性能是三件不同的事；初始化（掩码选择）可突破深度无法突破的结构性限制。论文不主张量子计算优势。

Abstract: In compressed quantum optimization, a variational state is converted into a classical assignment by decoding noncommuting observables. We show that this step creates structural restrictions that changing the initialization can remove, even when increasing circuit depth cannot. For an explicit infinite family of connected cubic graphs on $n=3r$ vertices, with a fixed Pauli encoding and deterministic sign decoder, every all-plus QAOA circuit has cut at most $2r$, at every finite depth and every real angle sequence. A uniform signed product-state mask at fixed depth-one angles has expected cut $13r/4$, an additive advantage $5n/12$; an infinite subfamily admits an optimal mask. A second construction retains a linear depth-one separation under weak weighted couplings, a bounded cost-angle domain and a specified production-encoder ordering. Noiseless simulations then test classical mask selection before energy optimization. On 1,699 random cubic MaxCut instances, one selected mask achieves mean ratio $0.9100$, versus $0.7067$ for the all-plus $X$ baseline and $0.9125$ for the best of $r^2$ separately optimized random masks. On complete cohorts of weighted MaxCut, sparse Ising, Max-2-XOR and Max-2-SAT, the selected mask also improves on the baseline at every size, but it trails the pool at small sizes and leads it significantly only at the larger MaxCut and Ising sizes. Finite-pool order statistics separate the effect of selection from that of drawing a random mask. The results distinguish decoded expressivity from optimized energy and empirical performance; they do not establish a quantum computational advantage.

</details>


### [84] [Simulating the quantum switch with quantum circuits is impossible](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10615)
*Caisheng Cheng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明：任何具有经典控制因果序的量子电路，无论调用输入信道多少次，都无法精确且确定性地模拟量子开关（以及对 r 个信道全置换相干控制的完整 r-switch），且该不可能性对量子比特信道即已成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子开关以不定因果序组合输入量子信道，是"因果序量子控制能力"的核心资源。一个自然问题是：普通量子电路（含经典控制因果序）能否模拟其作用、需要多少次信道调用？此前对"精确确定性模拟"这一问题尚无定论。

Method: 以不可能性证明（no-go 定理）为技术路线：对具有经典控制因果序的量子电路建立一般性论证，证明在任意有限次输入信道调用下，不存在对任意输入信道均精确且确定性等价于量子开关的电路；并将结论推广到完整 r-switch（相干控制 r 个信道的全部 r! 种置换），论证在最弱的量子比特信道情形下即已失效。

Result: 证明了该模拟的不可能性：对任意有限次调用，经典控制因果序的量子电路均不能精确、确定性地实现量子开关；结论对量子比特信道成立，并延拓至完整 r-switch。由此封闭了量子开关精确确定性电路模拟这一问题。

Conclusion: 不定因果序过程在该设定下一般不能被高效模拟：即便允许经典控制因果序的量子电路，也无法以有限次调用精确复现量子开关，表明不定因果序具有超越此类电路的信道调用复杂度优势。

Abstract: The quantum switch is a higher-order transformation that combines input quantum channels in an indefinite causal order. Whether quantum circuits can simulate its action, and how many calls to the input channels such a simulation requires, are natural questions about the power of quantum control of causal order. In this work, we prove that no quantum circuit with classically controlled causal order can exactly and deterministically simulate the quantum switch for arbitrary input channels using any finite number of calls. This impossibility holds already for qubit channels and extends to the complete $r$-switch, which coherently controls all permutations of $r$ input channels. These results settle the question of exact deterministic circuit simulation of the quantum switch. They demonstrate that processes with indefinite causal order cannot, in general, be efficiently simulated in this setting even by quantum circuits with classically controlled causal order.

</details>


### [85] [A Grover-Amplified Oracle for Maximum Clique on Graph, Minimum Hitting set and Dualizations on Hypergraphs Built on Quantum Phase Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10640)
*Francisco Piñero,Fernando L.\Pelayo,Hernan I. de la Cruz,Jose J. Paulet,Mauro Mezzini,Fernando Cuartero*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文提出图编码算子与超图编码算子，分别将图（的补图）与超图编码为可用于Grover放大的oracle，用于求解最大团与最小命中集，且仅需O(N)量子比特。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Grover搜索的量子算法在求解最大团和最小命中集时，表示图/超图所需的量子空间开销较大，尤其当超图边数随顶点数指数增长时更严重，因此需要更低空间复杂度的编码方案。

Method: 构造Graph Encoding Operator：作用于任意图G的补图$\bar G$，生成最大团问题的oracle，供Grover算法放大；构造Hypergraph Encoding Operator：作用于超图$\mathcal H$，生成最小命中集问题的oracle。给出两个算子的完整构造、正确性证明、复杂度分析，并在量子模拟器上实现；将结果与现有基于Grover搜索的量子算法进行对比。

Result: 两个算子均正确生成可放大oracle；超图oracle测量后可给出命中集数量的近似，从而解决以正不可冗余析取范式表示的正布尔函数的对偶化问题；空间复杂度上仅需O(N)量子比特（N为顶点数）即可表示图与超图，即使超图含指数级数量的超边，oracle仍只用O(N)量子比特，据作者所知优于此前所有算法。

Conclusion: 所提编码算子在空间复杂度上显著优于现有Grover类量子算法，为最大团、最小命中集及正布尔函数对偶化提供了O(N)量子比特的oracle构造，并通过模拟实现验证了可行性。

Abstract: In this paper, we use a quantum circuit called the Graph Encoding Operator which, applied to the complement $\bar G$ of any graph $G$, yields an oracle for the maximum clique problem suitable for amplification via Grover's algorithm. Analogously, we devise a Hypergraph Encoding Operator which, when applied to a hypergraph $\mathcal H$, yields an oracle for the minimum hitting set suitable for amplification via Grover's algorithm. Interestingly, the latter oracle provides, when measured, an approximation of the number of hitting sets of the hypergraph, thus solving the dualization problem for positive Boolean functions represented in a positive irredundant disjunctive normal form. We give the full construction of both operators, prove their correctness, analyze their complexity, and provide an implementation on a quantum simulator. We discuss the resulting complexity, contrast them with existing quantum computer algorithms based on Grover search and we will show that our algorithms outperform, to the best of our knowledge, all of the previous algorithms on space computational complexity by requiring only $O(N)$ number of qubits with respect to the number of vertices $N$, for representing both graphs and hypergraphs in the encoding operator and, consequently, results in an oracle for both graphs and hypergraphs that uses only $O(N)$ qubits even when the hypergraph contains an exponential (on the number of vertices) number of hyperedges.

</details>


### [86] [Demonstration of a partial knowledge quantum key distribution protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10670)
*Liran Shirizly,Leonid Vidro,Hagai S. Eisenberg*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出将SARG04推广到d维、q基的QKD协议Bridge[d,q]，并完成Bridge[2,3]实验演示，用单个普克尔斯盒实现三种无偏偏振基切换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SARG04类协议中双方仅掌握彼此原始密钥的部分信息，但足以提取确定的共享密钥；本文希望把这一思想推广到高维与多基情形，并解决三基QKD所需的高速偏振基切换问题。

Method: 定义Bridge[d,q]协议：在d维希尔伯特空间中使用q个基，Alice与Bob仅获得对彼此原始密钥的部分知识；实验上以单普克尔斯盒（Pockels cell）实现三个无偏偏振基的快速切换，构建Bridge[2,3]原理验证系统。

Result: 给出Bridge[2,3]协议的实验演示，验证了仅凭部分互信息仍可协商出确定的共享加密密钥；所用单普克尔斯盒偏振基切换方案对任意三基QKD协议及需快速操控偏振量子比特的应用均适用。

Conclusion: Bridge[d,q]为SARG04的高维多基推广提供了统一框架，其低维实例已获实验证实；所发展的基切换技术具有通用性，可支撑三基QKD与其它偏振量子比特操控场景。

Abstract: We demonstrate a novel QKD protocol, named \texttt{Bridge[$d,q$]}, which generalizes the \texttt{SARG04} protocol to $d$ dimensions and $q$ bases. In this protocol, Alice and Bob gain only partial knowledge about each other's raw key. Nevertheless, this partial information is sufficient to distill a definite mutual encryption key. We present an experimental demonstration of the \texttt{Bridge[$2,3$]} protocol. The three unbiased polarization bases were switched using a single Pockels cell. The applied polarization basis switching method is relevant to any 3-basis QKD protocol and to other applications that require fast manipulation of photon polarization qubits.

</details>


### [87] [From Machine-Discovered Graphs to Analytic NOON State Families](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10685)
*Ozra Mahmoudi,Fatemeh Ahmadi,Rahman Nouroozi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文针对机器搜索得到、经预告与输出光子数选择可产生NOON态的图，推导了任意光子数N的解析权重条件，并给出基于路径恒等（HOM干涉仪+光子对源）与Takagi分解（单模压缩器+无源干涉仪）的两种光学实现，证明纯零位移高斯方案至少需N−2个预告光子且两者均达到该界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器发现的图结构虽能在预告与输出数选择后产生NOON态，但其干涉机制与光学实现方式并不直观，缺乏对任意N的解析判据与可执行的光路方案。

Abstract: Machine-discovered graphs generate NOON states after heralding and output-number selection, but their interference mechanisms and optical implementations are not immediately apparent. </details>

Abstract: Fractional quantum Hall (FQH) states support fractionalized excitations and geometric phases, yet experimentally resolving the many-body coherence encoded in their wave functions remains challenging. We further prepare a one-flux-added quasihole and characterize its fractional charge response against finite-size benchmarks. Controlled quasihole motion yields an effective geometric-phase signature from the reconstructed dominant-eigenstate trajectory. These complementary measurements provide microscopic access to the coherent structure and quasihole response of a finite fermionic Laughlin state, demonstrating an experimental route to probing strongly correlated FQH wave functions on programmable quantum processors.

Abstract: Based on the vacuum tensor Green's functions of electrodynamics, the Lippmann-Schwinger-type equations were derived for the correlations of electromagnetic fields. These correlations were applied within the Maxwell stress tensor and the Lorentz force formula to determine Casimir dispersion forces for two arbitrary bodies. The analysis is performed by the excitation of bodies by electric and magnetic dipoles.

</details>


### [90] [Trainability-Simulability Tradeoff in Parameterized IQP-based Reinforcement Learning Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10793)
*André Sequeira*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文通过解码器的Fourier–Pauli展开刻画参数化IQP策略中"初始化非集中"与"经典可模拟性"的权衡，提出多数投票解码器在保持初始化非集中（但不可多项式枚举）的同时提升RL性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数化IQP电路生成的分布被猜想经典难采样，但作为RL策略时需用经典解码器将2^n个测量结果聚合为小动作空间；这种聚合虽缓解初始化集中，却使策略经典可模拟。作者希望定量刻画这一权衡，并据此设计更好的解码器。

Method: 以解码器的Fourier–Pauli展开为分析工具：在独立均匀初始化下，将策略方差精确表示为按"活跃生成元签名"分组的非恒定Pauli分量求和，每组贡献为聚合系数的平方乘以2^{-r}（r为该组活跃生成元的二元秩）。同时分析稀疏Pauli展开与连续动作分箱可实现经典加性估计，并提出将受保护低权重分量与指数级精确Fourier支撑相结合的多数投票解码器。

Result: 理论给出方差定律并指出：逆多项式的聚合系数配合对数秩可避免指数级策略集中。数值实验验证方差规律；在十量子比特CartPole中，多数投票解码器回报高于递归奇偶解码器及所测局部读出，且训练中高权重Pauli扇区对梯度幅度的相对贡献递增；21量子比特分块链式策略在回合效率上优于大量全连接经典神经网络。

Conclusion: 该工作为量子及量子启发式RL的解码器设计提供指导，明确区分了策略非集中性、学习性能与经典可模拟性三个维度，说明有界宽度、经典可模拟的结构仍可获得强学习表现。

Abstract: Parameterized instantaneous quantum polynomial-time (IQP) circuits generate distributions conjectured to be hard to sample classically. As reinforcement learning (RL) policies, their $2^n$ measurement outcomes are typically mapped to a smaller action space by a classical decoder. This aggregation can mitigate initialization concentration while making the policy classically simulable. We characterize this tradeoff through the decoder's Fourier--Pauli expansion. Under independent uniform initialization, we express policy variance as an exact sum over groups of nonconstant Pauli components with identical active-generator signatures. Each group contributes its squared aggregate coefficient times $2^{-r}$, where $r$ is the binary rank of its active generators. An inverse-polynomial aggregate coefficient with logarithmic rank prevents exponential policy concentration. Sparse Pauli expansions and contiguous action bins permit efficient classical additive estimation of action probabilities. We introduce a majority decoder combining protected low-weight components with exponentially large exact Fourier support, preserving initialization nonconcentration while excluding polynomial-size enumeration of the exact expansion. Numerical experiments corroborate the variance laws. In ten-qubit CartPole experiments, majority achieves higher returns than recursive parity and the tested local readouts, while higher-weight Pauli sectors contribute an increasing relative share of gradient magnitude during training. A complementary 21-qubit block-chain policy outperforms an extensive pool of fully connected classical neural networks in episode efficiency, even though its bounded-width structure permits efficient classical simulation. These results guide decoder design for quantum and quantum-inspired RL, distinguishing policy nonconcentration, learning performance, and classical simulability.

</details>


### [91] [Trained, Frozen, or Absent: Identifying What a Quantum Layer Contributes to Cross-Sectional Equity Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10801)
*Chi-Sheng Chen,Xinyu Zhang,En-Jui Kuo,Rong Fu,Qiuzhe Xie,Fan Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文通过严格可识别协议检验混合量子-经典模型的量子贡献，发现在2000只东京证券交易所股票的日度横截面排序任务中，训练电路与冻结电路在年化夏普上等价（差异界0.03，p=0.0055），且所有条件均无法与零区分，说明报告的收益无法归因于量子电路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合量子-经典模型被日益用于金融预测，但其报告的增益很少与周围经典组件的贡献相分离，导致量子部分的真实作用不可识别。作者旨在构建一个使量子贡献可识别的实验协议，以检验量子电路是否真正带来增益。

Method: 构建混合量子时序卷积网络，用于2000只东京证券交易所股票的日度横截面排序。协议设计包括：六种条件（电路训练、冻结、缺失、参数匹配的经典替代模型、仅电路训练/冻结）共用同一代码路径；四个walk-forward折、五个随机种子；自助法置信区间、Deflated Sharpe Ratio、回测过拟合概率与族系错误控制；五个经典基线（含梯度提升树）；以及锁定的留出集。采用两个单侧检验做等价性检验。

Result: 在500个样本外交易日中，训练对冻结的差异落在0.03年化夏普之内（p=0.0055，最小可检测效应0.02），即电路与冻结电路等价而非仅不可区分；仅以电路作为头部唯一输入时，两者在0.25内等价；相对无电路网络，设计仅能分辨0.50的效应。无任何条件可与零区分（最佳Deflated Sharpe Ratio为0.17）。留出集在盈利窗口复现该消融结果，2000只美股样本在所有条件均亏损的情形下同样复现。

Conclusion: 研究所见增益不能归因于量子电路的训练，量子部分未提供可识别的预测价值；呼吁金融量子机器学习研究报告必须采用可识别性协议与等价性检验，否则增益可能仅反映经典组件或过拟合。

Abstract: Hybrid quantumclassical models are increasingly proposed for nancial prediction, but reported gains are rarely separated from the classical components around them. We study a hybrid quantum temporal convolutional network for daily cross-sectional ranking of 2000 Tokyo Stock Exchange equities under a protocol built to make the quantum contribution identiable: six conditions (circuit trained, frozen or absent, a parameter-matched classical surrogate, and the circuit alone, trained or frozen) through one code path; four walk-forward folds with ve seeds; bootstrap intervals, Deated Sharpe Ratios, backtest-overtting probabilities and family-wise error control; ve classical baselines including a gradient-boosted tree; and a locked holdout. On 500 out-of-sample days two one-sided tests place the trained-versus-frozen dierence inside 0.03 annualised Sharpe (p = 0.0055, minimum detectable eect 0.02): the circuit is equivalent to a frozen one, not merely indistinguishable from it. With the circuit as the head's only input the two are equivalent inside 0.25; against the network with no circuit the design resolves only 0.50. No condition is distinguishable from zero (best De-ated Sharpe Ratio 0.17). The holdout reproduces the ablation on a protable window, and 2000 US equities reproduce it where every condition loses money.

</details>


### [92] [MEAT with source and detector correlations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10817)
*Devashish Tupkary,Amir Arqand*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文证明边际约束熵累积定理（MEAT）的若干变体，使量子密钥分发（QKD）在源端与探测器关联缺陷下可做有限码长安全性分析，并首次给出同时处理二者关联缺陷的统一方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MEAT类工具虽能处理有限码长与自适应权衡函数，但难以同时刻画源端关联（态依赖当前及前若干轮设置选择）与探测器关联（点击后记忆效应持续若干轮）这两类实际器件非理想性，缺乏联合安全分析框架。

Method: 证明MEAT的两个变体：源关联版允许第i轮所制备态依赖当前设置选择及有限个前序选择；探测器关联版要求记忆效应仅持续有限轮，且该期间所有探测器关闭。二者可组合，保留MEAT的即时公告与自适应权衡函数等能力，密钥率通过单轮优化求解，可用现有数值方法计算。

Result: 在qubit BB84协议上演示该方法，在存在源与探测器关联缺陷时仍获得有竞争力的密钥率，且密钥率可由单轮优化数值求得。

Conclusion: 作者给出首个能联合处理源端与探测器关联非理想性的QKD有限码长安全分析方法，扩展了MEAT适用范围，同时保持其灵活性与计算可行性。

Abstract: We prove variants of the marginal-constrained entropy accumulation theorem (MEAT) that enable finite-size security analysis of quantum key distribution with source and detector correlations. For source correlations, our results allow for the state prepared in a given round to depend on the current setting choice and a finite number of preceding choices. For detector correlations, our results require memory effects to persist for a finite number of rounds after a registered click, and all detectors to be switched off during those rounds. These results can be combined, providing, to the best of our knowledge, the first method to jointly treat correlated source and detector imperfections in quantum key distribution. Moreover, we retain all the benefits of the MEAT, such as (but not limited to) support for on-the-fly announcements and choice of adaptive tradeoff functions. The resulting key rates can be computed via a single-round optimization amenable to existing numerical methods. We demonstrate these results on qubit BB84, and exhibit competitive key rates even in the presence of correlated source and detector imperfections.

</details>


### [93] [Periodic Readout Induces a First-Order Generalization Transition in a Quantum Perceptron](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10887)
*Fabio Benatti,Masoud Gharahi,Giovanni Gramegna,Stefano Mancini,Vincenzo Parisi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究读出为方波函数的量子感知机，用复本对称理论揭示周期读出结构带来的记忆-泛化权衡：高频可增强随机标签存储，但会把均衡泛化误差推至随机猜测水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变分量子分类器因可观测量期望对电路参数的傅里叶依赖而具有高度结构化的决策边界，但表达力可能带来尖锐的统计代价。作者希望定量刻画纯由周期读出结构产生的记忆与泛化之间的权衡，并为傅里叶丰富的变分量子分类器提供基准。

Method: 考虑读出为加权输入的最简周期分类器——方波函数的量子感知机；在热力学极限下采用教师-学生复本对称理论，确定其典型泛化性能。

Result: 存在临界振荡频率：超过该频率后，增大训练负载会诱发从弱对齐到强教师对齐的一阶相变，泛化性能不连续跃升；而在固定负载下，提高频率最终使均衡泛化误差趋于随机水平，尽管训练样本被完美分类。

Conclusion: 同一周期结构在增强随机标签存储的同时会延迟学习的发生，周期读出本身即可产生记忆与泛化之间的定量权衡，为理解傅里叶丰富的变分量子分类器提供基准。

Abstract: Variational quantum classifiers can generate highly structured decision boundaries through the Fourier dependence of observable expectation values on circuit parameters. We show that expressivity can come at a sharp statistical cost, by considering a quantum perceptron whose readout is the simplest periodic classifier, namely a square-wave function of the weighted inputs, and determine its typical generalization in the thermodynamic limit using a teacher-student replica-symmetric theory. Above a critical oscillation frequency, increasing the training load induces a first-order transition from weakly to strongly teacher-aligned phases, resulting in a discontinuous improvement in generalization. At fixed load, however, increasing the frequency eventually drives the equilibrium generalization error to chance level, despite perfect classification of all the training examples. Thus, the same periodic structure that enhances random-label storage can delay the onset of learning. Our results expose a quantitative tradeoff between memorization and generalization generated purely by periodic readout, and provide a benchmark for understanding Fourier-rich variational quantum classifiers.

</details>


### [94] [One Measurement, Many Fronts in Kitaev and Cluster Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10912)
*Nicholas Musat,Stephan Haas,Rishabh Jha*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文精确求解了在Kitaev链、横场cluster链及幂律耦合Kitaev链中，对基态单点投影测量后各二次观测量响应的传播规律：远离测量点处，每个观测量都通过一个"前波面"响应，其位置与宽度仅由准粒子色散决定，而强度取决于"测量算符产生最快准粒子"与"被观测算符探测最快准粒子"两个因子之积；所有衰减律与剖面均以闭式给出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单点投影测量会同时引发两种效应：测量结果导致的基态关联瞬时重建（信息更新）与测量对被测点与其余部分关联的擦除，二者都以某种方式向外传播。已有工作多关注纠缠或单一观测量，缺乏对"一次测量如何同时驱动多个观测量、各观测量响应如何异同"的统一、可精确求解的描述，尤其缺乏对拓扑相、对称性选择与长程耦合情形的系统比较。

Method: 在一维自由费米子（可映射为二次型）模型中选取基态，对单点做局域投影测量，用关联矩阵/高斯态形式精确计算测量后各二次观测量的期望值；对Kitaev链、横场cluster链、幂律耦合Kitaev链分别用准粒子色散与Bogoliubov变换求解，并利用李–罗宾逊型锥形传播与色散驻相分析提取前波面位置、宽度与衰减律的闭式表达式。

Result: 发现远离测量点时各二次观测量都表现为前波面响应：位置与宽度只依赖准粒子色散；强度正比于两因子之积（测量算符对最快准粒子的产生强度、观测算符对其探测强度），任一因子为零则前波面更弱且衰减更快。同一测量可同时发射多个前波面，位置与宽度相同而衰减律不同。Kitaev链中密度与键能（最近邻哈密顿量两项）对一次密度测量的衰减律在拓扑相与平庸相间互换；cluster链中测量奇宇称自旋与偶宇称自旋会在固定哈密顿量下改变临界衰减律；幂律耦合下宽度律本身发生变化。所有衰减律与剖面均获闭式表达。

Conclusion: 局域测量引发的关联重构与擦除可被统一刻画为"一测量多前波面"的图像，其几何（位置、宽度）由色散普适决定，而振幅与衰减律由算符与最快准粒子模式的耦合细节决定，从而将拓扑相、对称性与耦合程长等信息编码在可解析观测的响应剖面中。

Abstract: A projective measurement of one site in the ground state of a quantum chain disturbs the chain in two ways: learning the outcome shifts distant expectation values at once, through ground-state correlations, and the measurement erases the correlations between the measured site and the rest. Both changes travel outward. We solve this problem exactly for the Kitaev chain, the cluster chain in a transverse field, and the Kitaev chain with power-law couplings, and we find that far from the measured site every quadratic observable responds through a front. Where the front is and how fast it broadens depend only on the quasiparticle dispersion. How strong it is depends on two operators: how strongly the measured one creates the fastest quasiparticles and how strongly the observed one detects them; if either factor vanishes, the front is weaker and decays faster. One measurement therefore launches many fronts, one per observable, with a common position and width but different decay laws. In the Kitaev chain the density and the bond energy, the two terms of its nearest-neighbor Hamiltonian, respond to one density measurement with decay laws that trade places between the topological and the trivial phase; in the cluster chain, measuring a symmetry-odd spin instead of a symmetry-even one changes the critical decay law at a fixed Hamiltonian; and with power-law couplings the width law itself changes. All decay laws and profiles are obtained in closed form.

</details>


### [95] [Bias-free superconducting microwave nonreciprocity from a passive nonlinear resonator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10915)
*Brendan MacKay,Abdul Mohamed,Andrea Alù,Shabir Barzanjeh*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作在无偏置的被动超导平台上实现微波非互易传输：利用单个无约瑟夫森结的氮化铌（NbN）非线性谐振器，通过两端口不对称耦合与尖锐Fano线型，将方向相关的非线性谐振频移转化为大传输对比度，在4.6 dB插入损耗下获得53.0 dB方向对比度，并在2 μs–1 ms脉冲路由中保持>30 dB对比度、开关时间约数微秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导芯片中的微波非互易路由通常依赖静态磁场、泵浦音或时间调制等外部偏置，增加了系统复杂度并可能与量子处理器中强泵浦/控制音的路由需求相冲突；因此需要一种无需外部偏置、可被动工作的非互易器件，且最好与超导放大器工艺兼容。

Method: 采用单层薄膜图形化的单个非线性超导谐振器（NbN），不含约瑟夫森结；设计两个馈电端口的不等耦合，使非线性谐振频移具有方向依赖性，并借助尖锐Fano线型将这种非对称响应放大为反向激励时的大传输对比度；辅以时间分辨测量表征脉冲路由性能。

Result: 器件在4.6 dB插入损耗下实现53.0 dB方向对比度，且对比度超过20 dB的带宽为9.7 MHz；时间分辨测量显示可对2 μs至1 ms脉冲实现>30 dB对比度路由，开关时间在数微秒量级。

Conclusion: 该无偏置、被动的非线性谐振器方案可在无需磁场、泵浦或时间调制的情况下实现强微波非互易性，且与超导放大器工艺同源，有望简化量子处理器中强泵浦与控制音在芯片上的路由。

Abstract: Routing microwave tones in a nonreciprocal fashion in a superconducting chip usually requires an external bias, commonly in the form of static magnetic fields, pump tones or temporal modulation. Here we demonstrate microwave nonreciprocity in a bias-free, passive platform, a single nonlinear superconducting resonator patterned in one film without Josephson junctions. Unequal coupling with the two feeding ports makes its nonlinear resonance shift direction-dependent, and its sharp Fano lineshape converts this asymmetric response into a large transmission contrast when excited from opposite directions. Our niobium nitride (NbN) device reaches a directional contrast of 53.0 dB at an insertion loss of 4.6 dB, and it exceeds 20 dB over 9.7 MHz. Time-resolved measurements show that the device routes pulses from 2 us to 1 ms with more than 30 dB of contrast and switches within a few microseconds. Made with the same technology as superconducting amplifiers, such bias-free nonreciprocal components could simplify the on-chip routing of strong pump and control tones in quantum processors

</details>


### [96] [Deterministically fabricated C-band single-photon sources based on quantum dot circular Bragg grating cavities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10988)
*Monica Pengerla,Yury Berdnikov,Alexander Kosarev,Dominik M. Feder,Avijit Barua,Aris Koulas-Simos,Paweł Holewa,Sven Rodt,Imad Limame,Elizaveta Semenova,Stephan Reitzenstein*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作将MOVPE生长的C波段InAs/InP量子点确定性集成到圆形布拉格光栅谐振腔中，获得寿命1.4 ns、线宽41 μeV的电信波段单光子源，非共振激发下后选择单光子纯度0.95、原始双光子干涉可见度35%（时间后选择后达70%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光纤量子网络需要可扩展的电信波长按需单光子源以获得高密钥率，而现有量子点光源在材料生长（MOVPE兼容性）、确定性器件集成与C波段发射三者的结合上仍不成熟。

Method: 采用金属有机气相外延（MOVPE）生长C波段InAs/InP量子点，并将其确定性集成至圆形布拉格光栅（circular Bragg grating）谐振腔；通过测量辐射寿命T1、光谱线宽、非共振激发下的单光子纯度（后选择）及双光子干涉可见度评价光源性能，并结合时间后选择分析。

Result: 辐射寿命T1 = (1400 ± 50) ps，线宽ΔE_FWHM = (41.0 ± 4.0) μeV；非共振激发下后选择单光子纯度(0.95 ± 0.02)，12 ns延迟下原始双光子干涉可见度V = (35 ± 2)%，时间后选择后提升至(70 ± 18)%。

Conclusion: 该结果将MOVPE生长、确定性器件集成与电信C波段发射三者结合，为可扩展的半导体量子光源及光纤量子网络提供了可行路径；作者提出通过辐射速率调控、电荷稳定化和更具选择性的激发方式进一步提升光子不可区分性。

Abstract: Telecom-wavelength quantum light sources are essential components for scalable fiber-based quantum networks, where on-demand emission of single photons is required for high secret key rates. Here, we demonstrate the deterministic integration of metal-organic vapor-phase epitaxy (MOVPE)-grown C-band InAs/InP quantum dots into circular Bragg grating resonators. The integrated emitters exhibit a radiative lifetime of $T_1 = (1400 \pm 50)~\mathrm{ps}$ and a spectral linewidth of $\Delta E_{\mathrm{FWHM}} = (41.0 \pm 4.0)~\mu\mathrm{eV}$. Under non-resonant excitation, we measure a post-selected single-photon purity of $(0.95 \pm 0.02)$ and a raw two-photon interference visibility of $V_{12\mathrm{ns}} = (35 \pm 2)\%$, which increases to $(70 \pm 18)\%$ under temporal post-selection. We discuss strategies to improve photon indistinguishability, including radiative-rate engineering, charge stabilization, and more selective excitation. Our results combine MOVPE growth, deterministic device integration, and telecom C-band emission, providing a route toward scalable semiconductor quantum light sources for fiber-based quantum networks.

</details>


### [97] [A Witness for Quantum Correlations Using Nonlinear Spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11021)
*Julia Liebert,Gregory D. Scholes*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出利用现有光谱技术可直接见证分子体系中的量子纠缠：核心是从容许量子态到有限维信号系数空间的"光谱映射"，据此构造有效可观测量及其联合期望值集合与可分态子集，从而判定纠缠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子体系作为量子技术平台的潜力依赖于对其量子关联的刻画，而全态层析代价高、实验难以实现；因此需要一种基于已有光谱手段、无需完整重构即可见证纠缠的判据框架。

Method: 引入光谱映射：将容许量子态映射为有限维信号系数空间中被测量的期望值，这些系数对应与态无关的有效可观测量。有效可观测量由分子响应模型与测量协议在固定系统二分下确定。通过该映射定义与所有容许态相容的联合期望值集合，并与可分态子集比较，从而给出纠缠见证判据。以转动不变量和光学信号谱结构、荧光动力学与光子符合作为示例说明该构造。

Result: 示例表明，转动不变量与光学信号谱结构足以构造有效可观测量及其期望值集合；荧光动力学与光子符合提供互补途径。所构造的期望值集合可实现容许态与可分态的区分，从而给出纠缠见证。

Conclusion: 该工作为利用成熟光谱技术进行纠缠认证提供了理论基础，使纠缠检验可与常规光谱测量兼容，无需完整态层析，为分子量子技术中的关联表征提供可操作框架。

Abstract: The ability to detect and quantify entanglement in molecular systems is essential for understanding their quantum correlations and to evaluating molecules as platforms for quantum technologies. Here we show how signals from existing spectroscopic techniques can provide sufficient information to witness entanglement. The main ingredient is a spectroscopic map from admissible quantum states to a finite-dimensional space of signal coefficients, which are expectation values of state-independent effective observables. The molecular response model and measurement protocol determine the effective observables for a fixed system bipartition, and the spectroscopic map defines sets of joint expectation values compatible with all admissible quantum states and their separable subset. We illustrate the construction of the effective observables and their expectation value sets using rotational invariants and the spectral structure of optical signals. Fluorescence dynamics and photon coincidences provide a complementary example. Our findings provide a theoretical basis for entanglement certification with established spectroscopic techniques.

</details>


### [98] [Exact sampling beyond Fock inputs and linear optics: photon-number superpositions, photonic matrix-product states, adaptive and non-linear circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11044)
*Jean Senellart,Stephen Wein,Pierre-Emmanuel Emeriau*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 将Clifford-Clifford逐模式精确采样框架推广到更一般的输入态与自适应/非线性电路：只要输出前缀与输入集上的非负权重满足一条"边际恒等式（集合链引理）"，随机顺序采样器即精确，且每次候选模式仅需一次权重求值并给出隐输入集的后验，由此构造四个精确采样器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Clifford与Clifford的算法仅针对单光子输入、以均匀随机顺序逐个模式精确采样玻色采样；作者希望把这一构造推广到其他输入态（真空-单光子叠加、光子矩阵乘积态）以及自适应与非线性电路，从而在保持精确性的同时控制求值代价。

Method: 提出集合链引理：当输出前缀与输入集上的非负权重满足一条边际恒等式时，随机顺序采样器是精确的，每个候选模式只需一次权重求值，并可给出隐输入集的后验分布。基于该引理实现四个采样器：(1) 真空-单光子叠加源——权重为偏移输入集上的矩形永久式；(2) 键维χ的光子矩阵乘积态——逐间隙Kraus收缩，每个权重化为少量振幅平方和；(3) 自适应线性光学——链从给定自适应计数后的后验出发，拒绝与计数矛盾的链；(4) 单模非线性相位——恒等式失效，改用线性分支拒绝采样，接受率1/||c||₁²。

Result: 四个采样器在小规模实例上均与强模拟结果一致；代价模型显示：最多三个自适应模式的自适应采样与单光子情形代价相差在10%以内，φ=π/2处单个非线性位点约为单光子代价的4倍。

Conclusion: 边际恒等式为精确随机顺序采样提供了统一判据，将Clifford-Clifford算法系统性地推广到非单光子源、矩阵乘积态、自适应线性光学与非线性元件，并在可控代价下保持与强模拟一致的精确性。

Abstract: Clifford and Clifford's algorithm samples single-photon boson sampling exactly, one output mode at a time, by adding the input photons in a uniformly random order. We extend this construction to other input states and to adaptive and non-linear circuits. It applies whenever non-negative weights on output prefixes and input sets satisfy one marginal identity, a set-chain lemma: the random-order sampler is then exact at one weight evaluation per candidate mode, and it gives the posterior over the hidden input set. We build four exact samplers on it. For sources in a superposition of vacuum and one photon, the weights are rectangular permanents on an offset input set, and the cost has the exponent of single photons under loss. For photonic matrix-product states of bond dimension $\chi$, a gap-by-gap Kraus contraction turns each weight into a short sum of squared amplitudes. For adaptive linear optics, the chain starts from the posterior given the adaptive counts and rejects chains that contradict them. For a single-mode non-linear phase the identity fails, and rejection from linear branches is exact with acceptance $1/\|c\|_1^2$. All four samplers agree with strong simulation on small instances, and cost models put adaptive sampling with up to three adaptive modes within $10\%$ of single photons and a non-linear site at $\varphi=\pi/2$ at about $4\times$.

</details>


### [99] [Bridging Gaps with Tensor Networks: From the Haldane Gap and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11049)
*Sirui Lu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文验证、消化并形式化了 OpenAI 关于周期自旋-1海森堡链一致能隙的计算机辅助证明：通过沿空间与虚时间方向读取配分函数张量网络，把有限环界传播到所有更长偶数环，将能隙下界从同一有限输入收紧19倍至 Δ_L>0.0729（L≥36 偶数），并识别反射正定性为证明背后的一般条件，进而在七个额外自旋链与梯子模型中认证了一致能隙。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型生成的证明往往难以理解、难以推广、也难以直接形式化；作者希望把机器生成的证明"消化"为透明的物理机制，从而在获得更锐利界的同时得到更广泛的定理和可复用的形式化验证。

Method: 沿空间方向和虚时间方向解读配分函数张量网络，追踪有限环界如何经过加倍长度和逆温传播到所有更长的偶数环；以张量网络图示结合 Lean 4 进行形式化验证，并提炼出反射正定性作为该证明的一般性条件。

Result: 在相同有限输入下，自旋-1的能隙下界被收紧约19倍，得到对所有偶数 L≥36 的 Δ_L>0.0729；此外在七个额外的自旋链与梯子模型中认证了一致能隙，并完成相应形式化。

Conclusion: 将机器生成的证明消化为透明的物理机制，可以同时获得更锐利的界、更广的定理与可复用的形式化验证；反射正定性被确认是该类一致能隙证明背后的普遍性条件。

Abstract: Proofs generated by large language models are often difficult to understand, generalize, or formalize directly. Here we verify, digest, and formalize OpenAI's computer-assisted proof of a uniform gap in the periodic spin-1 Heisenberg chain using tensor-network diagrams and Lean 4. Reading the partition-function network along space and imaginary time shows how finite-ring bounds propagate across doubled lengths and inverse temperatures to all longer even rings, sharpening the spin-1 bound from the same finite inputs by a factor of 19 to $\Delta_L > 0.0729$ for all even $L \geq 36$. We identify reflection positivity as the general condition behind the proof and certify uniform gaps in seven additional spin chains and ladders. More broadly, digesting machine-generated proofs into transparent physical mechanisms yields sharper bounds, broader theorems, and reusable formal verifications.

</details>


### [100] [Towards large-scale ab initio Molecular Dynamics Simulation with Efficient Sampling-based Quantum Computing Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11084)
*Ankit Khandelwal,Nirmal M R,Aaryav Mishra,Debasmita Bhoumik,Dhinakaran Vinayagamurthy,M Girish Chandra*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出SqDRIFT-AIMD方法，将随机采样Krylov量子对角化（SqDRIFT）与解析核梯度结合，在IBM Heron r3上实现40–52量子比特活性空间的从头算分子动力学，能量精度超越CCSD并准确复现Zundel阳离子的质子转移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AIMD每步都需高精度电子结构能量与核力，计算代价高；现有采样型量子对角化仅限约20量子比特活性空间（该尺寸可由精确对角化处理），限制了其实际价值，因此需要将量子中心AIMD推广到精确对角化不可及的更大活性空间。

Method: 将随机采样Krylov量子对角化（SqDRIFT）与解析核梯度（analytical nuclear gradients）耦合，构建SqDRIFT-AIMD流程：每步由量子线路采样得到更精确的基态能量，并以解析梯度给出核力驱动力学演化；在IBM Heron r3处理器上执行线路，应用于Zundel阳离子质子转移体系，含40量子比特双势阱势能面与52量子比特计算。

Result: 40量子比特活性空间下SqDRIFT-AIMD给出的基态能量优于传统CCSD驱动AIMD；SqDRIFT能量在40量子比特双势阱PES上全程低于CCSD，52量子比特下与CCSD相差仅数mE_h；40量子比特轨迹与SHCI驱动质子转移偏差在1.33 mÅ以内，总能量守恒优异，标准差仅0.245 mE_h。

Conclusion: SqDRIFT-AIMD将量子中心AIMD拓展至精确对角化无法处理的活性空间规模，兼具更高能量精度与良好动力学稳定性，展示了近期量子硬件上超越经典CCSD基线的实用潜力。

Abstract: Ab initio molecular dynamics (AIMD) requires the calculation of highly accurate electronic-structure energies and nuclear forces at every time step, which can quickly become computationally expensive. Sample-based quantum diagonalization offers a route to such calculations on quantum hardware, but its use in AIMD simulations has so far been limited to active spaces of about 20 qubits. In these spaces, exact diagonalization is tractable, and the quantum circuits are typically built from standard coupled-cluster amplitudes. Here, we introduce SqDRIFT-AIMD, which combines randomized sample-based Krylov quantum diagonalization (SqDRIFT) with analytical nuclear gradients. With SqDRIFT-AIMD, we obtain more accurate ground-state energies than the conventional coupled-cluster singles and doubles (CCSD) driven AIMD simulation for the $40$-qubit active space. We apply SqDRIFT-AIMD to study the dynamics of proton transfer in the Zundel cation, using circuits executed on IBM Heron r3 quantum processors. SqDRIFT energies fall below CCSD across the $40$-qubit double-well PES and stay within a few m$E_\text{h}$ of CCSD at 52 qubits. The $40$-qubit SqDRIFT-AIMD trajectory tracks the SHCI-driven proton transfer to within $1.33$ mÅ and exhibits excellent total energy conservation, with a standard deviation of only $0.245$ m$E_\text{h}$. These results extend quantum-centric AIMD to active spaces beyond the reach of exact diagonalization.

</details>


### [101] [Adaptive Quantum Metrology of Gravitational Waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11157)
*Wan-Ting He,Shuang-Quan Ma,Ke-Mi Xu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种反馈控制的量子度量方案，利用引力波与光的直接能量交换探测弱引力波；通过自适应SU(2)方向控制使多次相互作用间隔内的频移相干累加，并给出量子Fisher信息矩阵与标度律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统引力波探测依赖时空长度变化的干涉测量，信号极其微弱且需超长基线；本文转而关注引力波对光的直接能量（频率）交换这一效应，并希望通过量子度量与反馈控制提升弱信号积累效率。

Method: 基于对引力波频率与相位的实时估计实施自适应SU(2)方向控制，使连续相互作用间隔内获得的频移同相叠加而非相互抵消，并将持续的频率差转换为光学相位差做干涉读出；对椭圆偏振引力波，用相距45°的两条横向传感通道分别探测+与×分量，随后把两路信号映射到正交偏振模式以在同一路径上累积相位；最后推导相应的量子Fisher信息矩阵。

Result: 在理想相干情形下得到灵敏度随相互作用时间与相位累积时间呈现 T_int^2 T_ph^2 的标度；对固定光子数探针，理想情形下灵敏度呈现类海森堡的 1/n 标度。

Conclusion: 反馈控制的SU(2)方向调控可有效将引力波引起的频移转化为可累积、可读出的相位信号，并支持对引力波两个偏振分量的独立探测；所给出的量子Fisher信息与标度律表明该方案在理想条件下具有海森堡级灵敏度优势。

Abstract: We develop a feedback-controlled quantum metrology scheme to probe weak gravitational waves (GWs) through their direct energy exchange with light. Adaptive SU(2) direction control, based on current estimates of the GW frequency and phase, allows the frequency shifts acquired in successive interaction intervals to add rather than cancel. The resulting persistent frequency difference is converted into an optical phase difference for interferometric readout. We further extend the scheme to elliptically polarized GWs using two transverse sensing channels separated by $45^\circ$ to probe the $+$ and $\times$ components independently. After the GW interaction, the two signals are mapped onto orthogonal polarization modes, enabling phase accumulation along a common path. We derive the corresponding quantum Fisher information matrix and find a $T_{\rm int}^2T_{\rm ph}^2$ scaling in the ideal coherent regime. For a fixed-photon-number probe, the sensitivity shows Heisenberg-like $1/n$ scaling in the ideal case.

</details>


### [102] [Quantum entanglement as a resource for self-organization and stabilization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11175)
*Kazuki Ikeda*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文在驱动-耗散玻色子晶格中证明：局域量子纠缠可驱动输运，将空间涨落放大为宏观图灵斑图；对含噪声与无序的全非线性动力学，为所有可分离（含任意经典关联）演化给出斑图强度的严格有限时间上界，而量子动力学会在形成与稳定阶段超越该上界；随后通过环境反馈抑制局域相干、激活经典输运，使斑图在纠缠消失后长期存续，且两阶段由同一时间无关的Lindblad方程描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心问题是：量子纠缠能否产生在纠缠本身消失之后仍然存续的宏观序。既有研究多关注纠缠对输运的瞬时增强，缺乏对"可分离极限"的可判定、可检验的严格界，也缺乏描述"纠缠驱动→经典维持"这一转换的统一动力学框架。

Method: 构建驱动-耗散玻色子晶格模型；针对含噪声与无序的全非线性动力学，对所有可分离演化（允许任意经典关联）推导斑图强度的严格有限时间上界；以超出该上界的斑图强度作为量子优势判据，并据此给出超出可分离极限的累积输运贡献下界；再引入对环境反馈（抑制局域相干、激活经典输运）的机制，用单一时间无关Lindblad方程统一描述模式形成与稳定两个阶段。

Result: 量子动力学在噪声下于斑图形成期与稳定期均产生超过可分离上界的斑图强度；超出的强度量化了超越可分离极限的累积输运贡献下界；在包含噪声与无序的非线性动力学中结论依然成立；引入响应性环境后，局域纠缠虽消失，斑图仍持续存在，且两阶段可由同一Lindblad方程给出。

Conclusion: 瞬态量子资源（纠缠增强的输运）能够建立持久的宏观序，且"量子建立—经典维持"的转变可在单一时间无关开放量子动力学框架内自洽实现，为非平衡系统中利用自组织提供了新途径与可判定的量子优势基准。

Abstract: Can quantum entanglement generate macroscopic order that persists after the entanglement itself disappears? We establish such a mechanism in a driven-dissipative bosonic lattice, where local entanglement enables transport that amplifies spatial fluctuations into extended Turing patterns. For the full nonlinear dynamics with noise and disorder, we derive rigorous finite-time bounds on pattern intensity for all admissible separable evolutions, allowing arbitrary classical correlations. Quantum dynamics produces pattern intensities beyond these bounds during both formation and stabilization under noise. The excess intensity also provides a lower bound on the accumulated transport contribution beyond the separable limit. We further introduce an environment that responds to emerging order by suppressing local coherence and activating classical transport. The patterns then persist as local entanglement is lost, with both stages arising from a single time-independent Lindblad equation. These results show how transient quantum resources can establish persistent macroscopic order, opening new directions for harnessing self-organization in nonequilibrium systems.

</details>


### [103] [Hardware-Efficient Continuous-Variable Quantum Computation: Finite-$N$ Non-Gaussian Gates in a Floquet-Engineered Cavity-BEC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11245)
*Soi-Chan Lei*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 以单腔87Rb BEC作为宏观玻色逻辑模式，提出无需后选择的确定性Floquet工程TAT非高斯门，并配合MPO与Dicke投影张量网络算法，在N=50下确定性产生深度Wigner负性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续变量量子计算需要硬件高效且确定性、可产生Wigner负性的宏观非高斯门，而现有方案多依赖后选择或受Fock截断误差限制。

Method: 物理上基于正交光学双驱动架构，用正规序Holstein-Primakoff展开与非微扰自旋模拟，论证过压缩区（t>t_opt）中O(1/S)的立方剪切与四阶Kerr项可确定性产生负性；方法上构造SU(2)自旋基下精确自动机MPO（D_L=6，与N无关），并用带log-gamma归一化的精确双侧动态规划Dicke投影子将4^N维Lindblad演化约化为(N+1)×(N+1)对称投影。

Result: N=50基准中，t_opt≈0.64μs处ξ_R^2≈0.124，t=2.00μs处W_min≈-0.214，实现深度Wigner负性，且可追踪对称性保持率p_sym(t)，无矩截断误差。

Conclusion: 该工作为N~10^3–10^5的宏观BEC量子处理器提供了可扩展架构，兼顾确定性非高斯门与高效精确的开放系统模拟。

Abstract: Continuous-variable (CV) quantum computation in bosonic modes offers a hardware-efficient route toward fault tolerance, yet synthesizing deterministic, macroscopic non-Gaussian gates with Wigner negativity remains challenging. Here, we establish two physical breakthroughs and two tensor-network innovations using a single-cavity 87Rb Bose-Einstein condensate (BEC) as a macroscopic bosonic logical mode. Physically, building on our orthogonal optical dual-driving architecture, we propose deterministic, unitary Floquet-engineered Two-Axis Twisting (TAT) non-Gaussian gates without post-selection, and introduce a paradigm shift from quantum metrology to CV quantum computation: via a normal-ordered Holstein-Primakoff expansion and non-perturbative spin simulations, we show that the over-squeezed regime ($t>t_{opt}$, where Wineland squeezing $\xi_R^2$ degrades) harnesses finite-$N$ Bloch-sphere curvature - manifesting at $O(1/S)$ as cubic shearing and quartic Kerr $(a^\dagger a)^2$ interactions - to deterministically generate Wigner negativity. Methodologically, we formulate an exact automaton Matrix Product Operator in the compact SU(2) spin basis with an $N$-independent Liouvillian bond dimension ($D_L=6$), bypassing Fock cutoff errors ($N_{max}$) and $O(N^3)$ permutational-invariant scaling.
