Finally, we decouple $4^N$-dimensional Lindblad evolution from collective observation via an Exact Bilateral Dynamic Programming Dicke Projector with log-gamma normalization, extracting the $(N+1)\times(N+1)$ symmetric projection $\tilde{\rho}_{n_L,n_R}$ without moment truncation while tracking symmetry retention $p_{sym}(t)$. Our proof-of-principle $N=50$ benchmark resolves deep Wigner negativity ($W_{min} \approx -0.214$ at $t=2.00\mu$s, after $\xi_R^2 \approx 0.124$ at $t_{opt} \approx 0.64\mu$s), establishing a scalable architecture for $N \sim 10^3-10^5$ macroscopic BEC processors.

</details>


### [104] [Unbounded advantage of adaptivity in entanglement-assisted communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11333)
*Armin Tavakoli*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究量子通信中"自适应测量"相较于"非自适应协议"的根本优势：构造出仅需3条纠缠辅助消息即可由自适应协议完成、而非自适应协议通信代价无界的任务，证明纠缠辅助三元经典信道的全部关联无法用有界经典通信复现；同时给出两条互补限制（有限纠缠维度可用有限通信非自适应模拟、d条消息可被O(d log(1/ε))条非自适应消息近似模拟）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共享纠缠可减少分布式计算所需的经典通信量，其实现方式有两种：先测量纠缠再做经典通信，或将量子测量自适应地依赖于收到的消息。作者希望严格刻画"自适应"这一自由度在量子通信中究竟带来多大优势，以及该优势是否可被有界通信量的非自适应方案复现。

Method: 构造一类通信任务：自适应协议只需3条纠缠辅助消息即可求解，而非自适应量子接收机的通信代价随任务参数无界增长；据此论证不存在有界经典通信能复现纠缠辅助三元经典信道产生的所有关联。进一步建立自适应与非自适应量子协议之间的两条定量关系定理（有限纠缠维度下的有限通信模拟；以O(d log(1/ε))条非自适应消息实现误差ε内的模拟）。

Result: (1) 存在仅用3条纠缠辅助消息、自适应可解而非自适应通信代价无界的任务；(2) 纠缠辅助三元经典信道的关联集合无法用任何有界经典通信量复现；(3) 任何使用有限纠缠维度的自适应协议都可由有限通信量的非自适应协议模拟；(4) 任何发送d条消息的自适应协议都可用O(d log(1/ε))条非自适应消息在误差ε内模拟。

Conclusion: 自适应在量子通信中具有本质上无界的优势；但要观测到极大优势，必须同时具备高维纠缠与极高精度的测量。换言之，优势的无界性属于渐近/高维极限，实际中受纠缠维度与观测精度双重制约。

Abstract: Shared entanglement can reduce the amount of classical communication needed to perform distributed computation. This can be achieved either by first measuring the entanglement and then communicating classically, or by adapting the quantum measurement to the received message. Here, we introduce tasks that adaptive protocols can solve using just three entanglement-assisted messages, while the communication cost grows without bound when the quantum receiver is not adaptive. Consequently, no bounded amount of classical communication can reproduce all correlations generated by an entanglement-assisted ternary classical channel. We establish two complementary limitations on the relation between adaptive and non-adaptive quantum protocols. First, every adaptive protocol using a finite entanglement dimension admits a non-adaptive simulation at finite communication cost. Second, every adaptive protocol sending $d$ messages can be simulated to within error $\varepsilon$ using $O(d\log(1/\varepsilon))$ non-adaptive messages. Our results reveal the fundamentally unbounded advantage of adaptivity in quantum communication, but also that observing very large advantages requires both high-dimensional entanglement and very precise observations.

</details>


### [105] [What an Observer Reads off a Holographic Screen](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11356)
*Kenan Oggad,James F. Glazebrook,Chris Fields*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文以"量子比特阵列型全息屏"为对象，把广义全息原理中两可分离系统间相互作用写成对易±1观测量的加权和，用"权重谱(profile)"刻画探针读出内容；证明读数在任意加权下都携带profile当且仅当各比特非零迹在GF(2)上线性无关，并借助无限细化分组的射有限/凝聚集边界与monogamous纠缠模型（部分匹配）给出应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统全息屏被当作几何曲面，而量子信息视角下更像量子比特阵列。作者希望弄清：当屏是比特数组而非曲面时，观察者究竟能从中读出什么、哪些读出是普适的（只依赖权重表），以及这种刻画在量子关联（contextuality）与ER=EPR类模型中有何推论。

Method: 1）把两可分离系统的相互作用表示为对易±1可观测量在屏上的加权和乘全局前因子；权重非负、前因子为1、联合本征空间为一维线时，谱仅由权重表(profile)决定。2）以扩散探针在等权屏上读出的running dimension量化偏离2的程度，并考察几何profile经能级计数产生的任意正高度平台。3）把观察者读出形式化为比特分组，读出携带profile等价于"计入重数的能级与每组一权的屏相同"；用GF(2)上的线性无关性证明任意加权下的充要条件，并给出依赖情形的有限个真线性子空间结论。4）无限细化分组得到射有限边界/凝聚集，用正相容profile给出局部常值函数分离距离。5）应用于contextuality判定、模糊球极点间距的领头短fall，以及monogamous entanglement模型中weight-one thread可同时占据的对=部分匹配。

Result: 等权（和为1）屏上，扩散探针单位时间读出的running dimension与2之差不超过比特数倒数的两倍；几何profile通过能级计数给出任意正高度平台。读出在每种加权下都携带profile，当且仅当其比特的非零迹列表在GF(2)上线性无关；此时读出（至多差一个可逆重编码）是其覆盖比特的一个划分，而线性依赖的读出只在权重空间的有限个真线性子空间中携带profile。无限细化构成边界为射有限集（因而是凝聚集）的塔，严格正的相容profile在此以某距离分离局部常值函数。应用侧：认证概率性与强contextuality，给出模糊球两极点距离的领头短fall，并在monogamous entanglement模型中证明可同时占据的配对恰为部分匹配。

Conclusion: 比特屏的可观测量在profile条件下只留下权重表这一自由度，给出"读出=划分"的刚性刻画像，并把有限分组提升为射有限/凝聚框架；该语言统一处理contextuality认证、模糊球几何与ER=EPR类纠缠约束，其中部分匹配是weight-one thread的极限。

Abstract: What does an observer read off a holographic screen that is an array of qubits rather than a surface? In the generalized holographic principle the interaction between two separable systems acts on such a screen as a weighted sum, up to an overall prefactor, of commuting observables with values plus and minus one. When the weights are nonnegative and the prefactor is one and the joint eigenspaces are lines, everything a probe reads off the spectrum depends only on the list of weights, which we call the profile. On the screen with equal weights summing to one the running dimension read by a diffusion probe falls short of two at unit time by at most twice the reciprocal of the number of qubits; a geometric profile gives instead, through its level count, a plateau of any positive height. An observer correlates sets of qubits; its readout carries a profile at a given weighting when the levels it reads, counted with multiplicity, are those of a screen with one weight per set. We prove that a readout carries a profile at every weighting exactly when the distinct nonzero traces of its qubits are linearly independent over the field with two elements, the trace of a qubit being the list of sets that contain it; such a readout is a partition of the qubits it covers up to an invertible recoding, and a dependent one carries a profile only on finitely many proper linear subspaces of weightings. Refining a grouping without end builds a tower whose boundary is a profinite set and hence a condensed set, on which a strictly positive compatible profile separates the locally constant functions by a distance. Applications certify probabilistic and strong contextuality and give the leading shortfall of the distance between the poles of the fuzzy sphere; in a model of monogamous entanglement the pairs that weight-one threads representing operational ER=EPR can occupy at once are exactly the partial matchings.

</details>


### [106] [Information--control limits for record-indistinguishable reset of a monitored qubit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11393)
*Sinan Bugu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究连续监测下的量子比特重置问题：在使测量记录与未驱动基态记录统计上不可区分的前提下，刻画重置所需控制量，并给出高努力极限下控制代价与可区分度乘积的精确常数 K(η)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续监测的量子比特会把初始激发态这一制备信息写入测量记录，因此"重置到基态"不仅是态的重置，还意味着测量记录也必须与真正基态比特的记录在统计上接近。作者希望在无弛豫模型下定量刻画"记录不可区分度"与"控制努力"之间的权衡，并求出最优常数。

Method: 模型：σ_z 测量率 Γ_m、效率 η、无弛豫。以受控记录律相对参考律的 KL 散度 D 度量可区分性，以积分平方 Rabi 频率 J 度量控制代价，考虑对记录因果作用的反馈。证明开环驱动使 D 无穷，并建立不等式 DJ ≥ Γ_m U(η, D/κ_0)（κ_0=1−ln2），再通过构造两相位反馈策略序列求高努力极限常数。

Result: 1) 任意开环驱动的 D 为无穷；2) 任意容许控制满足 DJ ≥ Γ_m U(η, D/κ_0)；3) 高努力精确常数 lim_{ε↓0} inf{DJ/Γ_m : D≤ε} = K(η) = [∫_0^π √(2η(1+cosθ)²+(1−η)sin²θ) dθ]²，并由显式两相位反馈策略达到；4) 全局常数 c(η)=inf DJ/Γ_m 满足 K/2 ≤ F_2 ≤ c ≤ K，η=1 与 η→0 极限下已知精确值。

Conclusion: 重置代价由两部分证据构成：旋转向基态期间记录获得的布居证据，以及未记录部分测量使相干退相位、随后再纯化时记录获得的"杂质"证据。c(η) 是无弛豫模型特有的量：只要存在非零弛豫率，DJ 的无约束下确界为零。

Abstract: A qubit that is continuously monitored from the moment it is prepared in its excited state writes evidence of that preparation into its measurement record. We ask how much control is needed to reset such a qubit to its ground state while keeping its complete record statistically close to the record of an undriven ground-state qubit. The qubit is measured in $\sigma_z$ at rate $\Gamma_m$ with efficiency $\eta$ and has no relaxation. For feedback that acts causally on the record, distinguishability is the Kullback--Leibler divergence $D$ of the controlled record law from the reference law, and the control cost is the quadratic effort $J$, the integrated squared Rabi frequency. We prove that every open-loop drive leaves $D$ infinite and that every admissible control obeys $DJ\ge\Gamma_mU(\eta,D/\kappa_0)$, where $U$ is given by a one-parameter family of integrals and $\kappa_0=1-\ln2$. Our main result is the exact high-effort constant $\lim_{\epsilon\downarrow0}\inf\{DJ/\Gamma_m: D\le\epsilon\}=K(\eta)$, where $K(\eta)=[\int_0^\pi\sqrt{2\eta(1+\cos\theta)^2+(1-\eta)\sin^2\theta}\,d\theta]^2$; an explicit sequence of two-phase feedback policies attains it. The first term of the integrand is the population evidence that the record receives while the qubit is rotated toward the ground state. The second is the price of the impurity created when the unrecorded part of the measurement dephases the coherence that the rotation needs; the record receives this evidence later, when the measurement repurifies the qubit. The global constant $c(\eta)=\inf DJ/\Gamma_m$ satisfies $K/2\le F_2\le c\le K$, where $F_2$ is again given by an integral; $c$ is known exactly at $\eta=1$ and in the limit $\eta\to0$. With any nonzero relaxation rate the unconstrained infimum of $DJ$ vanishes, so $c(\eta)$ is specific to the relaxation-free model.

</details>


### [107] [High-Dimensional Maximally Entangled OAM States by Joint Pump and Modal-Space Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11395)
*Richard Bernecker,Baghdasar Baghdasaryan,Fabian Steinlechner,Stephan Fritzsche*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文揭示SPDC中空间泵浦结构与OAM子空间选择的相互作用，给出任意维度d的d×d子空间内最大纠缠态所需的泵浦轮廓，实现高维最大纠缠态的泵浦工程化制备。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SPDC产生的OAM双光子态因模式分布高度不均匀而非最大纠缠；已有泵浦整形方法多局限于低维子空间，缺乏对任意维度d>2生成最大纠缠态的一般性理解。

Method: 分析泵浦光束的空间结构与所选OAM子空间之间的相互制约关系，据此直接确定目标最大纠缠态所需的泵浦轮廓，并推广到任意d的d×d子空间。

Result: 建立了泵浦轮廓与OAM子空间选择的对应规则，可实现任意维度d的高维最大纠缠态制备，并表明其需对有效高维Hilbert子空间进行审慎且非平凡的设计。

Conclusion: OAM最大纠缠的制备依赖于泵浦结构与子空间的联合设计，为高维纠缠态的通用化工程制备提供了普适判据。

Abstract: Spontaneous parametric down-conversion (SPDC) generates correlated photon pairs that inherently exhibit high-dimensional spatial entanglement in orbital angular momentum (OAM) modes. In practice, the resulting two-photon states are typically not maximally entangled due to a highly non-uniform distribution of generated OAM modes. Although pump-beam engineering has emerged as a tool to prepare high-dimensional entangled states, it has usually been restricted to low-dimensional subspaces, i.e., small sets of selected OAM modes for each photon within the full infinite-dimensional two-photon Hilbert space. A general understanding of the requirements for the generation of maximally entangled states (MESs) for any dimensionality $d$ with $d>2$ is lacking. Here, we demonstrate how pump engineering enables the generation of high-dimensional MESs for arbitrary $d$ defined in $d \times d$ subspaces. By identifying the interplay between the spatial pump structure and the choice of OAM subspace, the required pump profile for a target MES is directly determined. Our results reveal that maximal entanglement with OAM requires a careful and nontrivial design of the effective high-dimensional Hilbert subspace.

</details>


### [108] [Universal robust nonadiabatic geometric quantum computation with shortened composite pulses on superconducting circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11420)
*Cheng-Yun Ding,Na Kuang,Yu Jin,Li Tang,Rui Huang,Li-Hua Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种基于缩短复合脉冲的通用鲁棒非绝热几何量子门方案，控制简单、演化参数充足，可同时抑制多类系统误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统非绝热几何量子计算方案结合最优控制提升保真度与鲁棒性时，往往需付出延长门时间、复杂脉冲控制为代价，且多局限于特定量子门或特定误差源，难以实现简单控制下的通用、鲁棒、短门时间几何门。

Method: 采用缩短的复合脉冲构造通用几何门，脉冲控制简单，并利用足够多的演化参数来降低对普适系统误差的敏感性；通过数值模拟与真实超导量子线路（基于实验已验证技术）进行验证。

Result: 数值模拟表明，所构造的通用几何门集合相比传统单环对应方案具有显著更强的抗误差能力；在真实超导量子线路上的验证也确认了该方案的鲁棒性优势。

Conclusion: 该工作为在多重误差下实现高保真、鲁棒的几何量子计算提供了可行途径。

Abstract: The nonadiabatic geometric quantum computation (NGQC) using the nonadiabatic geometric phases is a promising strategy to realize the high-fidelity quantum operation for large-scale quantum processing. However, conventional NQGC schemes combining with optimal control techniques to further improve the gate fidelity and robustness usually either comes at the cost of prolonged gate time or require complex pulse control and only are limited to several certain specific quantum gates or/and for a certain type error source. Thus, implementing universal and robust geometric gates with simple control while further suppressing gate times remains a major challenge. Here, we propose a scheme for universal robust geometric gates with shortened composite pulses, where the pulse control is simple and enough available evolution parameters can be utilized to effectively reduce the sensitivity to universal systematic errors. Numerical simulations demonstrate that our universal geometric gates set exhibit much stronger error resilience than their conventional single-loop counterparts. Finally, we also verify the robustness advantage of our scheme on realistic superconducting quantum circuits with experimentally demonstrated technology. Therefore, our work paves an alternative way to achieve high-fidelity and robust geometric quantum computation against multiple errors.

</details>


### [109] [Alchemically Transferable Parameters for Variational Quantum Eigensolvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11445)
*Jonas Motyl,Jakob S. Kottmann*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出"炼金式"几何投影方法：用替代氢原子沿目标分子键方向映射局部成键环境到小氢片段，从而将已有的氢体系机器学习电路参数预测器直接迁移到有机分子，无需按用例重训练；每个键类型仅需一个投影半径，在小参考分子上拟合一次即可复用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变分量子本征求解器(VQE)的经典优化环路是主要瓶颈（贫瘠高原、局部极小、能量与梯度评估代价高），机器学习直接预测电路参数可规避该环路。已有工作表明结构化电路设计+ML模型可在氢体系内跨体系尺寸与结构迁移，但迁移到有机分子通常被认为需要用例特定的重训练数据。

Method: 建立"炼金式"(alchemical)迁移途径：通过几何投影在目标分子各键方向上放置替代氢原子，把其局部成键环境映射到小型氢片段上，从而原样复用现有氢体系求解器。唯一新增量为每种键类型对应的单个投影半径，仅在一个小型参考分子上拟合一次后保持不变。方法在先前工作所用的可分离对(separable-pair)电路设计上验证，并扩展到跨越量子子空间有效基的多电路(multi-circuit)方法。

Result: 实验表明向有机分子扩展并不需要用例特定训练集：复用氢体系预测器即可获得参数（摘要未给出定量误差/能量精度数据）。方法在可分离对电路设计上得到验证，并成功扩展至多电路子空间基方法。

Conclusion: 有机分子上的ML预测电路参数迁移可通过几何"炼金"投影实现，避免了昂贵的重训练；每键类型单一投影半径的低维拟合使现有氢体系求解器可即插即用地复用，且框架可拓展到多电路/子空间有效基方案。

Abstract: Machine-learned circuit parameters could remove one of the main bottlenecks of variational quantum eigensolvers: the classical optimization loop, with its typical barren plateaus, local minima, and costly energy and gradient evaluations, is replaced by a direct prediction. Prior work showed that machine-learning models paired with a structured circuit design can transfer across system size and structure within hydrogenic systems. The next step: Extending them to organic molecules would seem to require re-training on use-case-specific training sets. In this work, we show that this is not necessary by establishing an alchemical approach. A geometric projection places surrogate hydrogen atoms along the bond directions of the target molecule and so maps its local bonding environment onto small hydrogenic fragments. Existing hydrogenic solvers can then be reused as they are. The only new quantity is a single projection radius per bond type, which is fitted once on a small reference molecule and reused unchanged thereafter. We demonstrate the approach on the separable-pair circuit designs that have been used in prior work and extend the approach to multi-circuit approaches that span effective bases for quantum subspaces.

</details>


### [110] [One Bit, Many Records: Information Replication in Quantum Many-Body Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11446)
*Dan-Bo Zhang,Yan-Ming Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出"动力学选择"机制：未受扰的非热参考态保持局域可辨识，而受局域扰动的分支在同一哈密顿量下扩散弛豫，从而在大量不相交子系统上同时可读地复制一个经典比特，并用熵-容量判据刻画其条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子多体系统中信息复制（单个局域编码的经典比特在大量不相交区域同时可读）的生成机制缺乏系统理解，需要给出机制与判据，并提供可实验检验的判据。

Method: 构造对照式的"参考-扰动"双分支演化（同一哈密顿量下，未扰动支为可辨识非热参考态，扰动支扩散弛豫），推导熵-容量判据；在嵌入二聚体链（embedded-dimer chain）与受稳定化的 PXP 动力学两类模型中数值/解析验证。

Result: 熵-容量判据表明：均匀面积律参考态配体积律扰动分支，可在固定且非零的信息阈值下保证广延数量的记录；嵌入二聚体链同时出现记录增殖，且读出可靠性随碎片尺寸增大而提高；PXP 动力学将该机制推广到相干回归型参考态。

Conclusion: 信息扩散可由可及记录的空间多重性来刻画，动力学选择为量子多体记录生成提供了通用机制，并给出可通过固定局域读出测量的实验特征。

Abstract: Information replication occurs when a single locally encoded classical bit becomes simultaneously readable across extensively many disjoint regions of a quantum many-body system. Here we identify dynamical selection as a mechanism for generating these records, whereby an unperturbed nonthermal reference remains locally recognizable while its locally perturbed counterpart spreads and relaxes under the same Hamiltonian. We establish an entropy-capacity criterion showing that a uniform area-law reference paired with a volume-law perturbed branch guarantees extensive records at a fixed, nonzero information threshold. An embedded-dimer chain demonstrates both record proliferation and increasing readout reliability with fragment size, while stabilized PXP dynamics extends the mechanism to a coherently recurrent reference. These results characterize information spreading through the spatial multiplicity of accessible records and provide experimental signatures measurable via fixed local readouts.

</details>


### [111] [Quantum State Reconstruction from Sklar-Type Relational Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11468)
*Yi-Yu Lin,Jie-Chen Jin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文借鉴Sklar定理将联合分布分解为边缘统计与依赖结构的思想，对量子态在各测量上下文中的Born概率表只保留各子系统间的关系数据（丢弃边缘统计），并证明这些"关系数据"在全部上下文上收集后足以唯一重构任意不可约多比特量子态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典概率中Sklar定理严格区分了联合分布的边缘统计与依赖结构（copula）。作者希望将这一视角引入量子情形，探究量子态在Born规则下产生的各上下文经典概率表中，"关系数据"是否承载了量子性的核心约束，以及丢弃的局部信息能否被完整恢复。

Method: 对给定量子态的每一个Born概率表，剥离各参与方的边缘统计，仅保留子系统间的关联（依赖结构）数据；随后将这些经典关系数据跨所有上下文汇总，并利用量子力学中非平凡的量子相容性（compatibility）约束进行态重构。

Result: 证明了：当把全部上下文上的Sklar型关系数据汇集后，量子相容性约束足以重构任意不可约（irreducible）多比特量子态本身；即逐表丢弃的子系统局部信息可被完全恢复。

Conclusion: 量子态的关联结构在Sklar意义下编码了完整的态信息，边缘统计的丢弃并不造成信息损失。作者将这一现象命名为"基于Sklar型关系数据的量子态重构"，为量子关联与经典copula理论的交叉提供了新视角。

Abstract: Sklar's theorem in classical probability theory rigorously separates the marginal statistics of a joint probability distribution from its dependence structure data. We apply this perspective to the context-dependent classical probability tables induced by the Born rule for a quantum state. For each Born probability table of a given quantum state, we discard the marginal statistics of the individual parties, retaining only the relational data among them. Yet we prove that, when these classical relational data are collected over all contexts, the nontrivial quantum compatibility constraints inherent in quantum mechanics suffice to reconstruct any irreducible multiqubit quantum state itself. This means that the local information of the subsystems, discarded table by table, is fully recovered. We call this quantum-state reconstruction from Sklar-type relational data.

</details>


### [112] [Weight-Space Programming of Variational Quantum Circuits: Large Language Models Write Quantum Circuit Parameters Without an Optimizer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11537)
*Debarshi Kundu,Swaroop Ghosh,Vasant G Honavar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出将经典网络的"权重空间编程"扩展到量子线路：直接让前沿LLM（GPT）根据问题、线路与门约定写出VQA的角度参数，且这些角度被原样执行、不提供任何损失/梯度/精度反馈；在139个请求上覆盖手写数字分类、VQE基态估计与MaxCut QAOA，结果显示当参数具备可解释结构时LLM可媲美或接近优化器，并可作为强热启动，但在高维不透明权重空间和门约定敏感性上失效。作者公开全部prompt、回复与评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VQA依赖对量子设备的反复评估来优化线路角度，常受梯度消失与高昂优化成本困扰。作者由此追问：能否让大语言模型"直接写出"这些角度，从而绕开迭代优化，并检验LLM是否具备对量子线路参数空间的先验理解。

Method: 将weight-space programming从经典网络迁移到量子线路：向GPT提供问题描述、线路结构与门约定，令其直接输出角度，执行时不回传loss、梯度或准确率。共139个请求，任务包括手写数字（3 vs 8，10类）分类、自旋链与H₂的VQE基态估计、MaxCut的QAOA；对照组为随机权重、经典优化器，以及以LLM权重为初值的优化。另设"允许LLM自行设计线路"的实验。

Result: 在参数具可解释结构时LLM匹配或接近优化器：每个VQE问题上达到能量谱的95–100%，以闭式推导出H₂精确基态，对每个图都找到最优深度一QAOA角度；用12–18个手写权重在3 vs 8上取得93–96%准确率，超过在同十个样本上训练的优化器。作为热启动时，优化在中位数1次评估后即达最佳值（而非数百次）。允许设计线路时，30例全部选择数据依赖的旋转链（二分类用1个量子比特，十类每比特一条链），十分位分类达65%。失效情形：固定线路参数超过30个时准确率降至随机水平而优化器反而改善；对门约定敏感，42个QAOA回复中6个混淆混合角符号而失败。

Conclusion: LLM具备可用的量子参数空间先验，能在结构化、低维且语义可解释的权重空间中直接给出近优角度，并作为高效热启动；但其能力随参数维度上升而崩溃，且对门约定等表示细节脆弱。说明"LLM直接编程量子权重"是可行但有明确适用边界的范式，需与经典优化互补。

Abstract: Variational quantum algorithms (VQAs) optimize circuit angles through repeated quantum-device evaluations, often facing vanishing gradients and high optimization costs. We ask whether a large language model (LLM) can write these angles directly. Extending *weight-space programming* from classical networks to quantum circuits, we provide a frontier LLM (\gpt) with the problem, circuit, and gate conventions. Its angles are executed exactly as written, without returning losses, gradients, or accuracy feedback. Across 139 requests, we test handwritten-digit classification, VQE ground-state estimation for spin chains and H$_2$, and MaxCut QAOA, comparing against random weights, classical optimization, and optimization initialized with LLM weights on the same circuit. When parameters have interpretable structure, the LLM matches or approaches the optimizer: it achieves 95--100\% of the energy spectrum on every VQE problem, derives the exact H$_2$ ground state in closed form, finds optimal depth-one QAOA angles for every graph, and reaches 93--96\% accuracy on digits 3 versus 8 with 12--18 hand-written weights, outperforming an optimizer trained on the same ten examples. Its weights are also strong warm starts: optimization reaches its best value after a median of one evaluation rather than hundreds. Allowed to design circuits, the LLM chooses data-dependent rotation chains encoding pixel importance in all 30 cases (one qubit for binary tasks, one chain per qubit for ten classes), reaching 65\% accuracy across ten digits. Yet the approach fails in opaque weight spaces: accuracy drops to chance when fixed circuits exceed 30 weights, while optimization improves. It is also sensitive to gate conventions: 6 of 42 QAOA replies reverse mixing-angle signs and fail. We release all prompts, replies, and scores.

</details>


### [113] [A Microscopic Model Underpinning Macroscopic QED in Active Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11557)
*Daigo Oue*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文从被驱动的三能级系统出发，用量子中心极限方法建立活性介质中宏观量子电动力学的微观基础，统一导出电磁极化率与量子噪声流关联，并揭示透明点处噪声不为零及其对近邻量子探针的加热效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统宏观量子电动力学（mQED）的储库算符多为唯象引入，缺乏与具体微观非平衡稳态的明确联系，尤其活性（增益/吸收）介质中噪声与响应的微观来源不清。

Method: 以驱动三能级系统为起点，绝热消去得到具有非平衡稳态布居的有效二能级描述；采用量子中心极限方法导出集体Langevin动力学，从同一微观稳态同时得到电磁极化率与量子噪声流关联，并与常规mQED储库算符对应。

Result: 光学响应由布居差决定，而噪声关联分别依赖两能级各自布居；在光学透明处极化率虚部为零但量子涨落有限。噪声统计可解释为有效储库占据数与涉及两个真空模式的纯化，并建立相应的有效温度涨落–耗散关系；透明处的残余涨落可对邻近量子探针产生有限加热。

Conclusion: 该工作为活性介质mQED提供了自洽的微观基础，表明响应与噪声虽源自同一稳态但受不同布居量控制，透明点并非"无噪声"，其残余真空涨落具有可观测的物理后果。

Abstract: We establish a microscopic foundation for macroscopic quantum electrodynamics in active media. Starting from a driven three-level system, we derive an effective two-level description with nonequilibrium stationary populations and employ a quantum central limit approach to obtain collective Langevin dynamics. This construction yields the electromagnetic susceptibility and quantum noise-current correlations from the same microscopic stationary state, providing a direct connection to the reservoir operators of conventional macroscopic quantum electrodynamics. We show that the optical response is governed by the population difference, whereas the noise correlations depend separately on the populations of the two levels. Consequently, at optical transparency, the imaginary part of the susceptibility vanishes while quantum fluctuations remain finite. We interpret the resulting noise statistics in terms of an effective reservoir occupation and a purification involving two vacuum modes, and establish a corresponding effective-temperature fluctuation--dissipation relation. Finally, we demonstrate that the residual fluctuations at transparency can induce finite heating of a nearby quantum probe.

</details>


### [114] [A geometrical approach for obtaining exact analytical solutions for a scalar field in a one-dimensional dynamical cavity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11595)
*Maxwell F. de Araújo,Odemar Julião do N. Neto,Stanley S. Coelho,Jeferson Danilo L. Silva,Alessandra N. Braga,Danilo T. Alves*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将 Cole 与 Schieve（1995）的几何方法从长期被视为数值手段，提升为推导 Moore 泛函方程精确解析解的有力工具，并给出多例原创解析解及可无限扩展解集的推广方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态 Casimir 效应自 Moore（1969）提出以来，其核心方程 Moore 泛函方程的精确解析解极为稀少，仅局限于少数特定运动规律；Cole-Schieve 几何方法虽给出初始静止腔的通用解公式，但一直被当作纯数值方法使用，其解析潜力未被挖掘。

Method: 以 (1+1) 维、Dirichlet 边界条件下含移动镜面的腔中实标量场为对象，将 Cole-Schieve 几何途径重新诠释为解析求解框架：把 Moore 函数解的构造转化为时空区域的几何拼接；并进一步推广该方法，证明只要在任一时空区域已确定 Moore 函数，即可据此构造其他区域的精确解。

Result: 获得三个原创精确解析解：(1) 匀速移动镜面的膨胀腔；(2) 镜面渐近趋近静止镜面的收缩腔；(3) 复现 Law（1994）关于共振镜面运动的首个解析解。同时证明扩展的几何方法可将已知解析解由有限几类运动扩展为无限集。

Conclusion: Cole-Schieve 几何方法不仅是数值工具，更是构造 Moore 方程精确解析解的系统性方法；其扩展形式允许通过组合新解与已有解生成任意多的新运动规律类别，从而将由少数几类运动构成的解析解集合拓展为无限集，为动态 Casimir 效应的解析研究提供通用途径。

Abstract: The dynamical Casimir effect was first predicted by G. T. Moore in 1969, who considered a real scalar field inside a cavity with a moving mirror in ($1+1$)D, satisfying Dirichlet boundary conditions. Moore wrote the general solution for the field in terms of a function that satisfies a functional equation nowadays known as the Moore  this http URL  the literature, exact analytical solutions to the Moore equation form a rather small set, restricted to specific classes of laws of motion. Cole and Schieve (1995) developed a geometrical approach that yielded a general formula for the solution of the Moore equation for cavities initially at rest. However, its application has always been done through numerical calculations, and their approach has therefore long been regarded solely as a numerical method. In this work, we show that their method can also be employed as a powerful method for deriving exact analytical solutions, and show the applicability of this approach to deriving analytical solutions to the Moore equation. We obtain original exact analytical solutions for an expanding cavity with a uniformly moving mirror, a contracting cavity with a mirror that approaches the static one asymptotically, and, in addition, we recover the first analytical solution to the Moore equation for a resonant mirror motion, originally obtained by Law in 1994. Moreover, we extend the Cole-Schieve approach, showing that exact solutions can be constructed from any spacetime region where the Moore function has already been determined. We show that this extended geometrical approach allows us to expand the set of known analytical solutions to the Moore equation from the few classes of motion reported in the literature to an infinite set, as it enables the treatment of new classes of laws of motion obtained by combining those presented here with each other as well as with previously known classes of trajectories.

</details>


### [115] [Proof of the Aaronson-Ambainis Conjecture: An Exposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11626)
*Guy Blanc,Jordan Docter,Carmen Strassle,Li-Yang Tan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文是对Aaronson–Ambainis猜想近期证明的阐释性综述，围绕四个主要步骤组织证明结构，不优化参数，所得指数弱于原证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Aaronson–Ambainis猜想断言：任意次数为$d$的多项式$p:\\{0,1\\}^n\to[0,1]$必存在影响度至少为$\mathrm{poly}(\mathrm{Var}(p)/d)$的变量。该猜想若成立，意味着判定问题的量子查询算法可在大多数输入上被经典高效模拟，并给出随机预言机世界与非相对化世界中$\mathsf{BPP}$ vs. $\mathsf{BQP}$问题的近似等价。作者旨在向更广读者清晰呈现这一新证明的核心思想。

Method: 采用教学式阐释（exposition）策略：将证明分解为四个主要步骤，并说明各步骤与布尔函数分析、概率组合学文献的联系；为突出关键思路而刻意放弃参数优化。

Result: 给出完整且可读的证明框架，推导出该猜想成立的结论（影响度下界形式为$\mathrm{poly}(\mathrm{Var}(p)/d)$），但所得多项式指数弱于原始证明。

Conclusion: 该证明可导出量子查询算法在多数输入上的经典高效模拟，并建立随机预言机与非相对化情形下$\mathsf{BPP}$与$\mathsf{BQP}$关系的近似等价；本文作为非正式阐释，不作为会议或期刊投稿。作者注明证明由David Turturean借助PSI研究系统与未公开的Anthropic模型发现，作者未获相关机构经济补偿。

Abstract: We give an exposition of a recent proof of the Aaronson-Ambainis conjecture, which states that every degree-$d$ polynomial $p : \{0,1\}^n \to [0,1]$ has a variable of influence at least $\mathrm{poly}(\mathrm{Var}(p)/d)$. This implies that quantum query algorithms for decision problems can be efficiently simulated classically on most inputs. It also yields a near-equivalence between the status of the $\mathsf{BPP}$ vs. $\mathsf{BQP}$ problem in the random oracle and unrelativized worlds.
 We organize the proof around four main steps and discuss how each connects to the literature on the analysis of boolean functions and probabilistic combinatorics. To bring out the key ideas, we do not optimize parameters, and our presentation yields weaker exponents than the original.
 $\textbf{Authors' note.}$ The proof was discovered by David Turturean using a research system developed at Physical Superintelligence (PSI), in combination with a non-public Anthropic model. The proof was then shared with the authors, who worked to understand it and prepare this exposition. Anthropic's public models were used in this process.
 The authors did not receive financial compensation from Anthropic or PSI. This article is not intended for publication at a conference or in a journal.

</details>


### [116] [Pauli propagation in adaptive local frames](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11647)
*Jonnathan Pineda-Jiménez,Mario Collura*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出框架自适应Pauli传播(FAPP)：逐门将各量子比特基底旋转至算符局域极化主轴，将Pauli复杂度分解为旋转不变的占据部分与可由基底消除的"味"部分，显著降低截断误差与内存开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Pauli传播的计算成本依赖于演化算符展开所用的局域基底，但此前缺乏对基底依赖性的定量刻画以及低成本选择基底的原则性方法。

Method: 把Pauli复杂度分解为两部分：对局域旋转不变的占据部分（由算符质量与长度分布给出下界）和可由合适基底消除的味部分；据此提出FAPP，在Pauli传播的内存结构内、精确地逐门将每个量子比特的基底旋转到其局域极化主轴，并以极化量预测任意局域框架可获得的增益上限。

Result: 横纵场Ising链中，固定内存下截断误差最多降低一个数量级，内存降低与体系尺寸无关的三倍；无限二维格点上达到给定认证精度所需字符串最多减少七倍；掺T门的Clifford电路中字符串减少二至八倍。

Conclusion: Pauli传播成本可分解为基底不变量与基底可消除量，简单的逐门主轴对齐规则即可接近最优局域框架，为Pauli传播提供高效、可预测的基底自适应方案。

Abstract: The cost of Pauli propagation depends on the local basis in which the evolving operator is expanded. We show that its Pauli complexity splits into an occupation part, invariant under local rotations and bounded from below by the operator mass and length distributions, and a flavour part that a suitable basis can remove. We introduce frame-adaptive Pauli propagation (FAPP), which rotates the basis of every qubit, gate by gate, to the principal axes of the operator's local polarisation, exactly and within the memory structure of Pauli propagation. This simple rule achieves most of what the best local frame can, and the polarisation predicts how much any local frame can gain. In the Ising chain in transverse and longitudinal fields, FAPP lowers the truncation error at fixed memory by up to an order of magnitude and the memory by a size-independent factor of three; on the infinite two-dimensional lattice it needs up to seven times fewer strings for a given certified accuracy, and in Clifford circuits doped with T gates two to eight times fewer.

</details>


### [117] [Model-family selection and recovery-gate design in generator-based noise tomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11748)
*Long Huang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将生成模型噪声层析中的"家族选择"从硬件属性重构为假设检验问题，给出纯相干噪声模型下闭式的射击下限 N*=λ_req/(r_b S)²，并说明该下限仅由绝对偏差 r_b S 控制、可由标定预先计算；同一计算框架同时为恢复门（指纹与Fisher信息）定价，故不存在与设计无关的结论，也不存在与家族无关的设计最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上"家族选择"被引述为硬件属性，缺乏可事先量化、可跨器件比较的判据。作者希望建立一个统一的、可在单次射击之前完成的定量框架，用以判断某设计是否值得采用、以及采用它的代价，从而把硬件属性转译为可检验的统计假设问题。

Method: 在纯相干噪声模型下推导射击下限的闭式解 N*=λ_req/(r_b S)²，指出其由绝对偏差 r_b S 而非相对残差 r_b 控制，可由标定对任意器件预先计算；用同一映射同时给出固定家族残差的指纹与对设计排序的Fisher信息（二者同源，故不存在设计无关的判决或家族无关的最优设计）；进一步做结构分析：局限于所有设计都同样测量的块内的偏差是"设计盲"的，而能触及码块的偏差可使代价被重定价达68倍；在64种设计中重建指纹、分析放置（placement）与计数（counts）效应、秩与rms变化，并在三比特真实标定与五比特情形下评估。

Result: 两个平台为极端，分别为98.1%与48.0%；参考点处每分量下限为1.1×10⁷和6.7×10⁶次射击，但该点属"易角"——其方向在采样点上的中位数为8.9×10⁸，贵83倍。在单次运行所花费的10⁵次射击下，检验在0.05水平上的功效仅0.053。在64种设计下重建指纹，下限在一个平台上移动68倍、另一平台1.7倍；最精确设计的判决代价比最便宜设计高十倍。64种放置均满秩；三道门时rms仅因放置变化即相差2.7与3.9倍，而计数上的最优值却是平坦的。五比特下同一规则使下限移动21倍，判决代价为10⁹次射击；对照真实三比特标定，该下限首先是系统控制的要求而非射击计数，缺乏该控制时任何预算都无法弥补。

Conclusion: 家族选择应被当作假设检验而非硬件标签：其成本由绝对偏差决定的闭式射击下限刻画，可标定先验计算；同一映射使指纹与Fisher信息共享定价，因而设计与家族的最优性相互耦合、不可分离；偏差的结构位置（是否触及码块）决定设计能否重定价；在真实标定尺度上，系统误差控制优先于射击预算。该工作提供了可复现、可比较的判据，用于在给定器件与预算下确定家族与设计。

Abstract: Family selection in generator-model noise tomography, usually quoted as a hardware property, is a hypothesis test whose shot floor is closed form under a purely coherent noise model, $N^{*}=\lambda_{\mathrm{req}}/(r_{\mathrm{b}} S)^{2}$, controlled by the absolute bias $r_{\mathrm{b}} S$ rather than by the relative residual $r_{\mathrm{b}}$, hence computable for any device from a calibration, before a single shot. The same computation prices the recovery gates: the fingerprint that fixes a family's residual and the Fisher information that ranks designs come from the same map, so there is no design-independent verdict and no family-independent design optimum. Whether a design can move that price is structural: a bias confined to the block every design measures identically is design-blind, while one that reaches the code blocks can be repriced by a factor of sixty-eight; the two platforms are its extremes, at $98.1\%$ and $48.0\%$. At the reference point the floor is $1.1\times10^{7}$ and $6.7\times10^{6}$ shots per component, and that point is the easy corner: the median of its own direction over the sampled points is $8.9\times10^{8}$, $83$ times dearer. At the $10^{5}$ shots a run spends the test has power $0.053$ at a level of $0.05$. Rebuilding the fingerprint under each of the sixty-four designs moves the floor by $68$ on one platform and $1.7$ on the other, and the most precise design costs ten times more shots for the verdict than the cheapest to test. All sixty-four placements are full rank: at three gates the rms varies by factors of $2.7$ and $3.9$ with placement alone, while the optimum over counts is flat. At five qubits the same rule moves the floor by $21$ while the verdict costs $10^{9}$ shots; against a real three-qubit calibration the floor is a requirement on systematic control before it is a shot count, and where that control is missing no budget supplies it.

</details>


### [118] [Advantage Distillation in Paired-State Quantum Key Distribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11754)
*Luis Adrián Lizama-Pérez,José Mauricio López-Romero*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究成对态回环（Loop-Back）QKD中固定光站—便携终端的优势蒸馏，提出基于最小连通图上两条独立公开奇偶校验的三记录块筛选方案，证明奇偶多边形的闭合边冗余（任一生成树给出相同接受集），给出精确接受概率、残余错误律与保留率；三记录滤波将主导错误项由 e 降至 e^2，并建立含任意量子边信息的模块化安全性定理，在单光子无源分光信道下得到正的条件熵界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回环QKD中便携终端光源与探测器受限、信道损耗与窃听风险高，需在传统纠错（reconciliation）之前以低开销方式压低错误率；同时需明确后选择式筛选的公开校验信息泄露对安全性影响，弥补缺乏精确统计刻画与模块化安全证明的空白。

Method: Alice制备两路偏振编码脉冲，Bob对每脉冲选择并施加偏振滤波，Alice由回传探测图样推断其选择（成对态成码）。优势蒸馏采用三记录（各两比特）一致块筛选，在最小连通图上施加两条独立公开奇偶校验；证明奇偶多边形闭合边冗余、任一生成树具相同接受集；推导精确接受概率、残余错误定律与保留率；建立模块化安全性定理，界定syndrome公开与块接受后对手（含任意量子边信息）额外猜测优势；并对指定单光子无源分光信道做定量评估。

Result: 在独立比特错误模型下，三记录滤波将主导误差项由 e 降为 e^2，并显式核算弃置记录与公开信息代价；精确给出接受概率、残余错误律及保留率；模块化安全定理给出含量子边信息条件下的额外猜测优势上界；单光子无源分光信道下获得正的条件熵界及精确的接受块安全量。

Conclusion: 该奇偶校验型优势蒸馏通过后选择检测不一致，不纠正单个比特错误，定位于常规纠错之前的预处理步骤；架构可延伸至混合滤波—纠错流程及便携用户间经由共享量子站的密钥建立。

Abstract: We investigate advantage distillation for paired-state Loop-Back quantum key distribution, with a fixed optical station serving a lightweight portable terminal. Alice prepares two polarization-encoded pulses, Bob selects and applies a polarization filter to each pulse, and Alice infers his selections from the returned detection pattern. The proposed advantage-distillation step selects consistent blocks of three two-bit records using two independent public parity checks on a minimal connected graph. The closing edge of a parity polygon is shown to be redundant: any spanning tree of the same vertices has the same acceptance set. We derive exact acceptance probabilities, residual-error laws, and retention rates. Under independent bit errors, the three-record filter reduces the leading error term from e to e^2, while its cost in discarded records and public information is accounted for explicitly. A modular security theorem bounds the additional guessing advantage available to an adversary after syndrome disclosure and block acceptance, including arbitrary quantum side information. We also evaluate a specified single-photon passive-tap optical channel, obtaining a positive conditional entropy bound and exact accepted-block security quantities. This parity-based advantage-distillation step detects discrepancies by postselection; it does not correct individual errors and is intended to precede conventional reconciliation. The architecture suggests two further directions: hybrid filtering and reconciliation, and key establishment between portable users accessing a shared quantum station.

</details>


### [119] [An optimal quantum strategy for the $2$-hat game](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11759)
*Georgios Baziotis,Sang-Jun Park,Sang-Gyun Youn*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文将两人帽子游戏建模为二输入三输出的非局域博弈，精确求出量子交换值并证明其等于量子值 ω_q(H_2)=ω_qc(H_2)=(15+√33)/36≈0.576238，非信号值为 2/3。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两人帽子游戏是研究非局域性与量子优势的典型小规模博弈，但 H_2 的精确量子值及量子交换值此前未被确定，需要给出精确刻画并厘清量子值与量子交换值的关系。

Method: 将帽子游戏表述为二输入三输出的非局域博弈；利用博弈在二面体群 D_8 下的对称性及其表示论大幅约化相应矩阵（半定规划/矩阵）问题的规模，从而作精确优化；构造共享非最大纠缠三能级态（qutrit）的策略实现该优势；并计算非信号值。

Result: 得到 ω_q(H_2)=ω_qc(H_2)=(15+√33)/36≈0.576238，量子值等于量子交换值；最优量子优势可由非最大纠缠 qutrit 态达到；非信号值 ω_ns(H_2)=2/3。

Conclusion: H_2 的量子值为精确代数数，量子优势无需最大纠缠即可实现，D_8 表示论是约化求解的有效工具；量子值严格低于非信号值，说明该博弈可区分不同非局域资源层级。

Abstract: We study the two-player hat game as a two-input, three-output non-local game. We calculate the quantum commuting value and show that it coincides with the quantum one. In particular, we show that \[\omega_{\rm q}(H_2)=\omega_{\mathrm{qc}}(H_2)=\frac{15+\sqrt{33}}{36}\approx 0.576238.\] We achieve the optimal quantum advantage using a shared non-maximally entangled qutrit state. To establish optimality, we exploit the game's symmetry under the dihedral group $D_8$ and apply its representation theory to substantially reduce the complexity of the underlying matrix problem. We also determine the no-signalling value $\omega_{\mathrm{ns}}(H_2)=2/3$.

</details>


### [120] [Super-resolution quantum imaging via multiphoton correlation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11768)
*Yu Gu,Yiqi Song,Meixue Chen,Yuchen He,Hui Chen,Huaibin Zheng,Yu Zhou,Fuli Li,Zhuo Xu,Jianbin Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 利用经典热光，通过对Hanbury Brown-Twiss干涉仪两臂光子数进行关联，实现宽度可调的多光子关联峰/谷，从而突破衍射极限，实验获得时间11.8倍、空间5.1倍超分辨增强，并使标准鬼成像无法分辨的双孔清晰可辨。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统成像分辨率受衍射极限根本性限制，作者希望仅用经典热光（而非量子纠缠或非经典态光源）突破该极限，并探索经典与非经典现象之间的边界。

Method: 基于Hanbury Brown-Twiss干涉仪，分辨并关联两臂探测到的光子数，构建多光子子系统关联函数；其关联峰/谷宽度可通过平均光子数调控；开展时间与空间超分辨实验，并以标准鬼成像完全无法分辨的双孔作为原理性验证，关联两光子与零光子信号进行成像。

Result: 实验实现时间超分辨增强达11.8倍、空间超分辨增强达5.1倍；双孔目标在标准鬼成像下完全不可分辨，而通过两光子-零光子关联变得清晰可辨。

Conclusion: 该超分辨成像原理可理解为经典超分辨成像与量子超分辨成像的结合，为超分辨成像与光刻提供通用平台，并为探索经典与非经典现象边界开辟新途径。

Abstract: The resolution of conventional imaging is fundamentally constrained by the diffraction limit. Here we demonstrate that this limit can be overcome using classical thermal light, through the correlation of multiphoton subsystems. By resolving the photon numbers detected in the two arms of a Hanbury Brown-Twiss interferometer, the correlation function exhibits a narrowed peak or dip whose width is tunable via the average photon number. Temporal and spatial super-resolution enhancements up to 11.8-fold and 5.1-fold, respectively, are experimentally achieved. As a proof-of-principle experiment, a double-pinhole that is completely unresolved by standard ghost imaging becomes clearly distinguishable via the correlation of two-photon versus zero-photon. The principle of this super-resolution imaging can be interpreted as a combination of classical super-resolution imaging and quantum super-resolution imaging. This work provides a versatile platform for super-resolution imaging and lithography, and opens a new avenue for exploring the boundary between classical and nonclassical phenomena.

</details>


### [121] [Long-distance quantum secure direct communication with Gottesman-Kitaev-Preskill qubits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11822)
*Meng-Dong Zhu,Jia-Wei Ying,Qi Zhang,Shi-Pu Gu,Xing-Fu Wang,Lan Zhou,Yu-Bo Sheng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出基于GKP编码与第三代量子中继器的量子安全直接通信（QSDC）协议，在23 dB压缩度下可实现超过1000 km的安全传输距离，并建立成本导向的优化框架刻画通信距离、保密容量与中继资源间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GKP码的晶格周期结构可有效纠正相空间中噪声引起的小位移误差，且已被用于量子中继器中的长距离纠缠信道构建与QKD随机密钥分发；但如何将GKP纠错与量子中继结合用于直接传输秘密消息的长距离QSDC，并克服光子指数衰减损耗、兼顾人员安全，尚缺乏系统方案与资源优化设计策略。

Method: 采用GKP编码设计QSDC协议，直接传输秘密消息并保护通信人员安全；借助第三代量子中继器克服光子指数衰减损耗；通过数值模拟评估不同压缩度下的安全传输距离；并构建基于成本的优化框架，分析安全通信距离、保密容量与中继资源之间的权衡关系。

Result: 数值模拟表明，在23 dB压缩度下安全传输距离可超过1000 km；成本优化框架给出了安全距离、保密容量与中继资源之间的权衡关系，可实现中继部署优化，并为可扩展量子中继架构提供系统化设计策略。

Conclusion: 结果展示了GKP纠错与长距离QSDC及量子网络相结合的潜力，为可扩展量子中继网络的部署提供了系统设计思路。

Abstract: The Gottesman-Kitaev-Preskill (GKP) code possesses a lattice periodic structure that enables the effective correction of small displacement errors induced by noise in phase space, which is indispensable for quantum computation. The GKP code was also used to construct a long-distance entanglement channel in quantum repeaters and distribute random keys in quantum key distribution (QKD). Using GKP encoding, we propose a quantum secure direct communication (QSDC) protocol that not only transmits the secret message directly, but also helps protect the safety of communication personnel. The exponential attenuation loss of photons can be overcome by the third-generation quantum repeater. Numerical simulation results demonstrate that secure transmission distance exceeding 1000 km is achievable with a 23 dB squeezing level. Furthermore, we establish a cost-based optimization framework to characterize the trade-off among secure communication distance, secrecy capacity, and repeater resources, enabling optimized repeater deployment and providing a systematic design strategy for scalable quantum repeater architectures. The results demonstrate the potential of combining GKP error correction for long-distance QSDC and quantum networks.

</details>


### [122] [Rotational Encoding of Objective Functions for Quantum Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11858)
*Sonia Yang,Ali Al-Bayaty,Shanyan Chen,Marek Perkowski*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出角旋转计数器（ARC），用单量子比特寄存器上的累积量子旋转编码可分解为目标函数，用于有限自动机/状态机状态分配的量子oracle，在26个基准上显著降低量子电路成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往文献多用多量子比特寄存器表示可分解为布尔函数加权和的目标函数，量子oracle的量子比特数与门数开销大，需要更高效的编码方式以降低量子搜索优化的电路成本。

Method: 提出ARC：将目标函数编码为累积量子旋转之和，作用于单个量子比特寄存器而非多量子比特寄存器；数学推导并实验验证这些旋转对任意解均求和为目标值，并给出测量概率与目标值的关系；用加权汉明距离代价（二次分配问题的特例）为有限自动机与状态机的状态分配问题构造量子oracle，在26个基准上与布尔型oracle按四项成本指标对比。

Result: 相对布尔型oracle，ARC oracle平均降低量子比特数20.8%、门数89.3%、电路深度92.4%、量子成本90.6%。

Conclusion: 在所评估基准上ARC大幅降低电路成本，旋转式编码是量子搜索类优化中表示目标函数的有前景方法。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce the Angular Rotational Counter (ARC), a novel approach to represent objective functions that can be decomposed as a weighted sum of Boolean functions. ARC encodes the objective as an accumulated sum of quantum rotations onto a single-qubit register instead of a multi-qubit register used in much of the previous literature. We mathematically establish and experimentally validate that these rotations sum to the objective value for all possible solutions and derive the relationship between the resulting measurement probabilities and the objective. This design was applied to construct a quantum oracle for the state assignment problem of finite automata and state machines using a weighted Hamming distance cost function, which is a special case of the quadratic assignment problem. We evaluate this oracle design on 26 benchmarks and compare it with corresponding Boolean-based oracles on four cost metrics. Across these benchmarks, the oracle with ARC produces a mean reduction in the qubit count of 20.8%, gate count of 89.3%, circuit depth of 92.4%, and quantum cost of 90.6%. These results demonstrate substantial reductions in circuit cost for the evaluated benchmarks and establish rotational encoding as a promising approach for representing objective functions in optimization based on quantum search algorithms.

</details>


### [123] [AI-Driven Discovery of Entanglement Witness in Spin Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11935)
*Jiwei Tian,Mouyang Cheng,Mingda Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出EntNet框架，仅用低阶Pauli关联函数的少量局域幺正不变特征，借助Transformer即可高精度区分多体纠缠态与全可分态，并具备跨体系、跨哈密顿量的可迁移性与物理可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多体纠缠是量子科学的核心资源，但仅凭实验可获取的可观测量来判定多体混合态是否纠缠仍十分困难，现有纠缠判据多依赖难以测量的量或依赖特定模型。

Method: 在一维自旋-1/2短程哈密顿量体系中，构造至四体阶的可测Pauli关联函数的局域幺正不变表示，稀疏筛选出16个候选不变特征通道，再用带旋转位置编码（RoPE）的Transformer学习其空间结构，从而组合出纠缠见证量。

Result: 仅用低阶可观测量期望值，对可认证纠缠态召回率>98%，对可分态假阳性率接近零；在系统尺寸与不同哈密顿量族（含阻挫与无序模型）间迁移时精度损失极小；并揭示成对关联的选择性空间组织，其有限温度判据边界与Jordan-Wigner准粒子激发能隙相吻合。

Conclusion: EntNet建立了从局域关联函数数据驱动地发现可迁移、物理可解释纠缠见证量的框架，为实验友好的多体纠缠检测提供了新途径。

Abstract: Entanglement is a crucial resource in quantum science and engineering, but detecting it in many-body states from experimentally accessible observables remains challenging. Here we introduce EntNet, an AI-driven framework to discover compact combinations of spin observables that accurately distinguish entangled from fully separable many-body mixed states in one-dimensional spin-$1/2$ chains governed by short-range Hamiltonians. We construct local-unitary-invariant representations of measurable Pauli correlators up to four-body order, sparsely select 16 candidate invariant feature channels, and learn their spatial structure using a Transformer architecture with rotary positional embeddings. Using only expectation values of these low-order observables, EntNet achieves $>98\%$ recall on certified entangled states with near-zero false-positive rates on separable states. Remarkably, it exhibits transferability across system sizes and distinct Hamiltonian families, including frustrated and disordered spin models, with minimal loss of accuracy. Beyond detection, EntNet provides physical insight into entanglement detection by revealing a selective spatial organization of pair correlations, while its finite-temperature decision boundary tracks the excitation gap of Jordan-Wigner quasiparticles. These results establish a transferable and physically interpretable framework for data-driven discovery of entanglement witnesses from local correlation functions.

</details>


### [124] [Parameterized equivalence checking of quantum circuit generators via weighted automata](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12049)
*Pavel Chuklanov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究量子电路生成器的等价性验证问题：在六种等价关系下穷举参数取值，判定生成器与参考实现是否等价，输出反例或带误差界的"相等"结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定规模（固定比特数）的测试无法发现随量子比特数增长的电路错误，需要对所有参数值进行等价性验证；但电路矩阵随比特数指数增长，无法直接比较。

Method: 针对"阶梯型程序"，证明在可计算阈值以上电路矩阵构成一致的矩阵乘积算子（MPO），即可用与量子比特数无关的有限维加权自动机表示；进而将等价性归约为差分自动机的零性判定，并在素数模下进行检验，最终给出反例，或给出精确/带计算误差界的"相等"结论。

Result: 实验表明该方法能发现固定规模检查所遗漏的错误，且未产生任何错误判定。

Conclusion: 借助MPO/加权自动机的有限表示与模素数零性检验，可在参数无穷族上精确验证量子电路生成器的等价性，兼具可判定性与可靠性。

Abstract: A generator builds a quantum circuit from the number of qubits and a bit string. We check a generator against a reference for all parameter values under six equivalence relations. For ladder programs, we prove that above a computable threshold the circuit matrices form a uniform matrix product operator, a weighted automaton of dimension independent of the number of qubits. Equivalence reduces to the zeroness of a difference automaton, checked modulo primes. The answer is a counterexample or "equal", exact or with a computed error bound. Experimentally, the method found errors missed by fixed-size checks and produced no incorrect verdicts.

</details>


### [125] [A Universal Fault-Tolerant Quantum Computing Architecture with Globally Driven Atom Arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12098)
*Zhihao Zhang,Shuaifan Cao,Xinyang Yu,Yanyan Chen,Yueyang Min,Yingzhou Li,Zhongcheng Yu,Lei Feng,Xiaopeng Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出基于全局驱动的双物种原子阵列通用容错架构，以种间里德堡相互作用实现比特寻址，构建原生指令集GRAIL，编译表面码与魔态培育后得到1.43%纠错阈值及魔态逻辑错误率低于10^{-9}。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可扩展量子计算需同时满足可编程性与简单高保真控制，而局域寻址量子门带来繁重的校准负担，因此希望以全局驱动替代局域寻址。

Method: 采用混合双物种中性原子系统，利用种间里德堡相互作用实现原子介导的选择性寻址；定义通用原生指令集GRAIL，将表面码纠错与魔态培育编译为GRAIL程序，并通过并行化编译抑制串扰；在由硬件推断的电路级噪声模型下评估性能。

Result: 串扰误差可抑制至10^{-4}量级；量子存储的纠错阈值为1.43%；在实验可达的里德堡拉比频率下培育的魔态，其逻辑错误率低于10^{-9}。

Conclusion: 以原子介导的选择性替代局域门寻址，消除了相应的校准需求，为大规模容错中性原子计算提供了更简单控制的可行路径。

Abstract: Scalable quantum computation requires reconciling programmability with simple, high-fidelity control. Here, we propose a universal fault-tolerant architecture using globally driven dual-species atom arrays. In a hybrid neutral-atom system, interspecies Rydberg interactions enable qubit addressability without locally addressed quantum gates. We construct a universal native instruction set, Global Rydberg Atom Instruction Language (GRAIL), and show that crosstalk errors can be suppressed to the $10^{-4}$ level. We compile surface-code error correction and magic state cultivation into GRAIL, and parallelize the compilation to suppress crosstalk. Under a hardware-inferred circuit-level noise model, we obtain an error-correction threshold of $1.43\%$ for the resulting quantum memory and a logical error rate below $10^{-9}$ for magic states cultivated at an experimentally accessible Rydberg Rabi frequency. By replacing local gate addressing with atom-mediated selectivity, this architecture removes the associated calibration requirements, offering a route to simpler control for large-scale fault-tolerant neutral-atom computation.

</details>


### [126] [Modular and Looping Quantum Photonic Processing Unit for Quantum Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12126)
*Denis Stanev,Nicolò Spagnolo,Fabio Sciarrino*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文提出一种基于环路（loop-based）结构的模块化通用光子量子计算架构，证明其具有通用性、确定性、与量子线路模型兼容、可重编程并支持自适应测量，并通过数值模拟与机器学习优化基本光学元件验证其可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光子平台被视为实现实用通用量子计算机的有前景方案，已有多种通用光子架构，但各有优劣；现有方案往往物理元件数量多、扩展性与灵活性受限，因此需要一种元件更少、可扩展的架构。

Method: 提出模块化、可扩展的环路型光子量子计算架构，并以数学证明论证其通用性、确定性、与量子线路模型的兼容性、可重编程性及自适应测量能力；同时采用机器学习方法在若干基本线性与非线性光学元件上获取架构所需构建模块，并通过数值模拟在具体实例上演示其运行。

Result: 证明了该架构具备通用性、确定性、线路模型兼容性、可重编程与自适应测量特性；环路结构显著减少了实验实现所需的物理元件数量；数值模拟在具体示例上验证了架构运行，机器学习辅助的元件优化给出了可用的构建模块。

Conclusion: 该架构在灵活性与可扩展性上具优势且元件开销低，结果同时揭示了其优缺点及通往物理实现的后续发展路径。

Abstract: Photonic processors are emerging as promising platforms in the race towards the realization of practical universal quantum computers. Several architectures with universal capabilities have been proposed for their implementation via a photonic platform, each with their own set of benefits and drawbacks. In this work we propose an architecture for a universal photonic quantum computer. The proposed architecture is modular, allowing for a high degree of flexibility and scalability, and relies on a loop-based structure, significantly reducing the number of physical components required to implement it experimentally. We prove that our architecture is universal, deterministic, and compatible with the quantum circuital model, as well as reprogrammable and capable of adaptive measurements. Furthermore, we showcase its functioning via numerical simulation on specific examples, by using machine learning techniques on a set of fundamental linear and nonlinear optical components to obtain some of the building blocks used in the architecture. The obtained results can be used to highlight the benefits and the drawbacks of the proposed architecture, as well as the route for future developments towards its physical implementation.

</details>


### [127] [High Rate Quantum Block Turbo Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12190)
*Khaled Jebari,Luiz Anet Neto,Ramesh Pyndiah,Jean-Louis de Bougrenet de la Tocnaye*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究由不同分量码构成的积形式级联量子分组Turbo码，分析码参数变化及分量码顺序对Turbo译码性能的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子分组Turbo码是积形式级联量子纠错码的迭代译码技术，现有工作需要进一步探索高码率方案，因此本文考察不同分量码组合下的新型高码率量子分组Turbo码。

Method: 以积码方式级联量子纠错码，采用迭代（Turbo）译码；构建由不同分量码组成的高码率量子分组Turbo码，并系统改变码参数与分量码的排列顺序进行对比实验。

Result: 摘要未给出具体数值结果，仅指出考察了码参数变化和分量码顺序对Turbo性能的影响。

Conclusion: 分量码的选择、码参数及分量码顺序均会影响高码率量子分组Turbo码的译码性能，可为该类码的设计提供依据。

Abstract: Quantum Block Turbo Code is an iterative decoding technique for concatenated quantum error correction codes in product form. In this paper, we study new high rate quantum block turbo codes with different constituent codes. We examine the impact of the change of codes parameters and the order of constituent codes on turbo performance.

</details>


### [128] [Testing non-classical gravitational phase dynamics under strong dephasing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12229)
*Tianfeng Feng,Vlatko Vedral*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在退相干的Bose–Marletto–Vedral装置中，即使最终态不出现纠缠，仍可通过对局域which-path退相干固定的精确有限时间判据，区分"引力条件相位演化"与"经典中介诱导的被动随机局域相位模型"（含始终可分的情形）；该区分在推广到保布居经典测量与反馈（对角PPT松弛）时以修正灵敏度依然成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验引力是否必须量子化通常依赖"两质量能否通过引力相互作用产生纠缠"，但弱引力相互作用与环境退相干竞争，可能使最终态无纠缠。作者希望回答：在退相干情形下，动力学的残余信息能否仍用于判别引力的量子性质（引力相位是否相干）。

Method: 针对分离良好的路径，论证主导的牛顿效应是累积相位而非干涉臂间布居转移（用量子波包重叠与偏转估计加以量化，并与物质波引力相位实验相符）；固定独立表征的局域which-path退相干，构造有限时间精确判据，将引力条件相位演化与所有被动随机局域相位模型（由经典中介诱导）区分；再通过对角PPT松弛，把比较扩展到保布居的经典测量与反馈模型。

Result: 证明了引力条件相位演化与任意被动随机局域相位模型之间的精确有限时间区分，包括输入态全程保持可分的区域；该区分在纳入保布居经典测量与反馈（对角PPT松弛）后依然存在，仅灵敏度有所改变。

Conclusion: 在强退相干区域可提出引力相位动力学的可操作检验，但其有效性以明确且物理上合理的经典中介模型为条件；判别不依赖纠缠的产生。

Abstract: Whether gravity must be quantum is often approached by asking whether two masses can become entangled through their mutual gravitational interaction. In practice, the weak interaction competes with environmental decoherence, so the final state may contain no entanglement even when the underlying gravitational phase remains coherent. We investigate what can still be learned from the dynamics in a dephased Bose--Marletto--Vedral setup. The dominant Newtonian effect for well-separated paths is an accumulated phase rather than transfer between interferometer arms, a regime motivated by matter-wave gravitational phase experiments and quantified here by wavepacket-overlap and deflection estimates. Keeping independently characterized local which-path dephasing fixed, we prove an exact finite-time distinction between the gravitational conditional-phase evolution and every passive stochastic local-phase model (induced by a classical mediator), including regimes where the chosen input remains separable at all times. The distinction survives, with a modified sensitivity, when the comparison is enlarged to population-preserving classical measurement and feedback through a diagonal PPT relaxation. These results identify an operational test of gravitational phase dynamics in the strong-dephasing regimes, conditional on explicit and physically motivated classical mediator models.

</details>


### [129] [Commuting-check gauging for fault-tolerant logical measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12236)
*Zongyuan Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文引入"completion qubits"（补全量子比特）使gauge化后的检查算符彼此对易，从而对qubit稳定子码的Clifford ℤ₂对称性实现保距、保LDPC性质的gauge化，并给出容错逻辑测量与晶格手术的构造框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gauge化把逻辑算符变成局域检查的乘积，是实现逻辑测量与晶格手术的途径；但这些检查在码空间外可能互不对易，测量其中一个会随机化另一个的结果，破坏综合征的确定性，也使容错性分析（故障传播、测量轮数）变得困难。

Method: 对qubit稳定子码的Clifford ℤ₂对称性引入"补全量子比特"：每对非对易的检查配置一个补全比特，使检查算符在全局对易且保持Clifford形式；证明该操作保持gauge码空间与码距，并以常数因子比特开销保持LDPC性质。进一步推广到qudit，并在素数维数下给出补全检查在Clifford层级中所处层级的界。基于全局对易性分析无故障轮次固定综合征、以及独立于测量轮数的故障传播界。

Result: 补全后所有检查全局对易，无故障的一轮测量可将综合征固定至下一次故障，并可给出与测量轮数无关的故障传播上界。对qubit码，证明对距离为d的母码进行gauge化再解除gauge化可在O(d)轮内实现电路故障距离为Ω(d)的逻辑测量。并以两个toric code gauge化成D(D₄)量子双模型再返回为例，给出显式电路，其电路故障距离等于母码距离。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个用对易检查实现容错逻辑测量的框架，可应用于经由晶格手术制备魔法态；补全构造在常数开销下保持码距与LDPC性质，并把非对易性带来的随机化问题转化为可控的全局对易结构。

Abstract: Gauging turns a logical operator into a product of local checks, providing a route to logical measurement and lattice surgery. These checks can fail to commute outside the code space, so measuring one can randomize the outcome of another. In this work, we introduce completion qubits that make the checks commute for Clifford $\mathbb{Z}_2$ symmetries of qubit stabilizer codes. Adding one completion qubit per noncommuting pair preserves the gauged code space and its distance while keeping the checks Clifford. Completion also preserves the low-density parity-check property with a constant-factor qubit overhead. We generalize completion to qudits and establish bounds on the Clifford-hierarchy level of the completed checks in prime dimensions. Global commutation ensures that a fault-free round fixes the syndrome until the next fault and allows us to bound fault propagation independently of the number of measurement rounds. For qubit codes, we show that gauging and ungauging a distance-$d$ parent code implements a logical measurement with circuit fault distance $\Omega(d)$ in $O(d)$ rounds. We demonstrate the construction by gauging two toric codes into the $D(D_4)$ quantum double and back, with an explicit circuit whose circuit fault distance equals the parent code distance. Our results provide a framework for fault-tolerant logical measurements using commuting checks, with applications to magic-state preparation by lattice surgery.

</details>


### [130] [Deploying QKD on the fiber links of the National Quantum Communication Infrastructure in Sweden](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12279)
*Giulio Foletto,Mira Abu Yahia,Shreya Gouravaram Navalur,Carlos Brotons Montoro,Hilma Karlsson,Dena Karunianto Wibowo,Pascal Lefebvre,Katia Gallo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文报告瑞典国家量子通信基础设施中光纤链路的表征方法，将可测链路参数与简化的QKD密钥率模型关联，并通过一条链路上为期一个月的成功测试验证了QKD可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欧洲正在部署QKD以评估其实际性能与实用性；部署前需勘察量子信道所用光纤链路的特性，并与QKD设备参数交叉核对，以保证兼容性与最优性能。

Method: 提出光纤链路的表征流程，用于评估其是否适合QKD；建立简化模型，把可测量链路属性映射到影响成码率的关键因素，以抓住QKD性能的本质。

Result: 给出了链路表征与适用性评估的方法学，并通过在一条实际链路上为期一个月的连续测试成功运行QKD，确认其可行性。

Conclusion: 链路表征与设备参数交叉核对是QKD部署前的关键环节；简化模型可有效指导链路筛选，实际链路测试证明QKD在真实光纤环境中可行。

Abstract: Quantum key distribution (QKD) is being deployed across Europe to evaluate its performance and practicality in real-world scenarios. When preparing this deployment, it is important to survey the properties of the fiber links that will be used for the quantum channel, cross-checking them with those of QKD devices, to ensure compatibility and optimal performance. We report here our studies from the Swedish chapter of this endeavor, the National Quantum Communication Infrastructure in Sweden. We focus on procedures to characterize the links and evaluate their suitability for QKD. We relate measurable properties to the key factors that affect the secret key rate using a simplified model that aims at the essence of QKD performance. Finally, we confirm the feasibility of QKD on one of our links by reporting a one-month long successful test.

</details>


### [131] [Calibration-free quantum thermometry with strain-swept two-level defects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12280)
*Asghar ullah,Özgür E. Müstecaplıoğlu,Mehmet Cengiz Onbaşlı*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 约瑟夫森结势垒中的本征二能级缺陷可利用其布居响应形状作为免校准局域温度计；但需同时边缘化读取偏置与对比度，引出一个单参数热测量界所不具备的可辨识性约束——窄扫描下可用信息随扫描宽度四次方标度。以应变扫描缺陷为例，单缺陷拟合精度约0.58%@20 mK。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导量子比特工作在特定温区，需要局域温度监测；而常规测温依赖对读取偏置与对比度的预先校准。作者希望利用结势垒中本征二能级缺陷的布居响应实现免校准（原级）局域测温，并指出在同时处理未知读取参数时，热测量精度受限于可辨识性而非传统Cramér–Rao式单参数界。

Method: 将二能级缺陷的布居响应作为温度信号，对其随应变（压电电压）扫描的响应曲线做拟合；将未知的读取偏置与对比度视为冗余参数并边缘化（贝叶斯/剖面似然），由此推导出可辨识性条件与信息随扫描宽度的四次方标度分析。实验示例：隧穿能Δ0/h=1.3 GHz的已报道缺陷，沿双曲线一支扫描至2.69 GHz（不对称度偏移2.36 GHz，即8 V压电电压），并检验响应是否符合Boltzmann形式与热性。

Result: 窄扫描下，可用费舍尔信息按扫描宽度的四次方增长，故窄扫描在局域上与偏置或对比度变化不可区分，测温失效。对上述缺陷，分析预测20 mK处单缺陷拟合精度为0.58%。同一扫描对Boltzmann形式构成超定约束，可识别显著偏离热平衡的行为，且无需预先校准偏置与对比度即可估计温度。

Conclusion: 本征结缺陷可作为免校准局域温度测量资源；可辨识性应作为原级温度传感的关键设计准则，扫描宽度/激励幅度是决定有效测温信息的设计参数。

Abstract: Superconducting qubits operate within a well-defined temperature range, making local temperature monitoring important. Here we show that intrinsic two-level defects in Josephson-junction barriers can act as local thermometers by exploiting the shape of their population response, without requiring calibration of the readout offset or contrast. Marginalizing over these unknown parameters imposes an identifiability requirement absent from single-parameter thermometric bounds: for narrow sweeps, usable information scales as the fourth power of the sweep width. Hence, a narrow sweep becomes locally indistinguishable from a change in offset or contrast. We illustrate this principle with a strain-swept two-level defect in a Josephson junction. For a reported defect with tunneling energy $\Delta_{0}/h=\SI{1.3}{\giga\hertz}$, swept along one branch of its hyperbola to \SI{2.69}{\giga\hertz} (an asymmetry excursion of \SI{2.36}{\giga\hertz}, or \SI{8}{\volt} of piezo voltage), the analysis projects a single-defect fit precision of \SI{0.58}{\percent} at \SI{20}{\milli\kelvin}. The resulting response enables temperature estimation without prior calibration of the readout offset and contrast, while the same sweep overdetermines the Boltzmann form and identifies significant deviations from thermality. These results establish intrinsic junction defects as a resource for calibration-free local thermometry and highlight identifiability as a key design criterion for primary temperature sensing.

</details>


### [132] [Attainable and unattainable quantum precision limits in ultrastrong light--matter coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12471)
*Wangjun Lu,Qing Yu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究超强耦合光-物质系统中量子精度的规范依赖性：规范间幺正变换携带耦合 $g$，不同截断定义不同族；量子Fisher信息随规范呈精确抛物线，谐振物质在 $\theta_\ast=\Omega/(\Omega+\omega)$ 处取极小，仅最小耦合界可达，不同规范间差异达 9.6 倍且趋势相反，Dicke 点处虚拟激发增益的发散属规范赝像并会饱和。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超强耦合区光-物质系统（如 Rabi/Dicke 模型）中，不同规范（最小耦合规范 vs. 偶极规范）在有限截断下给出不同物理预言，导致量子Fisher信息等精度度量缺乏规范不变性，亟需厘清其来源并辨别哪些结论是规范赝像。

Method: 引入依赖耦合 $g$ 的规范间幺正变换，把不同截断视为不同哈密顿量族；解析计算量子Fisher信息对规范参数的依赖关系，并以谐振子物质模型给出显式结果，分析 Dicke 点处虚拟激发行为。

Result: 量子Fisher信息在规范参数 $\theta$ 上呈精确抛物线；谐振物质下极小点位于普适值 $\theta_\ast=\Omega/(\Omega+\omega)$，仅由物质与腔频率决定；只有最小耦合给出的界可实际达到；不同规范框架间精度相差 9.6 倍且变化趋势相反；Dicke 点处虚拟激发增益的发散并非真实物理效应，而是规范赝像，实际会饱和。

Conclusion: 超强耦合系统中的量子精度估计强依赖规范与截断，须以规范不变的判据区分真实物理与赝像；在谐振物质情形下最小耦合规范可给出可达的最优（最小）精度界，Dicke 点发散应重新解释为饱和的规范效应。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the gauge dependence of quantum precision in ultrastrongly coupled light--matter systems. The unitary between gauges carries the coupling $g$, so each truncation defines a different family. The quantum Fisher information traces an exact parabola across frames. Harmonic matter minimizes it at a universal $\theta_\ast=\Omega/(\Omega+\omega)$, set by the matter and cavity frequencies. Only the minimal-coupling bound is attainable. Frames differ by a factor $9.6$ with opposite trends. Diverging virtual-excitation gain at the Dicke point is a gauge artifact and saturates.

</details>


### [133] [Universal two-qubit control across dilution refrigerators via a 10-km coherent optical link](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12481)
*Chuanlong Ma,Jiajun Chen,Jia-Qi Wang,Zheng-Xu Zhu,Jia-Hua Zou,Linze Li,Dingran Gu,Yuan-Hao Yang,Lintao Xiao,Xiaoxuan Pan,Xin-Biao Xu,Baile Chen,Haifeng Yu,Weiting Wang,Chang-Ling Zou,Luyan Sun*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出一种相干光链路（COL），可在两台稀释制冷机之间传输微波控制波形及其相位参考，跨10 km光纤实现远程超导量子比特的通用门操控与纠缠，Bell态保真度达80.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式量子网络与模块化量子处理器需要跨制冷机的直接经典链路以支持自适应控制，但远距离从独立制冷机控制超导量子处理器此前难以实现。

Method: 构建由激光器、微波—光换能器与光—微波光电二极管组成的相干光链路，在10 km光纤上传输微波控制信号；利用同一激光器衍生的光参考补偿两台设备间的频率失配，向另一制冷机中的transmon比特与腔比特施加通用门集合。

Result: 通过COL成功实现两比特的通用门操控与纠缠，测得Bell态保真度80.6%。

Conclusion: COL为模块化量子处理器提供了可行的经典骨干链路，验证了跨制冷机远程相干控制超导量子器件的可行性。

Abstract: Distributed quantum networks are important for scalable quantum processors and for potential applications in communication, distributed computation, and sensing, in which direct classical links for adaptive control are essential to harness the shared entanglement. However, controlling superconducting quantum processors from a separate refrigerator over long distances remains elusive. Here, we introduce a coherent optical link (COL) that delivers microwave waveforms and their phase references between dilution refrigerators. Combining a laser, a microwave-to-optical transducer and an optical-to-microwave photodiode, the COL transmits microwave control signals over 10~km of fiber, while optical references derived from the same laser compensate for the frequency mismatch between devices. Through the COL, we deliver a universal gate set to a transmon qubit and a cavity qubit in another refrigerator and entangle them, and achieve a Bell-state fidelity of 80.6\%. Quantum control delivered through the COL thus provides a classical backbone for modular quantum processors.

</details>


### [134] [An Alternative to Quantum Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12494)
*Julian Barbour,Maria I. R. Lourenço*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种基于整体性、尺度不变本体论的替代框架，仅假设欧氏空间中的点粒子，用单一尺度不变函数描述由其质量（电荷）比与相互间距比所确定的"形状"，无需动力学演化，试图在不依赖标准量子力学的前提下重构量子理论的实验结论，并统一描述嵌入无限多粒子构型中的有限子系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子理论虽在实验上极为成功，但测量诠释与纠缠关联的本质仍是未决问题；作者希望从一种整体论、尺度不变的本体论出发重新导出量子理论的实验结果，以绕开标准量子力学的诠释困难。

Method: 理论框架构造：仅假设欧氏空间中的点粒子，其状态由质量（与电荷）之比及彼此间距之比刻画；以单一尺度不变函数描述粒子构型的"形状"，不引入动力学演化；将有限子系统视为嵌入无限多粒子整体构型之中进行处理。

Result: 摘要属框架性、纲领性阐述，未给出定量推导或数值结果；作者称该框架在原则上可给出嵌入无限多粒子构型中有限子系统的统一描述，并可能成为导出原子、分子与宏观物体结构的可行途径。

Conclusion: 该文提出一种以尺度不变"形状"为核心的整体论替代方案，主张无需标准量子力学即可有望重现量子理论的经验结果；其可行性仍待具体推导（如原子、分子结构的定量复现）与对纠缠、测量问题的进一步说明来检验。

Abstract: Despite the remarkable empirical success of quantum theory, the interpretation of measurement and the nature of entanglement correlations remain open questions. We outline an alternative framework that seeks to recover the empirical results of quantum theory from a holistic, scale-invariant ontology. Assuming only point particles in Euclidean space, characterised by ratios of masses (and charges) and by the ratios of their mutual separations, the theory describes shapes through a single scale-invariant function; no dynamical evolution is required. In principle, this framework provides a unified description of finite subsystems embedded in a configuration of infinitely many particles and offers a possible route to deriving the observed structures of atoms, molecules and macroscopic bodies without invoking standard quantum mechanics.

</details>


### [135] [Singularising quantum channels preserving kappa-additivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12537)
*Sviatoslav V. Dzhenzher*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 通过关于可测交换群上κ-可加概率测度对酉移位共轭取平均，构造了一类量子信道，其在移位与有界可测乘法算子生成的算子代数对偶上作用，保持量子态的κ-可加性并具弱-*积分表示；若平均测度在单点集上为零，则所有输出态皆为奇异态。附录给出κ-可加测度的Fubini定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典的概率平均（混合）思想推广到量子设定，并以κ-可加测度取代通常的σ-可加框架，以覆盖非σ-有限的测度论情形。

Method: 在可测交换群上取κ-可加概率测度μ，对酉移位共轭映射 U_s(·)U_s^* 关于μ取平均，定义量子信道；研究其在由移位与有界可测乘法算子生成的算子代数之对偶（即态空间）上的作用，并建立弱-*积分表示；附录中证明κ-可加测度的Fubini型定理以支撑交换积分次序。

Result: 所得信道保持量子态的κ-可加性，并在该态类上具有弱-*积分表示；当μ无原子（在每个单点集上取值为零）时，任意输入态的输出态均为奇异态（与参考态相互奇异）。

Conclusion: 该工作把移位平均型信道从σ-可加推广到κ-可加测度框架，给出保持κ-可加性的弱-*积分描述，并刻画了无原子平均测度导致输出态全部奇异的机制。

Abstract: We construct quantum channels by averaging unitary shift conjugations with respect to $\kappa$-additive probability measures on measurable abelian groups.
 The channels act on the duals of operator algebras generated by shifts and bounded measurable multiplication operators.
 They preserve $\kappa$-additivity of quantum states and admit a weak-$*$ integral representation on this class.
 If the averaging measure vanishes on singletons, every output state is singular.
 In the appendix, we also establish the corresponding Fubini's theorem for $\kappa$-additive measures.

</details>


### [136] [All-to-all connectivity via adiabatic ZZ interactions in a superconducting flux qubit processor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12576)
*Elena Lupo,Nguyen H. Le,Eran Ginossar,Michael Stern*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种基带驱动的绝热ZZ门方案：在共腔耦合的磁通量子比特寄存器中，通过本地磁通线施加远低于比特跃迁频率的驱动，绝热倾斜量子化轴以产生可调纵向ZZ耦合，实现任意选定量子比特对的纠缠，数值模拟双比特门误差低于10^-4。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可扩展超导量子处理器通过可调耦合器连接近邻量子比特，带来大量布线与控制开销，且比特连通性受限。作者希望在不依赖近邻耦合器的架构下实现高连通性、低开销的多比特纠缠门。

Method: 系统为经公共腔耦合的磁通量子比特寄存器，比特保持直流偏置在磁通最优点（sweet spot）。通过局部磁通线选择性施加低频（远低于比特跃迁频率）强驱动，绝热地倾斜各比特的量子化轴，从而产生有效纵向ZZ相互作用。耦合强度由驱动幅度与相对相位调控，而非依赖共振条件，因此单次操作即可作用于任意选定比特对。

Result: 数值模拟显示，在较宽的驱动频率范围内相干门误差低于10^-4；在四比特寄存器中同时执行两个门的保真度与单门相当；方案对频率涨落具有鲁棒性，且在许多比特同时耦合到腔的情况下仍然有效。

Conclusion: 该基带驱动绝热ZZ门为共腔磁通比特架构提供了一种高连通、低布线开销、可并行且对噪声鲁棒的两比特纠缠方案，具备良好的可扩展性，可作为替代可调耦合器近邻架构的候选路线。

Abstract: Scalable superconducting quantum processors would benefit from high qubit connectivity, yet existing architectures connect neighbouring qubits via tunable couplers, which introduce substantial wiring and control overhead. Here, we propose a baseband-driven adiabatic ZZ gate that entangles any selected pair in a register of flux qubits coupled via a common cavity. The qubits stay dc-biased at their flux sweet spot. Strong drives applied selectively through local flux lines, at frequencies far below the qubit transitions, adiabatically tilt the quantization axes and generate an effective longitudinal ZZ interaction. The strength of the coupling is tunable and controlled by the amplitudes and relative phase of the drives rather than by any resonance condition. Numerical simulations give coherent gate errors below $10^{-4}$ over broad ranges of drive frequency, with comparable fidelities for two simultaneous gates in a four-qubit register. The protocol is robust against frequency fluctuations and remains effective even when many qubits are coupled to the cavity.

</details>


### [137] [Long-Range Ergotropy Transfer with Weakened Boundary-Coupling Spin Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12589)
*R. C. Krisnanda,M. S. Ukhtary,Abdurrouf*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究量子电池中"功存(ergotropy)"的远距离传输问题，提出以边界弱耦合的自旋链作为中介，利用弱耦合诱导的局域边缘态实现高保真、高功存传输，并系统分析宇称效应、相互作用类型与初始态的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ergotropy 衡量量子电池的最大可提取能量，但当能量提取端与电池不局部（分离）时，必须把 ergotropy 高保真地跨距离传输。常规均匀自旋链在长距离传输中保真度与传输功存衰减严重，需要更有效的传输中介方案。

Method: 以自旋链为传输通道，将链两端的边界耦合减弱，使链出现局域边缘态（能谱中的局域模），从而在给定时间内实现高保真传输；分别考察最近邻相互作用与长程相互作用两种模型；通过分析链的能谱结构解释传输功存的奇偶宇称效应；并研究发送端自旋初始态对 ergotropy 的相干分量与非相干分量传输及平均传输效率的影响。

Result: 减弱边界耦合可产生局域边缘态，实现给定时间窗内的高保真传输；长链情形下传输的 ergotropy 随自旋数呈奇偶交替的宇称效应，并可由能谱结构解释；在最近邻与长程相互作用两种模型中，边界弱耦合相较常规均匀链均提升长链的最大传输 ergotropy；长程相互作用虽降低传输 ergotropy，但缩短传输时间，链越长该优势越明显；初始态会差异化影响相干与非相干分量的传输及平均效率。

Conclusion: 边界弱耦合自旋链是提升长距离 ergotropy 传输的有效方案，其性能提升源于局域边缘态的形成；设计时需在传输功存与传输时间之间权衡（长程相互作用），并考虑链长宇称与发送端初始态的选择。

Abstract: Ergotropy is a key quantity of a quantum battery that measures the maximum extractable energy. When the extraction process is not local, a faithful transfer of ergotropy across distance is necessary. In this work, we investigate such transfer by using spin chains with weakened coupling at the boundaries as mediator. Weakening the coupling at the two boundaries of the chain gives rise to localized edge states allowing high fidelity transfer within given time duration. We found that, for long spin chains, the transferred ergotropy alternates between even and odd number of spins. We explain this parity effect by investigating the chain spectral structure. Furthermore, we consider both the nearest-neighbor and long-range interactions between spin. In both model of interactions, weakening the boundary couplings improves the maximum transferred ergotropy for long spin chain, compared with in the case of conventional uniform chain. Although the long-range interaction reduces the transferred ergotropy, the transfer time is reduced, too, in particular for longer chain. In addition to the total transferred ergotropy, we also investigate the effect of initial states of the sender spin to the transport of the coherent and incoherent components of the total ergotropy, and the average transport efficiency.

</details>


### [138] [Reducing Provers in Distributed Quantum Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12600)
*Alex Della Schiava,Atsuya Hasegawa,François Le Gall,Harumichi Nishimura*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文定义了k个非纠缠证明者的分布式量子Merlin-Arthur模型dQMAS(k)，研究每节点证明大小与减少证明者数目的通信代价，证明可将证明者数从k降至2（证明大小加倍、无额外通信），或降至1（加四轮通信，本地证明大小至多3倍加对数项），并证明完全完备协议下的放大性，得到dQMAS = dQMAS(k)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有dQMA模型关注证明大小与验证通信的权衡，Hasegawa等（PODC 2024）要求证明跨节点非纠缠；本文希望系统刻画多证明者情形（dQMAS(k)），弄清证明者数量与每节点证明大小、通信轮数之间的定量关系，并厘清单证明者与多证明者模型是否等价。

Method: 定义dQMAS(k)为k个跨节点非纠缠证明者的分布式QMA扩展；构造证明者数目归约：k→2时不增加通信、证明大小加倍；k→1时额外四轮通信，本地证明大小不超过原大小3倍加节点数的对数项；同时对完全完备协议建立误差放大（amplification）技术，在常数因子开销下将多证明者归结为单证明者。

Result: (1) 证明者数由k归约为2，证明大小加倍且无额外通信；(2) 加四轮通信可将证明者数由k归约为1，每节点证明大小至多3倍原局部大小加log n项；(3) 完全完备协议下放大成立，单证明者仅需常数因子开销，从而dQMAS = dQMAS(k)。

Conclusion: 多非纠缠证明者模型dQMAS(k)在常数因子证明大小与对数级附加项、至多四轮额外通信下等价于单证明者模型dQMAS，说明分布式量子验证中证明者数量不构成本质资源，代价可控。

Abstract: In the distributed quantum Merlin-Arthur ($\mathsf{dQMA}$) model introduced by Fraigniaud, Le Gall, Nishimura, and Paz (ITCS 2021), a network of quantum computers verifies a global property with the aid of a quantum proof supplied by an untrusted prover. While the nodes are computationally unbounded, the challenge is to limit proof size and the communication required for verification. In this paper, we consider the model of Hasegawa, Kundu, and Nishimura (PODC 2024), where the proof must be unentangled across nodes, and define its extension to $k$ unentangled provers, denoted by $\mathsf{dQMAS}(k)$. In this setting, we investigate the proof size per node and the communication costs of reducing the number of provers.
 Our first result reduces the number of provers from $k$ to two, doubling the proof size without additional communication. Moreover, with four additional rounds, we can reduce the number of provers from $k$ to one, using at most three times the original local proof size plus a logarithmic term in the number of nodes. Finally, we establish amplification for perfectly complete protocols. In this regime, a single prover suffices with a constant-factor overhead in our reductions, yielding $\mathsf{dQMAS} = \mathsf{dQMAS}(k)$.

</details>


### [139] [Effective Time Advance of Quantum Clocks under Postselection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12609)
*Youka Kaku,Akira Matsumura,Tomohiro Fujita*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文表明量子后选择（借助反常弱值）可"反转"双生子佯谬的结论：尽管每条运动分支都经历正常的相对论时间膨胀，后选择后的有效时间却出现超前，并在弱引力场与类氢原子体系中给出可观测表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典双生子佯谬中旅行者因相对论时间膨胀而更年轻，作者希望探究量子相干与后选择这一纯量子资源能否突破/修饰这一由相对论运动学给出的时间膨胀结论。

Method: 构造可解析求解的量子时钟模型：将时钟内部自由度与质心运动纠缠，对质心运动进行后选择；用反常弱值刻画后选择态下的有效时间演化。进一步把分析推广到弱引力场，并考察相干态与类氢原子情形。

Result: 后选择质心运动产生有效时间超前（反常弱值所致），即便每个运动分支仍服从普通时间膨胀；该超前在弱引力场中对简单相干态也成立，并在类氢原子波函数中有可辨识的印记。

Conclusion: 量子干涉与后选择可导致超越常规相对论时间膨胀的有效时间超前，说明时间膨胀在量子测量语境下并非不可改变的结论。

Abstract: In the familiar twin paradox, the traveling twin ages less than the one who stays behind as a consequence of relativistic time dilation. Here, we show that quantum postselection can effectively reverse this outcome. Using an analytically solvable quantum clock model, we demonstrate that postselection of the center-of-mass motion produces an effective time advance through anomalous weak values, even though every individual motional branch undergoes ordinary time dilation. We extend this analysis to a weak gravitational field and show that an effective time advance can arise even for simple coherent states of the quantum clock motion. We further illustrate this effect using a hydrogen-like atom, revealing how it manifests itself in the atomic wavefunction. Our results demonstrate that quantum interference and postselection can produce an effective time advance beyond conventional relativistic time dilation.

</details>


### [140] [Quantum Residual Exploration for Selected Configuration Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12672)
*Kentaro Yamamoto,Ikko Hamamura,Juan W. Pedersen,Duncan Gowland,Gabriel Laude,Shintaro Maekawa,David Muñoz Ramo,Elica Kyoseva,William Dawson*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出模块化量子-经典混合方法，在固定行列式预算下用昆腾测量结果补充并改进选定组态相互作用（SCI）的行列式选取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强关联体系中微扰式的行列式发现可能遗漏多步激发，需在同等行列式预算下提升SCI选型的有效性。

Method: 量子测量产生互补行列式提案，仅在统一能量判据下优于经典候选时被接受，从而与经典SCI流程模块化耦合。

Result: 在Quantinuum Reimei离子阱量子计算机上，对拉伸构型的H12，测得提案在相同最终行列式预算下使变分能量低于纯经典选取。

Conclusion: 量子测量可作为SCI行列式发现的有效补充，针对多步激发改进强关联体系的能量，并在真实硬件上得到验证。

Abstract: We propose a modular hybrid quantum-classical method to improve selected configuration interaction at a fixed final determinant budget. Quantum measurements supply complementary determinants, accepted only when they outrank classical candidates under a common energetic criterion. This targets multistep excitations that perturbative discovery may miss in strongly correlated systems. A demonstration on the Quantinuum Reimei trapped-ion quantum computer shows that measured proposals for H$_{12}$ at a stretched geometry lower the variational energy relative to classical-only selection at the same final determinant budget.

</details>


### [141] [No Exponential Quantum Advantage for Decoded Quantum Interferometry with Local Quantum Decoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12718)
*Brandon Augustino,Shouvanik Chakrabarti,Enrico Fontana,Dylan Herman,Guneykan Ozgul,Ruslan Shaydulin,Shreehari Sureshbabu,Jacob Watkins*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究解码量子干涉（DQI）在随机D-正则k-稀疏实例（k≥3）上的解码阈值，揭示了DQI解码阈值与统计物理中Gibbs测度相变（树重建阈值、凝聚阈值）的对应关系，证明局部解码器受限于树重建阈值（BPQM可达到），而最优解码器（pretty-good measurement）恰在凝聚阈值以下成功，从而给出DQI性能的紧上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DQI将稀疏线性约束问题的优化及Gibbs测度采样归约为从量子态解码线性码。实际常用BPQM等局部解码器，但其性能极限与量子优势是否存在尚不明确，作者希望用统计物理相变理论精确刻画DQI解码的阈值。

Method: 将DQI诱导的解码问题阈值对应到统计物理中已知的Gibbs测度相变：局部解码器对应树重建（tree reconstruction）相变，Max-k-XORSAT情形下的最优解码器对应凝聚（condensation）相变；据此作信息论下界分析与BPQM可达性分析，并结合空腔法预测局部马尔可夫链的快速混合。

Result: (1) 信息论阻碍：任何局部解码器在树重建阈值以上无法高概率恢复码字，而基于BPQM的解码器可达该阈值；因此对仅依赖违反约束数目的振幅函数，DQI配合局部解码器在采样上无指数加速、在近似优化上无优势。(2) 最优解码器（PGM，无已知高效实现）恰在凝聚阈值以下成功，故凝聚阈值给出DQI性能的紧上界，严格改进了Jordan等人由Holevo界得到的结果。

Conclusion: 两阈值之间的高效非局部解码器是这些实例上借助DQI实现量子优势的唯一可行路径。

Abstract: Decoded quantum interferometry (DQI) reduces optimization of sparse linear constraint problems, and sampling from their Gibbs measures, to decoding a linear code from a quantum state. We study DQI on random $D$-regular $k$-sparse instances with $k\ge3$, for which local decoders such as belief propagation with quantum messages (BPQM) are commonly used. Such decoders estimate a typical codeword symbol using only the qudits within logarithmic distance of it. Our main insight is that the thresholds of the decoding problem induced by DQI correspond to phase transitions of the Gibbs measure that are well studied in statistical physics: tree reconstruction for local decoders and, for Max-$k$-XORSAT, condensation for the optimal decoder. This insight allows us to prove two main results. First, we prove an information-theoretic obstruction: no local decoder can recover the codeword with high probability above the tree reconstruction threshold. Conversely, we show that a decoder based on BPQM reaches this threshold. Since the cavity method predicts that local Markov chains equilibrate rapidly up to the same threshold, we expect DQI with local decoders to give no exponential speedup for sampling and no advantage in approximate optimization with amplitude functions that depend only on the number of violated constraints. Second, we consider the optimal decoder (the pretty-good measurement, for which no efficient implementation is known) and show that it succeeds exactly below the condensation threshold. The condensation threshold thus provides a tight upper bound on the performance of DQI with these amplitude functions, providing a strict refinement of the limit that Jordan et al.\ derived from the Holevo bound. Our results point to efficient nonlocal decoders that succeed between the two thresholds as the only viable path to a quantum advantage with DQI on these instances.

</details>


### [142] [Quantum Robustness via Sandwiched Rényi Divergences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12738)
*Luc Rey-Bellet*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究"近似已知的量子态上可观测量期望最多能偏离多少"这一问题：以 sandwiched Rényi 散度作为参考态周围的置信预算，对任意阶 α∈[1/2,∞] 求最坏情形期望，并通过凸对偶给出单维优化刻画，最终证明该量子问题可"经典化"为可观测量谱区间上的一个概率测度问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
