Motivation: 量子态通常只能被近似制备或估计，因此需要量化"态不确定性"对可观测量期望值的最大影响。已有工作多针对特定散度（如保真度、熵风险）给出界，缺少对所有 Rényi 阶统一处理、并能直接继承经典集中不等式的框架。

Method: 以 sandwiched Rényi 散度为预算约束，取最坏情形期望为目标，利用凸对偶将其转化为关于 sandwiched Schatten 范数的一维优化；提出经典化定理：构造 Heinävaara 迹联合谱测度，在可观测量谱区间上得到概率测度 ω_α（α=1 时由 Stahl 定理给出），使量子问题与对应经典问题等价；再由 Hoeffding、Bernstein、Markov–Krein 界推出 Pinsker 型不等式及 Bernstein 型精化。

Result: 最坏情形期望随阶数呈连续谱：α=1/2 时为保真度风险，α=1 时为熵风险，α=∞ 时为量子条件风险价值（CVaR）。经典集中不等式可整体移植到量子情形，并给出带 Bernstein 型修正的 Pinsker 型不等式。ω_α 仅在 α=1/2 时等于 Born 测度；当 α>1/2 时，可观测量与态的非对易性使方差严格低于 Born 方差，从而削弱所有对方差敏感的界，即"量子紧缩（quantum deflation）"效应。

Conclusion: 该工作为 Rényi 散度预算下的量子最坏情形期望提供了统一的对偶刻画与经典化桥梁，使经典概率工具可直接用于量子误差分析，并揭示非对易性带来的方差收缩这一新颖量子效应。

Abstract: If a quantum state is known only approximately, how much can the expectation of an observable change? We answer with the worst-case expectation over all states within a sandwiched Rényi divergence budget of a reference state, for every order $\alpha \in [1/2,\infty]$. Convex duality gives this value as a one-dimensional optimization over a sandwiched Schatten norm, running from a fidelity risk at $\alpha = 1/2$, through the entropic risk at $\alpha = 1$, to a quantum conditional value-at-risk at $\alpha = \infty$. The main result is a classicalization theorem: the quantum problem coincides with the classical one for a probability measure $\omega_\alpha$ on the spectral interval of the observable, constructed from Heinävaara's tracial joint spectral measure and given by Stahl's theorem at $\alpha = 1$. The Hoeffding, Bernstein and Markov--Krein bounds of classical probability thereby apply to the quantum problem, and yield Pinsker-type inequalities with Bernstein-type refinements. The measure $\omega_\alpha$ is the Born measure only at $\alpha = 1/2$; for $\alpha > 1/2$, noncommutativity of the observable with the state strictly lowers its variance below the Born variance, and with it every variance-sensitive bound: a quantum deflation.

</details>


### [143] [$\mathsf{EQP}\subseteq\mathsf{IP}$ Does Not Relativize](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12746)
*Sinan Oral,Supartha Podder*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 构造递归神谕 $O$ 与一元语言 $L \in \mathsf{EQP}^O \setminus (\mathsf{IP}^O \cup \mathsf{PH}^O)$，将 Bouland 等人的 $\mathsf{BQP}$ vs $\mathsf{IP}$ 神谕分离强化到精确量子计算（EQP）层面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有 $\mathsf{BQP}$ 相对 $\mathsf{IP}$ 的神谕分离（Bouland 等，基于 Forrelation 问题），但 EQP 层面的分离尚属空白。本文旨在把分离从有界误差量子计算强化到零误差（精确）量子计算，并同时覆盖 $\mathsf{IP}$ 与 $\mathsf{PH}$ 两类经典上界。

Method: 采用 Forrelation 问题的变体：承诺一个布尔函数与另一布尔函数的 Fourier 变换的归一化相关值恰为 $+1/2$ 或 $-1/2$。核心技术贡献是一种"修复（repair）"方法，构造支撑在这些精确相关水平上的分布，同时保持 $\mathsf{IP}$ 与 $\mathsf{PH}$ 下界所需的不可区分性。均匀多项式时间量子算法仅用三次神谕查询即以确定性区分两种情况。

Result: 存在递归神谕 $O$ 与一元语言 $L$，使得 $L \in \mathsf{EQP}^O$ 但 $L \notin \mathsf{IP}^O \cup \mathsf{PH}^O$；量子侧由三次查询、零错误概率的均匀量子算法实现。

Conclusion: 该工作将 Forrelation 型神谕分离从 $\mathsf{BQP}$ 提升至 $\mathsf{EQP}$，表明精确量子计算相对 $\mathsf{IP}$ 与 $\mathsf{PH}$ 亦具有本质优势；其分布修复技术为在精确相关水平上保持下界不可区分性提供了通用工具。

Abstract: We construct a recursive oracle $O$ and a unary language $L\in\mathsf{EQP}^O\setminus(\mathsf{IP}^O\cup\mathsf{PH}^O)$, strengthening the $\mathsf{BQP}$ versus $\mathsf{IP}$ oracle separation of Bouland et al. to exact quantum computation. The separation is based on a variant of the Forrelation problem in which the normalized correlation of one Boolean function with the Fourier transform of another is promised to be exactly $+1/2$ or $-1/2$. The main technical contribution is a repair method that constructs distributions supported on these exact levels while preserving the indistinguishability needed for the $\mathsf{IP}$ and $\mathsf{PH}$ lower bounds. A uniform polynomial-time quantum algorithm distinguishes the two cases with certainty using only three oracle queries.

</details>


### [144] [Sequential vs parallel Strategies for Sharing Quantum Advantage in Communication Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12753)
*Satyaki Manna,Arindam Mitra,Sumit Mukherjee*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究量子信息在“并行共享”与“顺序共享”两种策略下的一致性：两观察者时二者统计等价，三及以上观察者时并行可实现的部分统计无法用顺序策略复现；并行策略在含两个解码测量的通信任务中无法共享量子优势，且该不可行性在三解码测量的3-bit RAC中仍然成立，但在3-bit奇偶无感RAC中可以被突破。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子系统中的信息可被可持续地提取，使同一系统可反复用于后续交互。作者希望厘清两种实验可行的多方信息提取路线——并行共享与顺序共享——在统计层面是否等价，以及量子优势能否、在何种任务结构下通过并行共享延续下去。

Method: 以“统计可实现性/可复现性”为框架，比较并行与顺序共享策略可产生的统计集合；分析仅含两个解码测量的一类通信任务中并行共享量子优势的不可行性，并进一步考察含三个解码测量的3-bit随机存取码（RAC）与3-bit奇偶无感RAC，其中观察者对初始量子系统的两个非理想克隆进行测量。

Result: (1) 仅涉及两个观察者时，并行共享策略产生的任意统计都可由顺序策略复现；(2) 观察者多于两个时，并行可实现的某些统计无法被顺序策略复现；(3) 对含两个解码测量的通信任务，并行共享量子优势不可能，顺序与并行在量子优势可持续性上存在任意大的差距；(4) 并行共享量子优势在3-bit RAC中仍不可能，但在3-bit奇偶无感RAC中变得可能，此时至多两个观察者可同时在两个非理想克隆上测量并获得量子优势。

Conclusion: 并行与顺序共享并非普遍等价：二者的一致性仅限于两观察者情形，多观察者下出现本质差别。量子优势的并行可持续性强烈依赖于通信任务的结构（解码测量数目与是否奇偶无感），不可行性并非普遍规律。这些结论对量子通信网络与分布式量子信息处理具有潜在应用价值。

Abstract: Information can be extracted from a quantum system in a sustainable manner, allowing the system to be reused for further information extraction through subsequent interactions. We investigate the possibility of consensus between two experimentally feasible methods, namely, parallel and sequential sharing, that enable multiple observers to extract information encoded in a quantum system. We show that any statistics generated by a parallel sharing strategy can be reproduced by a sequential strategy when only two observers are involved. However, when more than two observers participate in sharing quantum correlations, certain statistics achievable through parallel strategies cannot be reproduced by sequential strategies. Moreover, we demonstrate that sharing quantum advantage through a parallel strategy is impossible for a class of communication tasks involving two decoding measurements, thereby establishing an arbitrarily large gap between the sustainability of quantum advantage under sequential and parallel strategies. This naturally raises the question of whether this impossibility extends to communication tasks involving more than two decoding measurements. We show that, while parallel sharing of quantum advantage is impossible in a 3-bit random access code (RAC), it becomes possible in a 3-bit parity-oblivious RAC, where at most two observers can simultaneously obtain a quantum advantage by performing measurements on two imperfect clones of the initially prepared quantum system. Our findings have potential applications in the development of quantum communication networks and distributed quantum information processing.

</details>


### [145] [Succinct Infinite-Horizon Lyapunov and LQR Value Problems Are BQP-Complete](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12757)
*Mohamed Serry,Jun Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出并研究“简洁Lyapunov值问题”(SLV)与“简洁LQR值问题”(SLQRV)：系统矩阵稀疏、由布尔电路以对数尺寸简洁表示。作者证明SLV为BQP-难，并给出SLQRV的多项式时间量子算法，从而二者均为BQP-完全，除非BPP=BQP，否则不存在经典随机多项式算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性系统分析与控制中的基本任务（自治系统的无限时域输出能量、受控系统的最优值函数）分别由Lyapunov方程与代数Riccati方程刻画。经典算法在维度n上为多项式时间，但当矩阵以简洁（对数长度）方式描述时，输入规模远小于n，经典方法不再可行。作者希望刻画这类简洁输入下问题的计算复杂度，并探讨量子算法能否带来指数级加速。

Method: 将系统矩阵设为稀疏并以布尔电路指定非零元，输入长度为系统维度对数的多项式。针对耗散自治系统与坐标向量初始态定义SLV的promise问题；将其推广到线性二次最优控制得到SLQRV。通过从BQP问题（量子线路模拟）到SLV的多项式时间归约证明其BQP-难；并构造在简洁输入长度上多项式时间运行的量子算法求解SLQRV，结合SLV为SLQRV特例得到完全性结论。

Result: SLV是BQP-难的：任意BQP问题可多项式时间归约到它。更一般的SLQRV存在运行时间为简洁输入长度多项式的量子算法。由于SLV是SLQRV的特例，两问题均为BQP-完全。因此除非BPP=BQP，任何经典随机算法都无法在所有promise实例上以简洁输入长度的多项式时间求解。

Conclusion: 在矩阵简洁表示的设定下，Lyapunov值与LQR最优值的评估具有本质量子复杂性（BQP-完全），揭示了简洁线性系统分析与控制中经典与量子计算能力的分离；这为量子算法在控制与系统分析中的应用提供了复杂度理论依据。

Abstract: Fundamental tasks in linear systems analysis and control require evaluating infinite-horizon output energies for autonomous systems and optimal value functions for controlled systems. These quantities are characterized by Lyapunov and algebraic Riccati equations, respectively. In this work, we study promise problems for their evaluation when the system matrices are sparse and represented succinctly by Boolean circuits specifying their nonzero entries, with input length polynomial in the logarithm of the system dimension. For dissipative autonomous systems with coordinate-vector initial states, we define the succinct Lyapunov value problem (SLV). Its extension to linear-quadratic optimal control is the succinct LQR value problem (SLQRV). We prove that SLV is $\BQP$-hard: every problem in bounded-error quantum polynomial time ($\BQP$) reduces to it in polynomial time. We then establish that the more general SLQRV problem admits a quantum algorithm running in time polynomial in the succinct input length. Since SLV is a special case of SLQRV, both problems are $\BQP$-complete. Consequently, unless bounded-error probabilistic polynomial time coincides with $\BQP$, no classical randomized algorithm can solve either problem on all promised instances in time polynomial in the succinct input length.

</details>


### [146] [An efficient classical algorithm for OTOCs in chaotic quantum dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12773)
*Yunchao Liu,Yihui Quek,Shreya Vardhan,Tianci Zhou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出并实现了一个多项式时间的经典算法，可在典型一维量子线路实例中估计四点OTOC^(1)和八点OTOC^(2)，表明混沌敏感区间的OTOC在平均情形下经典可解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在随机量子线路的单个实例中估计OTOC被视为可验证量子优势的候选方案，且已被证明对张量网络、蒙特卡洛等传统经典算法困难。作者希望检验这一"量子优势"命题在典型实例与平均情形下是否成立，并利用量子混沌的洞察寻找经典可解性。

Method: 借助量子混沌的洞见，将OTOC组织为快速收敛的级数：主导项来自复制希尔伯特空间中置换自由度内部的历史，次主导项来自对该置换子空间的偏离。混沌引起的退相干效应压制了这些次主导项，从而使级数可截断。作者给出理论与数值证据支持该压制现象，并在实际实现中评估算法精度与信噪比。

Result: 算法在一维线路中以高精度估计OTOC^(1)与OTOC^(2)；在较大线路深度下，其信噪比高于Google近期二维实验。理论上证明并在数值上验证了混沌导致次主导项被指数压制，使得平均情形下OTOC估计为经典可行。

Conclusion: 混沌虽常被视为经典模拟的不可逾越障碍，但本文表明其引发的退相干反而可被利用，使一维典型线路的OTOC估计在平均情形下经典可解，从而削弱了基于OTOC的量子优势论证。将算法推广到更高维度、厘清二维OTOC中量子优势的地位，仍为开放问题。

Abstract: Estimating out-of-time-ordered correlators (OTOCs) in individual instances of random quantum circuits has been proposed as a candidate for verifiable quantum advantage, and demonstrated to be hard for conventional classical algorithms such as tensor networks and Monte Carlo. Using insights from quantum chaos, we develop and implement a polynomial-time classical algorithm for the four-point ${\rm OTOC}^{(1)}$ and the eight-point ${\rm OTOC}^{(2)}$ in typical instances of one-dimensional (1D) quantum circuits. Our results demonstrate average-case classical tractability of estimating OTOCs in the sensitive regime where circuit-to-circuit fluctuations are large. Our algorithm organizes the OTOC into a rapidly converging series, where the leading term includes contributions from histories within permutation degrees of freedom in the replicated Hilbert space, and subleading terms arise from excursions outside this space. Although chaos is often viewed as an insurmountable barrier to classical simulation, our algorithm exploits a form of dephasing caused by chaos, which suppresses the subleading terms. We provide strong theoretical and numerical evidence to support this suppression. Further, we demonstrate that practical implementations of our algorithm estimate ${\rm OTOC}^{(1)}$ and ${\rm OTOC}^{(2)}$ in 1D circuits with high accuracy, achieving higher signal-to-noise ratio at larger circuit depths compared to recent 2D experiments by Google. Extending our algorithm to higher dimensions, and resolving the status of quantum advantage in 2D OTOCs, remain open problems.

</details>


### [147] [Unified architecture for arbitrary two-qubit gates with neutral atoms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12779)
*Nader Mostaan,Peter Schmelcher,Rick Mukherjee*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出基于可调各向异性XXZ相互作用加单比特操作的统一架构，在中性原子平台上原生实现完整的两比特门集合，包括B、QFT、iSWAP、√iSWAP及CZ到iSWAP的连续门族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中性原子平台缺乏通用的两比特门资源，通常只能依赖固定的CZ类门，导致编译时需大量分解、电路深度与编译开销增大；作者希望用单一可调资源解锁全部两比特门，实现指令集灵活切换。

Method: 以可调各向异性的XXZ相互作用为核心资源，仅辅以单比特操作；给出里德堡原子实现可调XXZ相互作用的方案，并结合最优控制的全局微波脉冲来实现各类门。

Result: 成功实现B、QFT、iSWAP、√iSWAP门，以及从CZ经B门连续插值到iSWAP的连续门族。

Conclusion: 该架构使中性原子平台可原生实现任意两比特门，支持在指令集间灵活切换，从而降低电路深度与编译开销，提升量子算法实现效率。

Abstract: We propose a unified architecture for two-qubit gates in neutral-atom quantum processors which allows the implementation of the complete set of two-qubit gates. Our approach rests on a single resource that unlocks the entire gate set: an XXZ interaction with tunable anisotropy, combined only with single-qubit operations. We introduce a Rydberg-atom realization of the tunable XXZ interaction and combine it with optimally controlled global microwave pulses to implement the B, QFT, iSWAP, and $\sqrt{\mathrm{iSWAP}}$ gates, as well as a continuous gate family interpolating from CZ to iSWAP via the B gate. This work allows the implementation of any two-qubit gate natively in neutral-atom platforms, enabling flexible switching between instruction sets to reduce circuit depth and compilation overhead in quantum algorithm implementation.

</details>


### [148] [Self-Energy Construction for Chain Representations of Open Quantum Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12782)
*Pengju Chen,Da-Wei Luo,Ting Yu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 将自能视为环境对系统的响应，其在系统—环境截断位置平移下协变变换；该变换存在不动点，定义渐近边界自能（BSE），其谱密度为 Wigner 半圆；由此构造链延拓 BSE、伪双曲自能距离，用于与截断无关的误差度量与更优拟合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半无限链环境在数值处理中必须截断，但截断位置的选取会改变所表示的环境响应，导致闭包误差与链深误差混杂、结果依赖截断位置，且时间域关联函数拟合不够准确。作者希望建立一个与截断位置无关的、几何化的环境响应描述框架。

Method: 1）把自能解释为环境响应，并证明其在截断平移下按协变方式变换；2）对渐近均匀参数的半无限链求该变换的不动点，得到渐近边界自能 BSE，并给出其谱密度为 Wigner 半圆；3）利用保留链段的趋势构造链延拓 BSE 以提升环境表征精度；4）由该变换引入在截断平移下不变的伪双曲自能距离，作为比较环境响应的自然几何；5）以有限阻尼振子闭包为例，用该几何给出与截断无关的误差度量，分离闭包误差与链深误差，并设计拟合方案。

Result: BSE 的谱密度确为 Wigner 半圆；链延拓 BSE 在表征环境上精度更高；伪双曲自能距离在截断平移下不变，可给出与截断无关的有限阻尼振子闭包误差度量，并能将闭包误差与链深误差区分开；基于该几何的拟合方案优于直接拟合时间域关联函数。

Conclusion: 自能的协变变换性质为半无限链环境提供了统一且与截断无关的描述：不动点给出普适（半圆谱）的渐近 BSE，链延拓提升精度，伪双曲距离提供自然的比较几何与误差分解工具，从而改进开放系统的环境闭包与拟合。

Abstract: We identify the self-energy as the environmental response to the system, which transforms covariantly under shifts of the system--environment cut. For a semi-infinite chain with asymptotically uniform parameters, this transformation admits a fixed point that defines the asymptotic boundary self-energy (BSE), whose spectral density is the Wigner semicircle. A chain-extended BSE exploits the trend of the retained chain, offering improved accuracy in representing the environment. The transformation introduces a pseudo-hyperbolic self-energy distance that is invariant under shifts of the cut, providing a natural geometry for comparing environmental responses. As a demonstration, this geometry enables cut-independent error measure of finite damped-oscillator closures of the chain, distinguishing the closure error from the chain depth error, and offers a more accurate fitting scheme than fitting the time-domain correlation function.

</details>


### [149] [Quantum Simulation of Quantum Channels and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12784)
*Lúcio S. Fassarella*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 基于Choi-Kraus算符和表示与Stinespring膨胀，提出在数字量子计算机上模拟量子通道的统一策略，并给出混合态制备、POVM测量与马尔可夫演化三类应用的显式实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子通道是开放量子系统与量子信息处理的核心描述工具，但将其在门级数字量子计算机上统一、显式地实现仍缺乏系统梳理；同时传统马尔可夫动力学模拟依赖Trotter分解，需要替代方案。

Method: 以Choi-Kraus算符和表示与Stinespring膨胀为理论框架，构建量子通道模拟的统一方法；应用于混合态制备、POVM测量、马尔可夫演化三类场景；引入"剩余算符"（residual operator），由Liouvillian构造量子通道作为量子动力学半群的一阶近似，从而以类似欧拉法的方式模拟动力学而避免Trotter化；除Choi-Kraus定理外全部理论结果均给出证明。

Result: 以显式例子展示了三类应用，并重新导出混合态纯化、POVM对应的Naimark膨胀等基本概念；剩余算符方案实现了一阶精度的动力学模拟；所有模拟使用IBM Qiskit SDK的AerSimulator完成，代码已在GitHub公开。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个自洽、可实现的量子通道模拟教学式与实用框架，将纯化、Naimark膨胀与马尔可夫动力学纳入同一形式体系，并给出避免Trotter化的欧拉型模拟路径，便于在现有量子SDK上直接复现。

Abstract: We review the basic theory of quantum channels and develop a strategy for simulating them on digital quantum computers, drawing upon the Choi-Kraus operator-sum representation and Stinespring dilation to provide a unified approach for various applications. We discuss three types of applications, offering explicit examples for each: the preparation of mixed states, measurements described by positive operator-valued measures (POVMs), and the simulation of the evolution of Markovian quantum systems. We recover fundamental concepts from quantum theory, such as the purification of a mixed state and the Naimark dilation associated with a POVM. Regarding the simulation of the evolution of a Markovian quantum system, we introduce an operator, termed the residual operator, to construct, from the system's Liouvillian, a quantum channel that serves as a first-order approximation of the quantum dynamical semigroup. This allows the simulation of quantum dynamics to be performed in a manner analogous to the Euler method for ordinary differential equations, avoiding Trotterization. We prove all the theoretical results mentioned, with the exception of the fundamental Choi-Kraus theorem concerning the operator-sum representation of quantum channels, for which we provide the appropriate references. The explicit simulations discussed were implemented using the AerSimulator from IBM's Qiskit SDK, with the corresponding code available on GitHub.

</details>


### [150] [A short proof of the Aaronson-Ambainis Conjecture via the completion framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12795)
*Thomas Huffstutler,David Miloschewsky,Supartha Podder*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文给出Aaronson-Ambainis猜想的一个简短证明：对定义在δ比例输入上的部分布尔函数f，存在确定性算法用O(Q(f)^6/(εδ)^2)次查询在至少1−ε比例的定义域上正确计算f，并把该结论推广到以经典敌手度量为参数的界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Aaronson-Ambainis猜想断言量子查询复杂度低的函数可以被确定性算法在大部分定义域上以少量查询近似计算，是量子查询复杂度与经典多项式方法之间的重要桥梁，但此前缺乏简洁证明。

Method: 采用completion framework（补全框架），建立以经典敌手度量（classical adversary measure）表述的更一般界；仅使用Turturean近期证明中证书不等式的特例，并给出该特例的自包含证明。

Result: 得到短证明：任意0<ε<1，确定性算法以O(Q(f)^6/(εδ)^2)次查询在至少1−ε比例的定义域上正确计算f；附录中由主结果推出更强变体——有界多项式影响猜想（bounded-polynomial influence conjecture）。

Conclusion: Aaronson-Ambainis猜想成立，且证明更简洁、结论更一般（以经典敌手度量为参数），并蕴含其更强的有界多项式影响猜想变体。

Abstract: We give a short proof of the Aaronson-Ambainis conjecture. If a partial Boolean function $f$ is defined on a $\delta$-fraction of all $n$-bit strings and have quantum query complexity $Q(f)$, then, for every $0<\epsilon<1$, a deterministic algorithm computes $f$ correctly on at least a $1-\epsilon$ fraction of its domain using $O( Q(f)^6 / (\epsilon \delta)^2)$ queries. Our proof uses the completion framework and establishes a more general bound in terms of the classical adversary measure. The only result we use from Turturean's recent proof is a special case of his certificate inequality, for which we provide a self-contained proof.
 We also derive the stronger variant of the Aaronson-Ambainis Conjecture, the bounded-polynomial influence conjecture, from our main result in the appendix.

</details>


### [151] [GARI++: A General-Purpose Decoder for Quantum Error Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12833)
*Arshpreet Singh Maan,Francisco Garcia-Herrero,Daniel Bascones,Valentin Savin,Alexandru Paler*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: GARI++ 是一个两阶段解码流水线，在无需系综（ensembles）的条件下，于表面码、双变量自行车码和颜色码上达到与 SOTA 相当的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子纠错（QEC）码需要高精度且可实时实现的解码器。现有方法往往依赖系综训练或在大码距下延迟过高，难以兼顾精度与实时性。

Method: 两阶段流水线：第一阶段为 GARI（在 GARI 变换矩阵上随机串行调度的归一化 min-sum 解码器）；仅当第一阶段不收敛时启用第二阶段，其解码器依据所选的 QEC 码可配置。第一阶段向第二阶段传递残余 Syndrome 与软信息。流水线是预测式的，即选择预期能在第二阶段带来最佳精度的第一阶段迭代输出。整体面向 FPGA-CPU 混合架构的实时解码。

Result: 在物理错误率 0.001 的均匀线路级去极化噪声下，GARI 的 FPGA 实现在码距 7–25 时每轮平均延迟为 201 ns 至 11.8 μs；由于第一阶段输出的残余权重极低，在单核 Apple M2 上配合 Union-Find，每轮平均残余解码时间近似恒定且低于 1 μs（码距可达 51），精度与相关 MWPM 相当。

Conclusion: GARI++ 通过两阶段预测式流水线与混合 FPGA-CPU 架构，在多种 QEC 码上实现 SOTA 级精度并满足实时解码的低延迟需求。

Abstract: We present GARI++, a two-stage decoding pipeline achieving accuracy competitive with the state of the art across surface, bivariate bicycle and color codes without relying on ensembles. The first stage uses GARI, a normalized min-sum decoder with random serial scheduling on the GARI transformed matrix. The second stage decoder is used only for the cases that the first stage does not converge, and the decoder is configurable based on the choice of the QEC code. The first stage pipelines towards the second stage both the residual syndrome and the soft information. GARI++ pipelining is predictive, meaning that GARI++ chooses the first stage iteration outputs which are expected to deliver the best accuracy in the second stage. GARI++ motivates a hybrid FPGA-CPU architecture for real-time decoding. For example, under uniform circuit-level depolarising noise with a physical error rate of 0.001, the FPGA implementation of GARI achieves average latencies per round ranging from $201 ns$ to $11.8\mu s$ for surface code distances between 7 and 25. At the same time, due to the very low weight residuals output by the first stage, the average residual decoding time per round remains near constant and below one microsecond on a single Apple M2 core for surface code distances even up to 51 when using Union-Find and accuracy on par with correlated MWPM.

</details>


### [152] [The hidden symmetry operators of the asymmetric quantum Rabi model and its two-photon and two-mode variants in closed form](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12859)
*Marcus Kollar,Arka Dutta*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文给出非对称量子Rabi模型隐藏对称算子$J$对任意整数偏置$N$的闭合表达式：$J$等于对称模型宇称算符乘以$N$个一阶算子的continuant（有序三对角行列式），并证明其可推广至非对称双光子和双模模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非对称量子Rabi模型在偏置$\epsilon$为模式频率整数倍$N$时出现能级交叉，其根源是隐藏对称算子$J$，但此前对一般$N$没有通用公式，只能个别构造低阶情形。

Method: 以归纳法证明两条对易关系：将$J$写成对称模型宇称与$N$个一阶算子的continuant之积，这些一阶算子为中间偏置$N-1,N-3,\dots,1-N$处哈密顿量减去共同数算符；并利用$\mathfrak{su}(1,1)$生成元把同一结构移植到非对称双光子与双模模型；最后将闭合公式与$J$系数的已知递推关系对应，给出$\Delta$最低阶项。

Result: 对每个$N$得到$J$的闭合形式；几何上表现为$N$级偏置台阶的"阶梯"，恰在整数偏置处闭合到模型的镜像；同一continuant对双光子和双模模型给出任意阶对称算子（此前仅知最低阶）。

Conclusion: 隐藏对称$J$具有统一、显式的代数结构，可系统解释整数偏置处的能级交叉，并为相关多光子/双模模型提供可推广的对称算子构造框架。

Abstract: The asymmetric quantum Rabi model, a two-level system with splitting $\Delta$ and bias $\epsilon$ coupled with strength $g$ to a boson mode, has level crossings when $\epsilon$ is an integer multiple $N$ of the mode frequency. They are due to a hidden symmetry operator $J$, for which no general formula has been available. Here we obtain $J$ in closed form for every $N$. We find that $J$ is given by the parity of the symmetric model times the continuant (ordered tridiagonal determinant) of $N$ first-order operators, which are the Hamiltonians at the intermediate biases $N-1,N-3,\dots,1-N$ minus a common number operator. The symmetry is thus a staircase of $N$ bias steps that closes on the mirror image of the model precisely at integer bias. The proof uses induction on two commutation relations. The same relations hold in the asymmetric two-photon and two-mode models, written with the generators of $\mathfrak{su}(1,1)$, so that the same continuant gives their symmetry operators for every order, previously known only for the lowest ones. We also relate the closed formula for $J$ to the known recursion relations for its coefficients and give their terms of lowest order in $\Delta$ explicitly.

</details>


### [153] [The simulability frontiers of photonic quantum computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12876)
*Jean Senellart,Benjamin Stott,Stephen Wein,Esperanza Cuenca Gomez,Pascale Senellart,Shane Mansfield,Pierre-Emmanuel Emeriau*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文提出以"经典计算机从器件精确输出分布中抽取一个样本所需时间"作为统一的可模拟性度量，并发布开源GPU精确采样器库 photonic-engines，在单块NVIDIA H100上测得单光子、纠缠光子、压缩光等情形下的可模拟性边界（frontier）分别约为38.3、31.0和41.5个光子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前光子器件"超越经典模拟"的阈值估计难以比较：即便是单光子玻色采样，文献给出的阈值也从50到98个光子不等，原因在于各自依赖的任务与假设不同。近似采样的代价取决于所需精度，因而缺乏普适标准；作者希望建立一个对所有光态都同等适用的统一定义。

Method: 1) 以"精确采样一个样本的时间（精确到浮点舍入误差）"作为共同单位，定义可模拟性边界为最快的精确采样器在固定机器上达到每秒一个样本时对应的器件规模；2) 分析标度：每样本时间随探测光子数指数增长，故机器速度翻倍仅使边界移动约一个光子，而算法改进则改变增长率；3) 构建开源GPU库 photonic-engines，包含针对单光子、量子发射器纠缠光子、真空-单光子叠加态及压缩光的精确采样器；4) 每个采样器均与不共享代码的独立模拟器做交叉验证；5) 设定基准条件：Haar随机干涉仪、模式数为光子输入数的两倍、光子数分辨探测器。

Result: 在单块NVIDIA H100 GPU上，边界为38.3个单光子和31.0个来自单一发射器的纠缠光子；对压缩光，基于实测拟合的计时模型给出41.5个平均光子数为0.5的压缩光输入。相干性本身即增加模拟难度：当真空与单光子以概率1/2非相干混合（如有损单光子）时边界达64.2，而处于相干叠加时仅为50.0。对最多三个模式进行中间电路测量并前馈，仅使单光子边界从38.3降至38.1。

Conclusion: 精确采样时间提供了一个跨平台、跨光态可比较的"量子优势"标尺，其边界随硬件与算法演进而移动，可解释文献中阈值差异巨大的现象；相干叠加比非相干混合更难模拟，而有限规模的前馈对边界影响甚微。

Abstract: Estimates of when photonic devices outgrow classical simulation are hard to compare: even for single-photon boson sampling, quoted thresholds range from 50 to 98 photons, as they rest on different tasks and assumptions. We propose as a common unit the time a classical computer needs to draw one sample from the device's exact output distribution, up to floating-point rounding. Only exactness applies equally to every kind of light, since the cost of approximate sampling depends on the accuracy required. The simulability frontier is the device size at which our fastest exact sampler produces one sample per second on a fixed machine; this device size increases with better hardware and algorithms. As the time per sample grows exponentially with the number of detected photons, doubling the machine speed moves the frontier by about one photon, whereas a better algorithm changes the growth rate. We measure the frontier with photonic-engines, an open-source GPU library of exact samplers for single photons, entangled photons from quantum emitters, vacuum-single-photon superpositions and squeezed light. Each sampler is validated against a simulator sharing no code with it. On one NVIDIA H100 GPU, with Haar-random interferometers, twice as many modes as photonic inputs and photon-number-resolving detectors, the frontier lies at 38.3 single photons and at 31.0 entangled photons from one emitter. For squeezed light, a timing model fitted to measurements places it at 41.5 squeezed photonic inputs of mean photon number 0.5. For our samplers, coherence alone makes simulation harder: sources that emit a photon with probability one half reach 64.2 when vacuum and photon are mixed incoherently, as for lossy single photons, but only 50.0 when they are in coherent superposition. Feed-forward from mid-circuit measurement of up to three modes lowers the single-photon frontier only from 38.3 to 38.1.

</details>


### [154] [Chiral waveguide model for error-correctable quantum computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12882)
*Zixiang Hong*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文建立从微观波导散射到量子纠错与算法性能的第一性原理框架：对偏振编码单光子与手性波导中两能级原子系综相互作用，通过约化频率自由度导出等效偏振量子信道，以此为旋转平面码的噪声模型，分析阈值随原子数$N$与耦合强度$\Gamma$的标度（$\propto N\Gamma/\sigma$），发现对称无损耗原子情形下噪声强烈偏向$X$错误，并将其用于量子相位估计的电路设计以提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子纠错与算法层的研究通常依赖抽象噪声模型，而物理层（如波导量子电动力学中的光子散射）产生的噪声结构未被定量地贯通到编码与算法层面。作者希望建立第一性原理链条，说明微观散射参数如何决定有效量子信道、纠错阈值以及算法级表现，并利用噪声偏置指导电路设计。

Method: 1）构造偏振编码单光子与手性波导中两能级原子系综相互作用的散射模型；2）对频率自由度求迹，得到仅作用于偏振的等效量子信道作为物理噪声模型；3）以该信道作为旋转平面码的噪声模型，通过改变$N$与$\Gamma$数值研究阈值行为；4）对微观散射模型作渐近分析，导出阈值随$N\Gamma/\sigma$（$\sigma$为光子谱宽）的标度；5）分析对称实耦合、共振、无损耗原子条件下噪声的$X$偏置；6）将逻辑错误注入量子相位估计，比较不同实现方式。

Result: 旋转平面码在$N$与$\Gamma$变化下呈现阈值行为，阈值标度由$N\Gamma/\sigma$刻画，并由散射模型的渐近分析给出解释。在相同、无损耗、对称实耦合且光子谱中心位于共振的条件下，有效噪声强烈偏向$X$错误。将该偏置引入量子相位估计后，采用Hadamard基实现可降低对主导错误的敏感性，从而改善算法性能，表明噪声偏置可用于指导电路设计。

Conclusion: 微观波导散射可被系统地映射为偏振量子信道并直接决定纠错阈值；噪声偏置是由物理参数（$N$、$\Gamma$、$\sigma$、失谐与损耗）控制的可利用资源，而非仅为不利因素。通过匹配算法基与主导错误方向（如Hadamard基实现），可在纠错之后进一步提升算法层性能，体现了从器件物理到容错算法的端到端协同设计思路。

Abstract: We develop a first-principles framework connecting microscopic waveguide scattering to quantum error correction and algorithmic performance. For polarization-encoded single photons interacting with an ensemble of two-level atoms in a chiral waveguide, we derive an effective polarization quantum channel by tracing out the frequency degree of freedom. Using this channel as the physical noise model for a rotated planar code, we observe threshold behavior as the number of atoms $N$ and the atom-waveguide coupling strength $\Gamma$ are varied. An asymptotic analysis of the microscopic scattering model explains the threshold scaling with $N\Gamma/\sigma$, where $\sigma$ is the photon spectral bandwidth. For identical, lossless atoms with symmetric real coupling and a photon spectrum centered at resonance, the effective noise is strongly biased toward $X$ errors. We then incorporate the resulting logical errors into quantum phase estimation and demonstrate that the noise bias can guide circuit design. In particular, a Hadamard-basis implementation reduces sensitivity to the dominant error, improving algorithmic performance.

</details>


### [155] [Dephasing-Induced Melting of Crystalline Spectral Form Factors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12888)
*Preethi Gopalakrishnan,Adolfo del Campo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究开放系统中能量退相干对长程谱序（表现为谱形状因子在Heisenberg时间整数倍处的Bragg峰）的影响及其可控性，发现Bragg峰随复活阶数与退相干强度按幂律衰减 K_Bragg(p) ∝ (γp)^{-1/2}，并在极强退相干下仍能存活至最近邻相干寿命。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程谱序通常通过谱形状因子中的Bragg峰来探测，但以往研究多集中于封闭（近晶体）谱；开放系统中能量退相干如何破坏这种谱序、以及能否加以调控尚不清楚，这是本文的核心动机。

Method: 采用谱形状因子作为探针，在开放量子系统框架下引入能量退相干（速率γ），分析Bragg峰幅度随复活阶数p与退相干强度的标度行为，并借助谱涨落的时间关联刻画其演化。

Result: 在广泛的中等参数区间内，Bragg峰幅度按代数规律衰减，满足 K_Bragg(p) ∝ (γp)^{-1/2}；即便退相干速率极大，峰仍可存续至最近邻相干的寿命尺度。

Conclusion: 谱形状因子可作为开放量子系统中谱序的有效探针；谱涨落中的时间关联提供了一种控制谱序的手段。

Abstract: Long-range spectral order can be probed using spectral form factors, where it manifests itself as sharp Bragg peaks at integer multiples of the Heisenberg time in nearly crystalline spectra. We examine how energy dephasing in open systems affects this spectral order and how to control it. We find that Bragg peaks fade algebraically with revival order and dephasing strength, $K_{\rm Bragg}(p)\propto(\gamma p)^{-1/2}$, within an extensive intermediate regime, and persist up to the lifetime of nearest-neighbor coherences despite an extreme decoherence rate. Our results establish the spectral form factor as a probe of spectral order in open quantum systems and identify temporal correlations in spectral fluctuations as a means of controlling it.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [156] [Fast Accurate Quantum Monte Carlo without Metropolis Adjustment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10187)
*Reuben Cohn-Gordon,Gabriel Pescia,Sumner N. Hearth,Jakob Robnik,Uroš Seljak,Peter Lunts*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将计算统计中的“未调整”HMC（不做Metropolis-Hastings校正）引入量子蒙特卡洛，在高维自旋-费米子量子相变模型上验证其在每次梯度调用有效样本量上优于调整型HMC，同时保持结果精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子系统的物理量可写成概率分布的期望值，从而用MCMC（量子蒙特卡洛）估计；其中HMC因利用哈密顿动力学设计马尔可夫核而被广泛使用。通常采用经Metropolis-Hastings校正的HMC以保证无偏收敛，但近期计算统计研究表明未调整HMC效率更高，且其渐近偏差相对有限样本方差误差可忽略。作者希望检验该结论是否适用于QMC场景，特别是高维、难采样的自旋-费米子量子相变模型。

Method: 将未调整HMC（不施加MH接受/拒绝校正的哈密顿蒙特卡洛核）适配到量子蒙特卡洛框架中，在具有量子相变的高维自旋-费米子模型上进行数值实验，并与标准调整型HMC进行对比，评价指标为每次梯度调用的有效样本量（ESS per gradient call）及结果准确性。

Result: 实验显示未调整HMC在每次梯度调用的有效样本量上优于调整型HMC，且估计结果保持准确；即其引入的渐近偏差相对于有限样本方差误差较小，未损害预测可靠性。

Conclusion: 在QMC（尤其是高维自旋-费米子模型）中，舍弃MH校正的未调整HMC可带来更高采样效率，是调整型HMC的有力替代方案；但需注意其存在可控的小渐近偏差，应结合误差预算谨慎使用。

Abstract: In quantum systems, physical quantities of interest can be expressed as expectation values of a probability distribution, which allows their estimation by Markov Chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) methods. This approach, an instance of Quantum Monte Carlo (QMC), plays a central role in obtaining predictions of system properties relevant to condensed matter and high energy physics. The use of Hamiltonian dynamics to design a Markov kernel, known as Hybrid or Hamiltonian Monte Carlo (HMC), is widespread in quantum applications. In this context, it is standard to use an HMC kernel that is ``adjusted'' with the Metropolis-Hastings (MH) criterion, so that estimates of expectations converge to their true value in the limit of infinite sample size. However, recent work in computational statistics suggests that an \emph{unadjusted} HMC kernel can be significantly more efficient, while safely maintaining an asymptotic bias which is small relative to the error arising from the variance of the finite sample size. We adapt this approach to the QMC setting, focusing on a challenging and high-dimensional \emph{spin-fermion} model of a quantum phase transition. We find that unadjusted methods outperform adjusted HMC in terms of effective sample size per gradient call, while retaining accurate results.

</details>


### [157] [On the universal structure in deterministic generative diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10338)
*Defang Chen,Zhenyu Zhou,Can Wang,Zeyu Zheng,Siwei Lyu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文指出扩散模型的确定性采样轨迹具有与数据模态和模型实现无关的普适几何结构，并可由基于最优线性去噪器的可精确求解模型基本解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散生成模型已成为跨领域模拟高维结构化数据的通用框架，但对其生成机制的理解仍不充分，而这类理解是原则性地改进训练与推理的前提。作者希望揭示采样过程背后是否存在与模态、实现无关的内在规律。

Method: 从确定性采样（概率流ODE类轨迹）出发，实证考察不同数据模态与不同扩散模型实现下的采样轨迹几何形态，并构造一个以最优线性去噪器（Wiener型去噪器）为核心的精确可解模型来刻画该结构。

Result: 确定性采样轨迹共享统一的几何结构，且该结构在很大程度上能被最优线性去噪器给出的可解模型所解释，说明轨迹形态主要由线性去噪项而非网络细节决定。

Conclusion: 确定性扩散采样存在普适的几何规律，为统一理解扩散生成机制、简化建模分析以及指导训练与采样设计提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Diffusion-based generative modeling has emerged as a general computational framework for simulating high-dimensional, structured data across various domains. Understanding its generative mechanisms is paramount for advancing model training and inference in a principled way. Here, we demonstrate that deterministic sampling trajectories share a universal geometric structure invariant to data modalities and diffusion model implementations. This structure can be largely explained by an exactly solvable model based on the optimal linear denoiser.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [158] [Solution Size Growth as an Obstacle to Iterative Refinement for Linear Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11419)
*Henry Graven,Giacomo Nannicini*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究迭代精化（iterative refinement）应用于线性规划时解规模（solution size，即比特表示长度）的增长问题，构造了一类问题实例，使得解的规模随精化迭代次数呈指数增长；并证明若使用带Bland规则的单纯形法，这种指数增长确实发生，从而说明黑盒LP求解器必须"幸运"（返回优于终止容差所要求质量的解）才能避免该现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 迭代精化能指数级提升解的精度，但对运行时间多项式依赖于解规模的算法（如量子乘性权重更新算法、量子矩阵博弈算法），解规模的膨胀会直接导致复杂度恶化。此前缺乏对迭代精化序列所产生的解规模增长规律的刻画，因此需要给出下界分析。

Method: 构造一类线性规划问题族，分析迭代精化序列中各子问题的解比特规模；形式化定义"幸运"（lucky）求解器概念——即返回质量优于其终止容差所要求的解；并考察单单纯形法配合Bland主元规则的自然实例化在该问题族上的行为，给出解规模的下界。

Result: 证明了存在使解规模随精化迭代次数指数增长的问题类；表明为规避此行为，黑盒LP求解器须"幸运"；并证明采用Bland规则的单单纯形法在该实例上确实呈现指数级规模增长。

Conclusion: 解规模的指数下界对运行时间多项式依赖于解规模的优化算法（如量子乘性权重更新、量子矩阵博弈算法）具有直接影响，说明这些算法的复杂度分析不能简单依赖迭代精化带来的精度提升，需重新审视其规模依赖假设。

Abstract: Iterative refinement is a technique used to exponentially boost the precision of the solution to certain problems by solving a sequence of related problem instances. We study the size of the solutions arising from the sequence of problems produced by the iterative refinement algorithm when applied to linear optimization problems (LPs). We introduce a class of problems for which the size of such solutions can grow exponentially with the number of refinement iterations, and we show evidence that to avoid this undesirable behavior, a black-box LP solver would have to be \emph{lucky}---a concept introduced formally in this paper, by which we mean that the solver would have to return a solution of better quality than what is required by its termination tolerances. Furthermore, we show that the exponential scaling occurs if one uses a natural instantiation of the simplex algorithm using Bland's pivoting rule. We discuss the implications of our lower bound on the solution size for optimization algorithms whose running time depends polynomially on the solution size, such as quantum multiplicative weights update algorithms and quantum matrix games algorithms.

</details>

### [159] [Integer electron-count cuts in variational second-order reduced-density-matrix theory. II. A convergent hierarchy of rigorous relaxations for real-space pair-density representability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11707)
*Maho Nakata*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 该文基于点过程可实现性理论，把整数电子数条件嵌入一个收敛的有限占据线性规划（LP）松弛层级，用于给定单体密度下实空间对密度的计算；每一层都包含全部可实现对密度，嵌套细化下逐层收紧，其交集刻画了可延拓为对称 N 粒子概率测度的对密度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变分二阶约化密度矩阵计算存在使分子解离为分数电荷片段的失效模式；Part I 通过在固定划分上施加整数电子数条件基本消除了该失效，但该做法仍需被纳入严格的、可收敛的松弛框架。

Method: 借助点过程可实现性理论，在空间划分上定义有限占据线性规划（LP）层级，作为整数计数条件的一族严格松弛；在固定轨道基下给出对全组态相互作用能量的单调递增下界；分析 LP 最优值的度量意义（等于粗粒化候选与可实现集之间的 L1 距离），并由原始解构造可实现的近似、由对偶解得到被违反的有效不等式，用作分子计算中的割平面。

Result: 各层包含所有可实现对密度，嵌套细化下单调收紧，交集刻画可延拓的对密度；固定轨道基下 LP 层级给出单调递增的 FCI 能量下界，且即使对库仑排斥也不必达到该下界；LP 最优值随细化收敛到候选者的 L1 距离且粗粒化误差趋于零；Part I 的自旋求和实空间割二维胞元成员；存在满足包括 T1、T2 在内的全部三粒子正性条件、但通过高斯轨道给出的光滑对密度违反整数计数的矩数据；对分数电子数原子，带整数计数条件的系综松弛比零方差固定 N 形式更接近精确的分段线性能量。

Conclusion: 整数电子数条件提供了统一且收敛的松弛层级，既推广了 Part I 的两胞割，又揭示常用正性条件（含 T1、T2）不足以蕴含整数计数，并在系综情形给出更接近精确能量的结果。

Abstract: Variational second-order reduced-density-matrix calculations can dissociate molecules into fractionally charged fragments; Part I largely removed this failure with integer electron-count conditions on fixed partitions. Building on the realizability theory of point processes, we place these conditions in a convergent hierarchy of rigorous relaxations, defined by finite occupation linear programs (LPs) on spatial partitions, for real-space pair densities at fixed one-particle density. Every level contains all representable pair densities, the levels tighten under nested refinement, and their intersection characterizes pair densities admitting a symmetric $N$-particle probability-measure extension. In a fixed orbital basis the levels give monotonically increasing lower bounds to the full configuration-interaction energy, which they need not reach, even for the Coulomb repulsion. The spin-summed real-space cuts of Part I are its two-cell members. The LP optimum is also a measure: at fixed one-particle density it equals the $L^1$ distance of the coarse-grained candidate from representability and increases under refinement to that of the candidate, with a vanishing coarse-graining error; its primal law constructs a representable approximation, and its dual a violated valid inequality, used as a cut in molecular calculations. The count conditions are not implied by the usual positivity conditions: moment data that satisfy all three-particle positivity conditions, including $T_1$ and $T_2$, give through Gaussian orbitals a smooth pair density that violates an integer count. For atoms at fractional electron number, ensemble relaxations with the integer-count condition lie closer to the exact piecewise-linear energy than the zero-variance fixed-$N$ form.

</details>

### [160] [Hyperuniformity in active matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10605)
*Hartmut Löwen,Erdal C. Oğuz*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该文评述了Lei等人(2019)将超均匀性与活性物质联系起来的工作：相互作用的自驱动圆 swimmers 可自组织形成无序超均匀结构，并梳理由此衍生的活性物质超均匀性研究进展与展望。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超均匀系统与活性物质原属两个基本独立的研究领域；Lei等人(2019)首次建立二者联系，作者认为有必要系统评述该工作的影响与后续发展。

Method: 采用综述/观点文章（perspective）形式，围绕"无序超均匀体系长波密度涨落异常抑制"的物理特征与"自驱动活性粒子"的研究现状，剖析Lei等人成果的桥梁作用，并以文献梳理方式归纳后续在活性物质中发现的超均匀性实例。

Result: Lei等人证明相互作用的圆 swimmers 可自发组织成无序超均匀结构；受此启发，研究者已在多种活性物质体系中识别出超均匀性现象，形成一个新的交叉研究方向。

Conclusion: 活性物质中的超均匀性已成为重要研究主题，作者对该领域与超均匀性物理的进一步交叉联系给出了简要展望。

Abstract: Disordered hyperuniform systems are characterized by an anomalously strong suppression of long-wavelength density fluctuations. As a result, these unusual materials behave as though virtually incompressible, even though the particle arrangement itself can remain fully disordered at short range. This peculiar state of matter has been the topic of intense research in physics and mathematics in recent years. Another flourishing research arena is active matter, composed of self-propelled agents that convert energy into motion. While the two fields remained largely disjunct for a long time, the paper by Lei and coworkers (2019) created a link between hyperuniformity and active matter by demonstrating that interacting circle swimmers self-organize into disordered hyperuniform structures. Inspired by this work, many other examples of hyperuniformity in active matter have been identified. We shall describe the impact and outgrowth of this development, and give a brief perspective on further links between hyperuniformity and active matter.

</details>


### [161] [Odd Darcy Law: Odd Viscosity and Edge Currents in Porous Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11138)
*Raphael Maire,Ignacio Pagonabarraga*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文推导并数值验证了手性活性流体在多孔介质中的“奇Darcy定律”：反对称渗透率张量可诱发垂直于压力梯度的横向流动，其来源为流体的奇粘度或边缘流与惯性的非线性耦合，并揭示横向输运对孔隙率、边界摩擦与手性的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Darcy定律以对称渗透率张量描述渗流，无法刻画手性活性流体在多孔介质中可能出现的横向（垂直压力梯度）输运；澄清其微观机制与调控因素具有必要性。

Method: 理论推导结合数值验证：构建含反对称渗透率张量的奇Darcy本构关系，分别考察奇粘度机制与边缘流–惯性非线性耦合机制，并进行参数扫描。

Result: 确认存在垂直于压力梯度的横向流动；识别出两条独立产生奇响应的机制（流体奇粘度、边缘流与惯性的非线性耦合）；给出横向输运随孔隙率、边界摩擦和手性强度的变化规律。

Conclusion: 手性活性流体在多孔介质中的渗流需用含反对称渗透率张量的推广Darcy定律描述；奇响应可由奇粘度或边缘流–惯性耦合产生，且其强度可通过孔隙结构、边界条件和手性加以调控。

Abstract: We derive and numerically validate an odd Darcy law for chiral active fluids in porous media, in which an antisymmetric permeability tensor induces a flow transverse to the applied pressure gradient. We identify two distinct mechanisms leading to this odd response: odd viscosity in the fluid or the nonlinear coupling of edge currents to inertia. We then show how the transverse transport depends on porosity, boundary friction, and chirality.

</details>

### [162] [Linear response in generalized Lotka--Volterra systems with a finite number of species](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12074)
*Koya Katayama,Sosuke Ito*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文不假设随机性，对任意有限物种数的Jacobian矩阵的数值域与谱给出严格几何界，将长期/短期稳定性与丰度加权相互作用的对称部分联系、将最大振荡频率与反对称部分联系，并在海洋食物网数据上显示其优于失效的RMT预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机矩阵理论(RMT)被广泛用于预测大型随机生态系统的扰动响应，但其预测可能无法准确刻画单个有限生态系统，需要不依赖随机性假设的、适用于任意有限物种数的严格约束。

Method: 推导Jacobian矩阵数值域(num erical range)与谱的严格几何界，不假设随机性；将长期稳定性与短期稳定性关联到丰度加权相互作用的对称分量，将最大振荡频率受反对称分量约束；再将界应用于随机集合，用标准RMT参数给出集合平均的约束；最后用海洋食物网经验数据检验。

Result: 界在RMT参数下给出长期稳定性、短期稳定性与最大振荡频率集合平均的约束；在海洋食物网数据上，即使RMT预测失效，该界仍能对短期稳定性和最大振荡频率给出有信息量的约束。

Conclusion: 研究建立了局部生态系统响应的结构性约束，与RMT针对大型随机生态系统的统计性预测相辅相成。

Abstract: Understanding how species interactions shape ecosystem responses to perturbations is a central challenge in ecology. Random matrix theory (RMT) has been widely used to predict the responses of large random ecosystems, but its predictions may not accurately describe individual finite ecosystems. Here, we derive rigorous geometric bounds on the numerical ranges and spectra of Jacobian matrices that are valid for any finite number of species without assuming randomness. These bounds link long-term stability and short-term stability to the symmetric component of abundance-weighted interactions, and constrain the maximum oscillation frequency by the skew-symmetric component. Applied to random ensembles, the bounds also yield constraints on ensemble averages of long-term stability, short-term stability, and the maximum oscillation frequency in terms of standard RMT parameters. Using empirical data from marine food webs, we show that our bounds provide informative constraints on short-term stability and the maximum oscillation frequency even where RMT-based predictions fail. These results establish structural constraints on local ecosystem responses that complement RMT-based statistical predictions for large random ecosystems.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.mtrl-sci'></div>

# cond-mat.mtrl-sci [[Back]](#toc)

### [163] [Temperature-Driven Evolution of Magnetization Dynamics in Cobalt-Doped Fe$_5$GeTe$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10225)
*Sourabh Singh,Soumik Aon,Andrew F. May,Joost Ridderbos,Ramanathan Mahendiran,Wilfred G. van der Wiel*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文用宽带铁磁共振研究了钴掺杂Fe5GeTe2(CFGT)单晶的温度依赖磁化动力学：室温下表现为单一宽共振，有效g因子约2.45、有效Gilbert阻尼约0.15；约200 K以下出现第二支共振并演化为双模响应，持续至25 K，而静态磁化无明显对应异常。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金属性范德华铁磁体的磁化动力学是理解其磁相互作用、评估高频自旋电子学应用潜力的关键；化学替代对这类材料磁性的影响值得探究。

Method: 对Co掺杂Fe5GeTe2(CFGT)单晶进行宽带铁磁共振(FMR)谱测量，作为温度的函数；拟合共振场与线宽随频率的变化，提取有效g因子、有效Gilbert阻尼及有效磁各向异性。

Result: 高温区由单一宽共振主导，降温时向低场移动；室温附近g_eff≈2.45，α_eff≈0.15；有效各向异性随温度强烈演化，200 K附近易面贡献增强。约200 K以下出现额外共振并发展为双模响应，一直持续到25 K；两支在共振场与线宽的温度依赖上表现不同，且新模式的出现并未在静态磁化中伴随同等的显著异常。

Conclusion: CFGT中存在一个体磁强计难以察觉的动力学转变；FMR对化学替代范德华铁磁体的磁行为具有高灵敏度。结果有助于理解该类材料的磁相互作用并评估其高频自旋电子学应用前景。

Abstract: Understanding magnetization dynamics in metallic van der Waals ferromagnets is essential to uncover their magnetic interactions and evaluate their potential for high-frequency spintronics applications. In this study, we investigate the temperature-dependent magnetization dynamics of a single crystal of cobalt-doped $Fe_{5}GeTe_{2}$ (CFGT) using broadband ferromagnetic resonance (FMR) spectroscopy. At high temperatures, the FMR response is dominated by a single broad resonance that shifts toward lower magnetic fields upon cooling. Fits to the frequency dependence of the resonance field and linewidth yield an effective $g$ factor of approximately 2.45 and an effective Gilbert damping parameter of approximately 0.15 near room temperature. The effective magnetic anisotropy evolves strongly with temperature, with an increasingly pronounced easy-plane contribution upon cooling toward 200 K. Below approximately 200 K, an additional resonance emerges and evolves into a distinct two-mode response that persists to the lowest measured temperature of 25 K. The two branches exhibit different temperature dependences of their resonance fields and linewidths, and the emergence of the additional mode is not accompanied by a comparably pronounced anomaly in the static magnetization. These results reveal a dynamical crossover in CFGT that is not readily apparent from bulk magnetometry and highlight the sensitivity of FMR to the magnetic behavior of chemically substituted van der Waals ferromagnets.

</details>


### [164] [Effect of Correlation on the Pressure-Induced Insulator to Metal Transition in Complex Spin-Orbit Coupled Double Perovskite La$_2$CoIr$_{1-x}$Ti$_x$O$_6$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11253)
*Vineeta Yadav,Sromona Nandi,Rudra Sekhar Manna*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文利用DFT+U研究电子关联(U)与静水压对双钙钛矿La2CoIr1-xTixO6 (x=0, 0.5, 1)电子性质的影响：增大U使带隙单调增大而结构变化甚微；加压使带隙闭合，三组分分别在约39、42、53 GPa发生绝缘体-金属转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双钙钛矿中过渡金属d电子的强关联与晶格压缩是调控绝缘-金属态的两个独立自由度，但针对La2CoIr1-xTixO6体系中Ti替代与关联强度、压力协同作用的定量认识尚不清楚，故需系统考察U和静水压对带隙与结构的调控规律。

Method: 采用密度泛函理论(DFT)第一性原理电子结构计算，在环境压力下系统扫描在位库仑相互作用U（同时设U_Co与U_Ir）以考察其对带隙及晶格参数的影响；并对x=0、0.5、1三个组分施加静水压，计算带隙随压力的演化以确定带隙闭合的临界压力。

Result: 随U增大，三个组分的带隙均显著增大，而结构参数仅有轻微变化；带隙在环境压力下分别于约39 GPa（x=0，U_Co=5 eV、U_Ir=2 eV）、42 GPa（x=0.5，U_Co=5 eV、U_Ir=2 eV）和53 GPa（x=1，U_Co=4 eV）闭合，表现为压力诱导的绝缘体-金属转变。

Conclusion: 电子关联增强使体系更趋绝缘，静水压则通过带隙闭合稳定金属态，二者对电子性质呈相反调控方向；表明关联氧化物可通过关联强度与压力实现电子态的可调设计，为材料设计提供依据。

Abstract: We study the effect of electron correlation ($U$) and hydrostatic pressure on the electronic properties of double perovskites La$_2$CoIr$_{1-x}$Ti$_x$O$_6$ for $x$ = 0, 0.5, and 1, using first-principles electronic structure calculations within density functional theory (DFT). At ambient pressure, we systematically vary the electron correlation, i.e., on-site Coulomb interaction to study its effect on band gap and structural parameters. Increasing the value of $U$ enhances the band gap of all three compositions while producing only a slight variation in structural parameters. The effect of hydrostatic pressure is also studied for the same compositions. The estimated band gap values at ambient pressure closes at approximately 39, 42, and 53 GPa for $x$ = 0 (for $U_\mathrm{Co}$ = 5 eV and $U_\mathrm{Ir}$ = 2 eV), 0.5 (for $U_\mathrm{Co}$ = 5 eV and $U_\mathrm{Ir}$ = 2 eV), and 1 (for $U_\mathrm{Co}$ = 4 eV), respectively, indicating pressure-induced insulator-to-metal transitions. These results show that electron correlation stabilises the insulating state, whereas hydrostatic pressure favours the metallic state by closing the band gap. These findings highlight the tunability of correlated oxides through electron correlations and pressure, offering insights for materials design.

</details>


### [165] [Engineering ferroelectric nanocapacitors through interfacial phonon bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11256)
*Iñigo Robredo-Magro,Urmimala Dey,Suehyun park,Karam Cho,Raseong Kim,Jorge Íñiguez-González*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 论文提出"界面声子偏置"这一新概念，即在保持常规弹性与电学边界条件不变的前提下，通过电极的八面体倾转（声子能量景观）来调控相邻铁电超薄膜的相稳定性，并借助机器学习原子间势与分子动力学模拟在BiFeO3纳米电容器中验证了该机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 钙钛矿氧化物纳米电容器中，外延应变与静电屏蔽已是公认的控制参量；电极的晶格动力学状态虽被认为有助于超薄铁电膜的外延稳定，却从未被单独作为独立调控自由度加以研究。作者希望将这一被忽视的界面耦合通道显式分离出来并加以利用。

Method: 采用第一性原理精度的机器学习原子间势（ML-IAP）结合分子动力学模拟，研究对象为与金属性钙钛矿氧化物电极接触的对称BiFeO3纳米电容器；在固定常规弹性与电学边界条件的同时，独立调节电极八面体倾转景观的取向与幅度。

Result: 电极的声子能量景观可改变BiFeO3竞争相的相对稳定性，具体表现为可移动铁电转变温度，甚至在环境条件下诱导出类反铁电的翻转行为。

Conclusion: 界面声子偏置是一种与应变、屏蔽互补且强有力的策略，可用于修饰与优化超薄外延薄膜的性质，为纳米尺度铁电器件的界面工程设计提供了新的自由度。

Abstract: As ferroelectric devices approach the nanometer scale, their behavior becomes increasingly sensitive to boundary conditions. In perovskite oxide nanocapacitors, epitaxial strain and electrostatic screening are well-established control parameters. Then, while expected to contribute to the epitaxial stabilization of ferroelectric ultrathin films, the lattice-dynamical state of the electrodes has not been isolated as an independent tuning knob. Here we call this poorly explored coupling interfacial phonon bias, whereby the electrodes' phonon energy landscape reshapes that of the adjacent ferroelectric across their shared interface. Using first-principles-accurate machine-learned interatomic potentials and molecular-dynamics simulations, we examine this mechanism in symmetric BiFeO${}_3$ nanocapacitors in contact with a metallic perovskite oxide. By independently controlling the orientatio and amplitude of the electrodes' octahedral-tilt landscape, while keeping conventional elastic and electric boundary conditions fixed, we show that the electrodes' phonon energy landscape can modify the stability of competing BiFeO${}_3$ phases. In particular, this allows us to shift the ferroelectric transition temperature and even induce antiferroelectric-like switching at ambient conditions. We thus showcase interfacial phonon bias as a complementary and powerful strategy to modify and optimize the properties of ultrathin epitaxial films.

</details>


### [166] [Orbital texture of valence band and interband transitions of the type-II multiferroic van der Waals NiI$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11425)
*A. De Vita,O. E. Giehl,T. Mukherjee,M. Wolf,L. Rettig,R. Ernstorfer,T. Pincelli,L. T. Lloyd*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文利用时间分辨与角分辨光电子能谱（tr-ARPES）结合第一性原理计算，系统表征了块体NiI₂的能带结构及最低价带到导带的跃迁，明确了Ni与I轨道的贡献、价带的主要动量分辨特征及导带的浅极小值，为后续理论与应用研究提供基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NiI₂是范德华半导体，具有复杂的电子与磁相，在功能器件方面有应用前景；但对其能带结构与元激发缺乏细致认识，而这对于理解其电子与输运性质至关重要。

Method: 时间分辨与角分辨光电子能谱（tr-ARPES）实验测量，辅以第一性原理计算进行对照与指认。

Result: 给出了Ni与I轨道流形的轨道特征，识别出价带的动量分辨主要特征以及导带的浅极小值，并描述了最低价带—导带跃迁。

Conclusion: 该研究为NiI₂的电子结构及单粒子带间电子激发提供了基础性认识，可作为理论模型的检验基准，并为该材料的后续研究提供框架。

Abstract: NiI$_2$ is a van der Waals semiconductor characterized by complex electronic and magnetic phases, holding promise towards functional applications. For this purpose, a detailed understanding of its band structure and its excitations is essential to elucidate the electronic and transport properties. We present a comprehensive description of the band structure and of the lowest valence-to-conduction band transition in bulk NiI$_2$, by means of time- and angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy supported by first-principles calculations. We describe the orbital character of Ni and I manifolds, and identify the main momentum-resolved features of the valence band and the shallow minima of the conduction band. With this study, we provide a foundational understanding of the electronic structure of NiI$_2$ and its single-particle interband electronic excitation, a benchmark for theoretical models, and a framework for future investigations on this material.

</details>


### [167] [Giant gas permeance through a nanoporous crystalline 2D polymer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11518)
*Jonathan D'Emidio,Daniel J. Murray,William B. Thompson,Jarvis C. Humlick,K. C. Schwab,Adrian Del Maestro,Benjamin T. King*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 报道了一分子厚、悬空的纳米多孔二维聚合物（poly(carboxyfantrip)）膜的气体输运测量：其孔隙尺寸均一、孔面积占表面近四分之一，归一化渗透率接近泄流极限，比气体筛分石墨烯膜高3–4个数量级，且符合Graham定律、趋近无相互作用弹道输运极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 化学分离是工业能耗的最大来源之一，而许多分离过程依赖多孔介质中的选择性输运。传统自上而下加工难以实现原子级精确的孔结构，因此需要借助二维材料构建原子级精确孔隙，通过提高通量来提升分离性能。

Method: 制备并悬空一分子厚的纳米多孔二维聚合物poly(carboxyfantrip)膜（孔尺寸等同、纳米级、孔面密度接近表面四分之一），测量H₂、He、N₂、CO₂、SF₆五种分子量跨越近两个数量级的气体的渗透率，并与Graham定律及弹道输运极限比较；同时用模拟复现实验结果并给出原子尺度输运图像。

Result: 归一化气体渗透率接近泄流极限，比气体筛分石墨烯膜高3–4个数量级；尽管存在强烈的、依赖于气体种类的气-孔相互作用，各气体仍遵循Graham定律并趋近非相互作用弹道输运的理想极限；模拟结果在实验不确定度内与实测渗透率一致。

Conclusion: 将极限薄厚与高孔面密度结合，可实现近弹道极限的高通量气体输运，展示了合成晶态二维聚合物在化学分离、水过滤和量子传感等方面的应用潜力。

Abstract: Chemical separation processes represent one of the largest contributors to industrial energy consumption, underscoring the need for the most efficient separation strategies. Many separation processes rely on selective transport through porous media. In this context, two-dimensional (2D) materials offer a promising route to atomically precise porosity beyond the reach of traditional top-down fabrication, with the potential to enhance separation performance by increasing flow. Here we report measurements of gas transport through a one-molecule-thick layer of suspended, nanoporous poly(carboxyfantrip), a 2D polymer that has identical, nanometer-sized pores that cover nearly a quarter of the surface. This combination of ultimate thinness with high areal pore density gives normalized gas flows (permeances) that approach the effusion limit, three to four orders of magnitude above gas-sieving graphene membranes. We find that gases spanning nearly two orders of magnitude in molecular weight (H$_2$, He, N$_2$, CO$_2$, and SF$_6$) obey Graham's law and approach the ideal limit of non-interacting ballistic transport, despite strong, species-dependent gas-pore interactions. Simulations reproduce the measured permeances within experimental uncertainty and provide an atomistic picture of the transport process. This work demonstrates the potential of synthetic crystalline 2D polymers for chemical separation, water filtration, and quantum sensing.

</details>


### [168] [Revealing laser-induced modification of weak ferromagnetism through a spin-reorientation phase transition in iron borate Fe$_3$BO$_6$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12460)
*A. V. Kuzikova,S. N. Barilo,R. A. Lanovsky,A. V. Sadovnikov,R. V. Pisarev,A. M. Kalashnikova*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 利用磁光泵浦-探测技术揭示Fe₃BO₆中Γ₂→Γ₄自旋重取向转变由弱铁磁矩的骤降触发，其源于激光对Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用与各向异性自旋倾斜的调控，且具有非热、电荷转移（2p→3d）主导的特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fe₃BO₆的弱铁磁性由双位点DMI与两个非等价Fe³⁺位点（4c、8d）的局域各向异性相互竞争决定，其自旋重取向转变Γ₂→Γ₄包含90°自旋翻转和35%弱铁磁矩变化，是研究弱铁磁贡献调控的理想模型；作者希望厘清飞秒激光如何超快改变这些贡献并驱动该转变。

Method: 采用磁光泵浦-探测（pump-probe）技术，在飞秒时间尺度上追踪Γ₂→Γ₄自旋重取向的动力学；并通过调节泵浦脉冲的光子能量，在不同光学吸收区间对比激发效应。

Result: 自旋重取向由Γ₂相中弱铁磁矩的突然下降所触发，对应激光对DMI和各向异性的改性；泵浦光子能量依赖表明该效应对自旋倾斜的影响是非热的，主要经由Fe³⁺O₆²⁻八面体内的电荷转移跃迁2p→3d实现，而非局域3d→3d跃迁；弱铁磁矩的降低同时控制转变阈值、延迟与末态，末态通过激光诱导加热激活的进动翻转进入Γ₄相；末态为Γ₄与Γ₂共存相，其改性自旋倾斜可持续数百皮秒。

Conclusion: 飞秒激光可通过电荷转移跃迁非热地调控Fe₃BO₆中的DMI与各向异性竞争，从而控制弱铁磁矩并触发自旋重取向；该过程呈现阈值—延迟—末态的可控层级结构，末态为长寿命的Γ₄/Γ₂共存相。

Abstract: In the iron oxide Fe$_3$BO$_6$ weak ferromagnetism stems from competing contributions from two-site Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interactions (DMI) and from on-site anisotropies at the two $4c$ and $8d$ types of nonequivalent Fe$^{3+}$ sites. As a result, spin reorientation (SR) transition $\Gamma_2$ $\rightarrow$ $\Gamma_4$ in Fe$_3$BO$_6$ uniquely comprises 90$^\circ$ spin switching and a 35% change of weak ferromagnetic moment. We explore ultrafast kinetics of such a transition as a proxy for weak ferromagnetism contributions modified by femtosecond laser pulses. Using magneto-optical pump-probe technique we reveal that the SR transition is triggered by an abrupt decrease of the weak ferromagnetic moment in the $\Gamma_2$ phase, and relate it to the laser-induced modification of the DMI and anisotropy contributions to the spin canting. By tuning the pump pulse phonon energy between different optical absorption ranges, we show that laser-driven impact on spin canting possesses a nonthermal character and is achieved through the charge-transfer transitions $2p\rightarrow3d$ within Fe$^{3+}$O$^{2-}_6$ octahedra rather than by localized $3d\rightarrow3d$ transitions at Fe$^{3+}$. The decreased weak ferromagnetic moment controls the threshold and the latency of the SR transition, as well as a final state. The latter is achieved via the precessional switching to the $\Gamma_4$ phase activated by the laser-induced heating. Final state is found to be a coexistence of $\Gamma_4$ and $\Gamma_2$ phase with modified spin canting persisting even at hundreds of picoseconds.

</details>


<div id='gr-qc'></div>

# gr-qc [[Back]](#toc)

### [169] [Qubit representation of the mass observable in quantized Schwarzschild interior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10933)
*Babak Vakili*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文建立惠勒–德维特量子化施瓦西黑洞内部质量可观测量的量子计算框架：在关联双模振子态张成的物理扇区内构造质量算符的有限维表示，通过泡利分解映射为量子比特算符，解析给出非零泡利项数与独立测量设置数随截断尺寸的标度，并用两比特VQE验证最小非平凡截断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在定量刻画编码与测量黑洞质量可观测量所需的量子资源，并在惠勒–德维特量子引力与量子计算之间建立直接联系，为引力可观测量的量子模拟提供结构化路径。

Method: 限定于关联双模振子态构成的物理扇区，构造质量算符的有限维矩阵表示；借助泡利分解将其映射为量子比特算符；对递增截断尺寸分析表示结构，并解析推导非零泡利项数量与独立测量设置数的标度；以两比特变分量子本征求解器（VQE）作为基准实现；另行设计独立于质量估计的量子线路以验证惠勒–德维特约束。

Result: 解析得到非零泡利项数与独立测量设置数随截断尺寸的标度关系；最小非平凡截断下的两比特VQE结果与精确有限维谱高度吻合；给出约束验证的量子线路方案。

Conclusion: 实现了惠勒–德维特量子引力与量子计算之间的直接衔接，并确立了引力可观测量的量子模拟路线；所提资源标度为后续扩展截断与多比特实现提供了定量依据。

Abstract: We develop a quantum-computational framework for the mass observable of the Wheeler--DeWitt quantized Schwarzschild black-hole interior. Working in the physical sector spanned by correlated two-mode oscillator states, we construct finite-dimensional representations of the mass operator and map them onto qubit operators through Pauli decomposition. We analyze the resulting representations for increasing truncation sizes and establish analytically the scaling of the number of nonvanishing Pauli terms and independent measurement settings. These results provide a quantitative characterization of the quantum resources required to encode and measure the black-hole mass observable. As a representative benchmark, we implement a two-qubit variational quantum eigensolver for the smallest nontrivial truncation and find excellent agreement with the exact finite-dimensional spectrum. We further describe a quantum-circuit procedure for verifying the Wheeler--DeWitt constraint independently of the mass estimation. Our results provide a direct connection between Wheeler--DeWitt quantum gravity and quantum computation and establish a structured route toward the quantum simulation of gravitational observables.

</details>


<div id='physics.optics'></div>

# physics.optics [[Back]](#toc)

### [170] [Intensity limits for phase-only holographic optical-tweezer arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10302)
*Anzhou Wen,Hengyu Lian,Kevin Yipu Wu,Maxwell F. Parsons,Praneeth Chakravarthula*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文研究相位全息图在光镊阵列中分配光效率的基本极限，推导了最小阱强度的上界，并指出单纯相位优化的节能空间有限，照明与阱几何的选择才是关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将光镊扩展到大规模阵列需要向每个捕获位提供足够光强，同时最小化总激光功率，但相位全息图的光分配效率存在何种根本极限尚不清楚。

Method: 对仅相位（phase-only）全息图的效率极限做理论分析：推导捕获位最小强度的上界，考虑照明方式、目标几何以及空间光调制器（SLM）像素衍射；分别针对单平面阵列和双轴向平面阵列给出极限随阱数与SLM像素数的标度关系，并确立三维聚焦条件；辅以数值实验验证优化全息图与理论上界的接近程度。

Result: 得到最小阱强度的上界及其随阱数与SLM像素数的标度律；数值实验中优化全息图可紧贴上界，说明相位优化本身的进一步改进只能带来有限的功率节省。

Conclusion: 相位优化的节能潜力已接近饱和，提升光效率应转向照明方案与阱几何的设计；论文为此提供了可操作的准则，用于构建更大规模、更低功耗的光镊阵列。

Abstract: Optical tweezers use tightly focused laser beams to trap and manipulate individual particles, enabling advances in quantum computing, drug discovery, and biological research. Scaling these technologies to large arrays requires delivering sufficient light to every trapping site while minimizing the total laser power required. Here, we investigate the fundamental limits of how efficiently a phase-only hologram can distribute light across an array of optical traps. We derive upper bounds on the minimum intensity across trapping sites, accounting for illumination, target geometry, and diffraction from spatial light modulator pixels. For arrays arranged in a single plane or across two axial planes, we show how these limits scale with the number of traps and SLM pixels, and establish conditions for focusing light in three dimensions. Numerical experiments demonstrate that optimized holograms can closely approach these theoretical limits, suggesting that further improvements in phase optimization alone offer limited power savings. Instead, our results reveal how the choice of illumination and trap geometry can improve light efficiency, providing practical guidelines for designing larger, more power-efficient optical-tweezer arrays.

</details>


### [171] [2D polaritons for Squeezing and Shrinking Light](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10754)
*Eduardo Bede Barros,Niclas Sven Müller,Stephanie Reich,Christian Thomsen*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该工作建立了一个无量纲的量子微观哈密顿量框架，用于描述二维材料中的极化激元，推导出集体激发及光锥外的横电/横磁模式，并揭示极端波长压缩与本征电场正交分量量子压缩的协同效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维材料能将光场限制在深亚波长尺度，是极化激元学的重要平台；但当厚度降至纳米极限时，宏观电动力学模型失效，无法准确描述二维材料中的光-物质耦合，现有理论对二维极化激元的理解存在明显空白。

Method: 从可极化二维表面的量子性质出发，构建一个通用的、无标度的哈密顿量，形成完整的量子微观理论框架；据此推导集体激发，并识别出存在于光锥之外的横电（TE）与横磁（TM）模式。

Result: 理论分析表明，二维体系可同时实现极端的波长压缩与电场正交分量的本征量子压缩，二者形成强协同效应；所建立的形式体系给出了二维极化激元的自洽量子描述。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了在纳米尺度操控光场非经典态的理论路线图与基础架构，为高精度量子传感及下一代光电子器件的发展开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Two-dimensional (2D) materials have emerged as an extraordinary platform for polaritonics, given their remarkable ability to confine light to deep sub-wavelength scales. However, as material thicknesses reach the nanometer limit, macroscopic electrodynamic models become inadequate for the description of light-matter coupling in 2D materials, leaving a significant gap in our understanding of 2D polaritons. In this work, we develop a comprehensive quantum microscopic framework that describes 2D polaritons through a general, scale-less Hamiltonian. Starting from the quantum properties of a polarizable 2D surface, we derive collective excitations and we identify both transverse electric magnetic modes existing outside the light cone. Our results demonstrate that these 2D systems facilitate a powerful synergy between extreme wavelength shrinking and intrinsic quantum squeezing of electric field quadratures. By establishing this theoretical roadmap, our work provides the essential architecture for controlling non-classical states of light at the nanoscale, opening new avenues for high-precision quantum sensing and the development of next-generation optoelectronic devices

</details>


### [172] [A reachability criterion for spectrally factorable SPDC in coupled waveguides without periodic poling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11086)
*Qiang Sun,Ke Wang,Andrew D. Greentree*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 提出基于耦合波导（非线性主波导+线性辅助波导）的联合可达性判据，利用相位闭合确定杂化参数，将群延迟比与参与度关联，用于筛选免周期极化的SPDC材料与模式对；AlN/TiO2实例实现直接相位匹配，5 mm内未滤波Schmidt纯度0.789。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无需周期极化的SPDC可拓宽非线性材料选择，但要求模式同时满足相位匹配与合适的群速度关系，缺少能同时约束相位匹配、色散控制与非线性参与度的设计判据。

Method: 对恒定截面耦合器（非线性主波导+线性辅助波导）作双模描述，推导联合可达性判据：相位闭合固定杂化参数，该参数同时决定主波导参与度与可达群延迟比；据此筛选材料/模式对并给出失谐量与参与度目标，再由全矢量计算精化；建立一阶纯度图，将可达延迟比与带宽—长度积映射到光子对光谱。

Result: 判据给出可达延迟比的显式约束；AlN/TiO2全矢量可行性实例满足直接相位匹配，泵浦—闲频与泵浦—信号群延迟失配比为-0.899，5 mm长度下计算得未滤波Schmidt纯度0.789。

Conclusion: 相位闭合、色散控制与非线性参与度通过单一杂化参数相互耦合，该判据可作为评估耦合波导SPDC源、超越周期极化材料的实用筛选与设计工具。

Abstract: Producing spectrally pure photon pairs by spontaneous parametric down-conversion without periodic poling would broaden the choice of nonlinear materials. It requires optical modes that provide both phase matching and suitable group velocities. We derive a joint reachability criterion for a constant-cross-section coupler with a nonlinear main guide and a linear auxiliary. In a two-mode description, phase closure fixes the hybridization parameter that also determines the main-guide participation and the attainable group-delay ratio. This relation screens material and mode pairs and specifies detuning and participation targets for full-vector refinement. A first-order purity map connects the reachable delay ratio and bandwidth--length product to the photon-pair spectrum. A full-vector AlN/TiO$_2$ feasibility example satisfies direct phase matching with a ratio of pump--idler to pump--signal group-delay mismatches of $-0.899$ and yields a calculated unfiltered Schmidt purity of 0.789 over 5 mm. The criterion links phase closure, dispersion control, and nonlinear participation when assessing coupled-waveguide sources beyond periodically poled materials.

</details>


### [173] [Comments on: "Optimized thermal control of a dual-wavelength-resonant nonlinear cavity"](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11590)
*Roman Schnabel*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文为一篇学术评论（Comment），针对 Appl. Opt. 65, 7090 (2026) 所声称的对某项专利发明的“优化”提出反驳，论证该方案实质上是降低最大可达温差的倒退，削弱而非改进了原发明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原专利发明提出双波长共振非线性腔的多温度管理方案：在束腰处优化晶体温度以实现相位匹配，同时用同晶体其他区域独立可调的温度满足双共振条件。该发明的早期形式曾被用于Virgo引力波探测器压缩光激光器。被评论文献声称提出了一种“优化”变体，作者认为有必要澄清其实际效果，纠正概念误解。

Method: 以评论（Comment）形式进行理论辨析：对比原发明的温度管理原理与被评论方案的温度差上限，分析温差对补偿相位失配的作用机理，并指出温差可动态调小、故最大化可用温差并无代价。

Result: 被评论方案减小了可达到的最大温差，因而无法有效补偿由介质膜、Gouy相移以及晶体端面子极化长度不理想所引入的相位差，尤其在短晶体情形下更为突出。

Conclusion: 该“优化”不成立，实为拆解原发明的功能倒退；高质量实现该发明本质上依赖于最大化可达温差，而较小温差在需要时可随时动态选取，故最大化温差是更优且无损的设计取向。

Abstract: This comment addresses the publication [Appl. Opt. 65, 7090 (2026)] which targets an alleged ``optimization'' of a patented invention conceived by the author. The invention had introduced a multi-temperature management scheme for a dual-wavelength-resonant nonlinear cavity that optimized the crystal temperature around the beam waist for phase-matching, while utilizing independently adjustable temperatures in other regions of the same crystal to simultaneously satisfy the double-resonance condition. A foundational form of this invention was initially utilized by co-author H. Vahlbruch without the author's prior knowledge during the construction of the squeezed-light laser for the Virgo gravitational wave detector. In their publication, the three authors now propose a variant that reduces the maximum achievable temperature differential. Here, I demonstrate that this approach does not constitute an optimization but, on the contrary, represents a regression that dismantles the invention. In general, a high-quality implementation of this invention fundamentally depends on maximizing the achievable temperature differential. Only then can the problematic phase differences---introduced by dielectric coatings, Gouy phase shifts, and suboptimal poling lengths at the crystal facets---be effectively compensated for, particularly in short crystals. If desired, smaller temperature differences can always be selected dynamically anyway.

</details>


### [174] [Tunable Near-Unity Light Absorption via Surface Exciton-Polaritons in Periodic Gratings of Anisotropic Polarizable Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12224)
*M. V. Rodrigues,M. S. Barreiro,M. I. Vasilevskiy,N. M. R. Peres*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文在简单平面几何中同时突破了超薄薄膜近完美吸收的两大障碍：通过J-聚集体各向异性条带光栅提供倒格矢动量以激励表面激子极化激元(SEP)，并利用介质间隔层下方的金属镜使反射场与入射场干涉、打破50%吸收上限；作者建立半解析矩阵方法计算完整光学响应，证明仅调节间隔层厚度即可使一级SEP共振吸收率从近1连续调至近0。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超薄薄膜的近完美吸收受两个基本障碍限制：一是动量失配，普通光照下难以共振激发表面激子极化激元(SEP)，通常需借助衰减全反射(ATR)构型；二是几何对称性导致的50%吸收上限，使自由悬浮导电薄片的吸收率无法超过一半。作者希望在同一平面结构内同时解决这两个问题。

Method: 提出一种平面结构：由承载明亮分子激子的J-聚集体构成的一维周期性各向异性可极化条带光栅，为SEP提供无需ATR的倒格矢动量；介质间隔层下方的金属镜将反射场折回吸收层，通过入射波与反射波干涉突破50%极限。同时建立半解析矩阵公式，用于计算该结构的完整光学响应。

Result: 仅改变间隔层厚度，一级SEP共振处的吸收率即可从近1连续调至近0；光栅周期与填充因子提供额外的光谱调控自由度；斜入射可激发第二支面外极化激元分支。

Conclusion: 周期性激子介质光栅可作为光谱选择性近完美吸收的多功能、实用且易集成的平台。

Abstract: Near-perfect light absorption in ultrathin films is hindered by two fundamental obstacles: the momentum mismatch that prevents resonant excitation of surface exciton-polaritons (SEPs) under ordinary illumination, and the 50% absorption ceiling imposed on free-standing conductive sheets by geometric symmetry. Here we show that both obstacles can be overcome simultaneously in a simple, planar geometry. A one-dimensional periodic grating of anisotropic polarizable ribbons composed of J-aggregates supporting bright molecular excitons provides the reciprocal lattice momentum needed to excite SEPs without employing an attenuated total reflection configuration, while a metallic mirror beneath a dielectric spacer folds the reflected field back onto the absorber, breaking the $50\%$ limit through interference between the incident and reflected waves. We develop a semi-analytical matrix formulation to compute the full optical response of such structure, and demonstrate that the absorptance at the first-order SEP resonance can be continuously tuned from near-unity to near-zero by varying the spacer thickness alone. The grating period and filling factor offer additional spectral control, and oblique incidence unlocks a second, out-of-plane polariton branch. These results establish periodic gratings of excitonic media as a versatile, practical and integrable platform for spectrally selective, near-perfect light absorption.

</details>


<div id='hep-ph'></div>

# hep-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [175] [Spin entanglement in $pp$ and $pA$ collisions: high-energy TMD effects and fragmentation probes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11568)
*Michael Fucilla*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 在 $k_T$ 因子化与 shockwave 框架下构建质子-质子/质子-离子碰撞中夸克-反夸克对的全自旋密度矩阵，揭示包容通道中由线偏振胶子诱导的 $\cos(2\phi)$ 量子关联调制及衍射通道的纠缠与 Bell 非局域性，并提出用 Collins/双强子碎裂函数与双 Collins 不对称度作为纠缠见证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 夸克-反夸克对的纠缠与量子关联是非极化强子碰撞中尚未充分探索的量子信息维度；小-$x$ 胶子 TMD（尤其是线偏振胶子）与饱和效应如何影响这些关联缺乏系统描述，且实验上缺少可操作的探测量。

Method: 基于 shockwave 形式体系的 $k_T$ 因子化，在背对背（back-to-back）极限下构建包容与衍射通道的完整自旋密度矩阵；用 concurrence、stabilizer Rényi 熵与 quantum discord 量化关联，并结合六个小-$x$ 胶子 TMD 的贡献分析其 $\cos(2\phi)$ 调制；对衍射通道分析密度矩阵的秩与边缘混合度，并讨论 Collins 与双强子碎裂函数及双 Collins 不对称度作为探针。

Result: 包容通道中，线偏振胶子导致 concurrence、stabilizer Rényi 熵和 quantum discord 呈现 $\cos(2\phi)$ 调制，该调制取决于六个小-$x$ 胶子 TMD 的相互竞争；在稀薄（BFKL）图像下调制幅度最大且与横动量不平衡度 $q$ 无关，而饱和效应使其显著压低。衍射通道中夸克对始终纠缠，性质与衍射 DIS 相同，源于 Pomeron 交换的排他性与 eikonal 特性，对应秩至多为二、边缘极大混合的自旋密度矩阵；该类态下纠缠与 Bell 非局域性重合，且在整个相空间持续存在。

Conclusion: 小-$x$ 胶子（线偏振 TMD 与饱和效应）可显著调控产生夸克对的量子关联，衍射通道提供了稳健的纠缠与 Bell 非局域性来源；非极化强子碰撞中可通过 Collins、双强子碎裂函数及精心选取的双 Collins 不对称度（无质量夸克情形）作为纠缠见证，为实验检验量子纠缠提供可行途径。

Abstract: Spin-spin correlations of a quark-antiquark pair produced in proton-proton or proton-Ion collisions are investigated in $k_T$-factorization within the shockwave formalism. We construct the full spin-density matrix of both inclusive and diffractive channels in the limit in which the two quarks are back-to-back. In the inclusive channel, due to linearly polarized gluons, we show that a $\cos (2 \phi)$-modulation of the concurrence, stabilizer Rényi entropy, and quantum discord emerges and depends on the interplay between six small-$x$ gluon TMDs. The modulation is predicted to be maximally strong and independent of the transverse momentum imbalance, $q$, from a dilute (BFKL) perspective, while it is considerably suppressed by saturation effects. In diffraction, we observe that the pair is always entangled and exhibits the same properties found in diffractive DIS. We explain how these properties follow directly from the exclusive and eikonal nature of the Pomeron exchange. We trace this behavior to a spin-density matrix of rank at most two with maximally mixed marginals. For this class of states, entanglement and Bell nonlocality coincide even when the state is mixed, while the explicit form of the light-front helicity amplitudes ensures that both persist in the whole phase space. We suggest accessing the spin correlations in unpolarized hadron collisions through the Collins and dihadron fragmentation functions. We point out that, in inclusive production, for massless quarks, suitably chosen double-Collins asymmetries provide promising entanglement witnesses.

</details>


<div id='cs.IT'></div>

### [176] [BP-based qLDPC Decoding under Long-range Correlated Noise: Trapping Configurations and Prior Mismatch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10796)
*Abhinav Dangi,Avhishek Chatterjee*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 该文研究Relay-BP与beam search两种BP类qLDPC解码器在空间相关噪声（marginal不变）下的鲁棒性：用隐高斯马尔可夫随机场生成可控强度的相关误差，发现相关性可使逻辑错误率变化数个量级、削弱有限尺寸距离优势并改变解码器排序；在其中一个电路上二者劣于BP-OSD，且增大relay memory或beam width无法补偿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有BP类qLDPC解码器（Relay-BP、beam search）针对独立噪声设计，而实际硬件噪声存在空间相关性；作者希望考察在单机制错误概率不变、仅联合分布变为相关的情形下，解码器所假设的联合误差模型发生失配时性能如何退化，作为相关性感知解码研究的补充对照。

Method: 采用隐高斯马尔可夫随机场生成相关误差，可在精确保持各边缘错误概率的前提下控制相关强度与关联范围；在多个测试电路与码实例上比较Relay-BP、beam search与BP-OSD的逻辑错误率，并做配置（trap配置）分析与匹配错误权重下的对照实验，最后向解码器提供以隐场为条件的边缘概率。

Result: 相关性可使逻辑错误率变化数个量级，削弱有限尺寸距离优势，并改变三种解码器的相对排序；在两个测试电路之一上Relay-BP与beam search劣于BP-OSD，增大relay memory或beam width无法补偿退化；相关性主要提高已知对该类解码器困难的trap配置出现概率，匹配错误权重下相关误差的译码失败率仍高出i.i.d.数倍；提供条件边缘概率可恢复大部分损失性能。

Conclusion: BP类qLDPC解码器在噪声相关性下性能显著退化，且不能靠增大relay memory或beam width弥补；主要成因是先验（联合误差模型）失配导致trap配置概率上升，修正边缘先验可恢复大部分性能，说明先验匹配是相关性条件下性能退化的主要来源。

Abstract: We study the robustness of two state-of-the-art BP-based qLDPC decoders, Relay-BP and beam search, designed for independent noise when applied to spatially correlated noise with unchanged single-mechanism error probabilities. We generate correlated errors with a hidden Gaussian Markov random field, which lets us control the strength and range of the correlation while keeping every marginal error probability exact. This setting complements correlation-aware decoding: it asks how existing decoders perform when their assumed joint error model is mismatched. Across multiple tested circuits and code instances that we simulated, we observe that correlations can change logical error rates by orders of magnitude, reduce the finite-size distance advantage, and alter the relative ranking of Relay-BP, beam search, and BP-OSD. On one of the two tested circuits, Relay-BP and beam search become worse than BP-OSD, and increasing relay memory or beam width does not compensate for the degradation. Configuration analysis indicates that correlation primarily increases the probability of trapping configurations that are known to challenge these decoders; at matched error weight, correlated errors still cause decoding failures several times more often than i.i.d. errors. Finally, supplying the decoders with the marginals conditioned on the hidden field recovers most of the lost performance in the tested settings identifying prior mismatch as a major contributor to performance degradation under correlation.

</details>


<div id='math.PR'></div>

# math.PR [[Back]](#toc)

### [177] [The satisfiability conjecture for random k-SAT formulas. Human-friendly exposition of the recent OpenAI resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10139)
*David Gamarnik*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本短文对 OpenAI 内部模型关于 k-SAT 可满足性猜想（一般 k）的证明给出"人类友好"的详细解读，作为该近期结果的通俗化阐述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: k-SAT 可满足性猜想是离散概率中的核心开放问题：预言存在一个固定的子句-变量比阈值，使公式可满足概率随比值增加从近 1 骤降至近 0。大常数 k 情形十年前已由 Ding–Sly–Sun 用二阶矩方法解决，但一般 k 长期未决，最近由 Carenini 与 OpenAI 分别破解，因此需要一份可读性强的解读。

Method: 本文属于阐述性短文（exposition），并非提出新方法：以 OpenAI 对猜想的解法为主线，进行细节化、"人类友好"的重新梳理与讲解。

Result: 一般 k 情形的可满足性猜想现已获证；本文对该证明给出细致、易读的解说（原文摘要未给出具体技术结论）。

Conclusion: 该短文本身不贡献新结果，价值在于把机器产出的证明转述为人类可理解的论证，便于社区审查与传播。

Abstract: The satisfiability conjecture was one of the most important open problems in discrete probability. It was resolved recently first by Carenini~\cite{GCarenini}, and shortly after by OpenAI~\cite{k-sat-OpenAI}, among other 370 problems solved by its internal model. The conjecture concerned the satisfiability property of random instances of the k-SAT problem. It posits the existence of a fixed value for the clause-to-variables ratio around which the probability that the formula is satisfiable drops sharply from nearly one to nearly zero as the ratio increases. While the case of large constant $k$ was resolved a decade ago by Ding, Sly and Sun~\cite{ding2015proof,ding2022proof} using the heavy machinery of the second moment method, the case of general $k$ eluded proof until ~\cite{GCarenini} and~\cite{k-sat-OpenAI}.
 In this short note we provide a ''human-friendly'' and detailed exposition of the OpenAI's resolution of the conjecture.

</details>


<div id='nucl-th'></div>

# nucl-th [[Back]](#toc)

### [178] [Spin Asymmetries for Twisted-Neutron Capture by Polarized $^3$He Nuclei](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10829)
*Rakhee Roy,Carl E. Carlson,Andrei Afanasev*

Main category: nucl-th

TL;DR: 对携带轨道角动量(OAM)的"扭转中子"引发的核反应进行理论分析，推导出自旋极化³He热中子俘获反应的角动量选择定则，并预测可观测的自旋不对称。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扭转中子（沿传播方向携带额外OAM）为核反应引入了常规平面波中子不具备的自由度，其在自旋依赖的核过程中的作用尚缺乏理论与定量刻画，需要明确OAM对反应选择定则与可观测量的影响范围。

Method: 以³He(n,γ)⁴He型热中子俘获为对象，结合光束的相位奇异性（涡旋结构）与核自旋自由度，推导角动量选择定则；分析OAM耦合对自旋振幅的修正，并考察束流相位奇异点附近德布罗意波长尺度区域内的自旋效应，给出在实验约束条件下的自旋不对称预言。

Result: 由OAM引起的显著新奇自旋效应仅局限于束流相位奇异点周围约一个德布罗意波长量级的空间区域；在该区域内对自旋极化³He俘获反应预言了自旋不对称，并给出一系列实验约束下的可测量范围。

Conclusion: OAM可作为一种新的调控手段影响自旋相关核反应，但效应具有强烈的空间局域性（相位奇点附近的波长尺度），因此实验上需以高空间分辨的极化靶置于涡旋束轴心才能检验。该工作为涡旋粒子核物理提供了选择定则与可观测量基础。

Abstract: We provide a theoretical analysis of a nuclear reaction caused by twisted neutrons that carry an additional Orbital Angular Momentum (OAM) along the propagation direction. Angular momentum selection rules are derived for the thermal-neutron capture reaction on a spin-polarized $^3$He nucleus. We demonstrate that noticeable novel spin effects due to OAM are limited to the region of the size of de Broglie wavelength around the phase singularity of the beam. Spin asymmetries are predicted for a range of experimental constraints.

</details>


<div id='hep-lat'></div>

# hep-lat [[Back]](#toc)

### [179] [Symanzik Effective Hamiltonian for Lattice Quantum Field Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12520)
*Fernanda Steffens,Carsten Urbach*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 提出一个基于量纲分析与哈密顿量形式下对称性的框架，用于直接构造格点量子场论的Symanzik有效哈密顿量，从而对给定空间格距阶数下所有可贡献算符进行分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Symanzik改进多在拉格朗日形式下进行，作者希望为格点量子场论的哈密顿量形式建立系统化的有效理论构造手段，以便未来开展非微扰改进计划。

Method: 在哈密顿量表述下，利用量纲分析与对称性枚举给定空间格距阶数的全部可贡献算符，再借助运动方程及其他恒等式约化算符列表；并以φ⁴理论与纯规范理论作为示例。

Result: 对φ⁴理论及纯规范理论得到与既有文献在经典改进情形下一致的结果，验证了框架的正确性。

Conclusion: 该框架可用于系统构造哈密顿量形式的Symanzik有效哈密顿量，并为今后发展非微扰改进方案提供基础。

Abstract: We propose a framework to construct the Symanzik effective Hamiltonian for lattice quantum field theories directly based on dimensional analysis and symmetries in the Hamiltonian formulation. This allows to classify all operators which can contribute at a given order in the spatial lattice spacing. This list can be reduced using the equations of motion and other identities. We exemplify the framework for $\phi^4$ and pure gauge theory, obtaining results consistent with earlier publications for the case of classical improvement. It provides the means to develop a non-perturbative improvement programme in the Hamiltonian formulation of lattice quantum field theories in the future.

</details>
